FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/DeviceCrypto.kt SHA256 2bf5b48681590f243a6ff3dee5c2856805c0d6598001e32ae69cd24baa662e64
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

import android.content.Context
import android.security.keystore.KeyGenParameterSpec
import android.security.keystore.KeyProperties
import android.util.Base64
import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.SecureRandom
import javax.crypto.Cipher
import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator
import javax.crypto.SecretKey
import javax.crypto.SecretKeyFactory
import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec
import javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec

internal object DeviceCrypto {
    private const val alias = "finanzas_locales_storage_v1"
    @Synchronized private fun key(): SecretKey {
        val store = KeyStore.getInstance("AndroidKeyStore").apply { load(null) }
        if (store.containsAlias(alias)) return store.getKey(alias, null) as SecretKey
        return KeyGenerator.getInstance(KeyProperties.KEY_ALGORITHM_AES, "AndroidKeyStore").apply {
            init(KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder(alias, KeyProperties.PURPOSE_ENCRYPT or KeyProperties.PURPOSE_DECRYPT)
                .setBlockModes(KeyProperties.BLOCK_MODE_GCM)
                .setEncryptionPaddings(KeyProperties.ENCRYPTION_PADDING_NONE)
                .setKeySize(256).build())
        }.generateKey()
    }
    fun encrypt(value: String): String {
        val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
        cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key())
        return "v1:" + Base64.encodeToString(cipher.iv + cipher.doFinal(value.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)), Base64.NO_WRAP)
    }
    fun decrypt(value: String): String {
        require(value.startsWith("v1:"))
        val bytes = Base64.decode(value.substring(3), Base64.NO_WRAP)
        require(bytes.size >= 28)
        val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
        cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, key(), GCMParameterSpec(128, bytes.copyOfRange(0, 12)))
        return String(cipher.doFinal(bytes.copyOfRange(12, bytes.size)), Charsets.UTF_8)
    }
    @Synchronized fun databaseKey(context: Context): String {
        val preferences = context.getSharedPreferences("storage_security", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val wrapped = preferences.getString("database_key_v1", null)
        if (wrapped != null) return decrypt(wrapped)
        val secret = ByteArray(32).also { SecureRandom().nextBytes(it) }.joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it.toInt() and 255) }
        check(preferences.edit().putString("database_key_v1", encrypt(secret)).commit())
        return secret
    }
    fun deriveBackupKey(password: String, salt: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val spec = PBEKeySpec(password.toCharArray(), salt, 310000, 256)
        return try { SecretKeyFactory.getInstance("PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256").generateSecret(spec).encoded }
        finally { spec.clearPassword() }
    }
}


FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/MainActivity.kt SHA256 45fa34dc5fa377d3373d627399dee2c5385d8296ea4c78817f2a124359224aeb
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

import android.Manifest
import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.Settings
import io.flutter.embedding.android.FlutterActivity
import io.flutter.embedding.engine.FlutterEngine
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

class MainActivity : FlutterActivity() {
    private val channelName = "finanzas_locales/notification_listener"
    private val notificationPermissionRequest = 701
    private var initialAction: String? = null
    private var reminderWorker: ReminderWorker? = null
    private var pendingNotificationPermission: MethodChannel.Result? = null

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: android.os.Bundle?) {
        initialAction = intent?.action
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    }

    override fun onNewIntent(intent: Intent) {
        super.onNewIntent(intent)
        setIntent(intent)
        initialAction = intent.action
        if (intent.action == PaymentReminderAgenda.OPEN) NotificationQueue.onCaptured?.invoke()
    }

    override fun configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine: FlutterEngine) {
        super.configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine)
        MethodChannel(flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger, "finanzas_locales/security").setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            when (call.method) {
                "databaseKey" -> try { result.success(DeviceCrypto.databaseKey(this)) }
                    catch (_: Exception) { result.error("storage_key_unavailable", "No se pudo abrir el almacenamiento protegido.", null) }
                "deriveBackupKey" -> {
                    val password = call.argument<String>("password")
                    val salt = call.argument<ByteArray>("salt")
                    if (password == null || salt == null || salt.size != 16) {
                        result.error("invalid_key_parameters", "Parámetros de backup inválidos.", null)
                    } else Thread {
                        try {
                            val derived = DeviceCrypto.deriveBackupKey(password, salt)
                            runOnUiThread { result.success(derived) }
                        } catch (_: Exception) {
                            runOnUiThread { result.error("backup_key_failed", "No se pudo descifrar el backup.", null) }
                        }
                    }.start()
                }
                else -> result.notImplemented()
            }
        }
        val reminderWorker = ReminderWorker { reply -> runOnUiThread(reply) }
        this.reminderWorker = reminderWorker
        MethodChannel(flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger, "finanzas_locales/payment_reminders").setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            try {
                when (call.method) {
                    "permission" -> result.success(PaymentReminderAgenda.permission(this))
                    "requestPermission" -> requestOwnNotificationPermission(object : MethodChannel.Result {
                        override fun success(value: Any?) { result.success(PaymentReminderAgenda.permission(this@MainActivity)) }
                        override fun error(code: String, message: String?, details: Any?) { result.error(code, message, details) }
                        override fun notImplemented() { result.notImplemented() }
                    })
                    "replaceSchedule" -> {
                        val requests = call.argument<List<Map<String, Any?>>>("requests") ?: emptyList()
                        reminderWorker.execute({ PaymentReminderAgenda.replace(this, requests) }) { outcome ->
                            outcome.fold(
                                onSuccess = { result.success(it) },
                                onFailure = { result.error("reminder_schedule_failed", "No se pudieron actualizar los recordatorios.", null) },
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    "consumeOpenAction" -> {
                        // Capture this intent before a subsequent onNewIntent replaces it.
                        val openIntent = intent
                        reminderWorker.execute({ PaymentReminderAgenda.consume(this, openIntent) }) { outcome ->
                            outcome.fold(
                                onSuccess = { result.success(it) },
                                onFailure = { result.error("reminder_schedule_failed", "No se pudieron actualizar los recordatorios.", null) },
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    else -> result.notImplemented()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                result.error("reminder_schedule_failed", "No se pudieron actualizar los recordatorios.", null)
            }
        }
        val captureChannel = MethodChannel(flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger, channelName)
        NotificationQueue.onCaptured = { captureChannel.invokeMethod("notificationCaptured", null) }
        MethodChannel(flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger, channelName).setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            val preferences = getSharedPreferences(NotificationQueue.preferencesName, MODE_PRIVATE)
            when (call.method) {
                "isAccessGranted" -> result.success(isNotificationAccessGranted())
                "openAccessSettings" -> {
                    openNotificationAccessSettings()
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "requestOwnNotificationPermission" -> requestOwnNotificationPermission(result)
                "readActiveWalletNotifications" -> {
                    if (!BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED) {
                        result.error("developer_tools_disabled", "Herramientas de desarrollo desactivadas.", null)
                    } else if (!isNotificationAccessGranted()) {
                        result.error("notification_access_required", "Concedé acceso a notificaciones en los ajustes de Android.", null)
                    } else {
                        val listener = WalletNotificationListenerService.connectedInstance
                        if (listener == null) {
                            android.service.notification.NotificationListenerService.requestRebind(
                                ComponentName(this, WalletNotificationListenerService::class.java),
                            )
                            result.error("listener_not_connected", "El lector aún no está conectado. Esperá unos segundos y volvé a consultar.", null)
                        } else {
                            try {
                                result.success(listener.readActiveWalletNotifications())
                            } catch (_: Exception) {
                                result.error("active_notifications_unavailable", "Android no permitió consultar las notificaciones. Revisá el acceso y volvé a intentar.", null)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                "sendTestNotification" -> {
                    if (!BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED) {
                        result.error("developer_tools_disabled", "Herramientas de desarrollo desactivadas.", null)
                    } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33 && checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                        result.error("notification_permission_required", "Concedé permiso para mostrar notificaciones de prueba.", null)
                    } else {
                        val merchant = call.argument<String>("merchant")?.trim() ?: ""
                        val alias = call.argument<String>("alias") ?: ""
                        val amount = call.argument<Number>("amount")?.toLong() ?: 0
                        if (merchant.isEmpty() || merchant.length > 160 || !alias.matches(Regex("[0-9]{4,6}")) || amount <= 0) {
                            result.error("invalid_test_notification", "Revisá comercio, identificador e importe.", null)
                        } else {
                            val manager = getSystemService(android.app.NotificationManager::class.java)
                            val channelId = "wallet_tests"
                            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) manager.createNotificationChannel(android.app.NotificationChannel(channelId, "Pruebas de Wallet", android.app.NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT))
                            val notification = androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat.Builder(this, channelId)
                                .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_dialog_info)
                                .setContentTitle("PRUEBA Wallet: $merchant")
                                .setContentText("PYG $amount · ••$alias")
                                .addExtras(android.os.Bundle().apply { putString("testMerchant", merchant) })
                                .setTimeoutAfter(60000)
                                .build()
                            manager.notify("wallet_developer_test", (System.nanoTime() and 0x7fffffff).toInt(), notification)
                            result.success(null)
                        }
                    }
                }
                "configure" -> {
                    val packages = call.argument<List<String>>("allowedPackages") ?: emptyList()
                    preferences.edit()
                        .putBoolean(NotificationQueue.enabledKey, call.argument<Boolean>("enabled") == true)
                        .putBoolean(NotificationQueue.diagnosticKey, BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED && call.argument<Boolean>("diagnosticMode") == true)
                        .putStringSet(NotificationQueue.packagesKey, packages.filter { NotificationQueue.defaultPackages.contains(it) || (BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED && it == "com.google.android.apps.messaging") }.toSet())
                        .apply()
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "queuedEnvelopes" -> {
                    val queue = NotificationQueue.read(preferences)
                    queue.forEach { if (!it.has("captureId")) it.put("captureId", java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString()) }
                    NotificationQueue.write(preferences, queue)
                    val visible = queue.filter {
                        BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED || (!it.optBoolean("isTest", false) &&
                            NotificationQueue.defaultPackages.contains(it.optString("packageName")))
                    }
                    if (!BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED) NotificationQueue.write(preferences, visible)
                    result.success(visible.map { it.toFlutterMap() })
                }
                "acknowledgeEnvelope" -> {
                    val captureId = call.argument<String>("captureId")
                    if (captureId != null) NotificationQueue.write(preferences,
                        NotificationQueue.read(preferences).filter { it.optString("captureId") != captureId })
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "drainEnvelopes" -> {
                    val queue = NotificationQueue.read(preferences).filter {
                        BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED ||
                            (!it.optBoolean("isTest", false) && NotificationQueue.defaultPackages.contains(it.optString("packageName")))
                    }
                    NotificationQueue.write(preferences, emptyList())
                    result.success(queue.map { it.toFlutterMap() })
                }
                "clearCapturedEvents" -> {
                    NotificationQueue.write(preferences, emptyList())
                    result.success(null)
                }
                "getInitialAction" -> {
                    val action = initialAction
                    initialAction = null
                    result.success(action)
                }
                else -> result.notImplemented()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun cleanUpFlutterEngine(flutterEngine: FlutterEngine) {
        NotificationQueue.onCaptured = null
        reminderWorker?.close()
        reminderWorker = null
        super.cleanUpFlutterEngine(flutterEngine)
    }

    private fun requestOwnNotificationPermission(result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU ||
            checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            result.success(true)
            return
        }
        if (pendingNotificationPermission != null) {
            result.error("permission_request_active", "Ya hay una solicitud de permiso activa.", null)
            return
        }
        pendingNotificationPermission = result
        requestPermissions(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS), notificationPermissionRequest)
    }

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(
        requestCode: Int,
        permissions: Array<out String>,
        grantResults: IntArray,
    ) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, permissions, grantResults)
        if (requestCode == notificationPermissionRequest) {
            pendingNotificationPermission?.success(
                grantResults.isNotEmpty() && grantResults[0] == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED,
            )
            pendingNotificationPermission = null
        }
    }

    private fun isNotificationAccessGranted(): Boolean {
        val enabled = Settings.Secure.getString(contentResolver, "enabled_notification_listeners") ?: return false
        val component = ComponentName(this, WalletNotificationListenerService::class.java)
        return enabled.split(':').any { ComponentName.unflattenFromString(it) == component }
    }

    private fun openNotificationAccessSettings() {
        val detailIntent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_DETAIL_SETTINGS).apply {
            putExtra(Settings.EXTRA_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_COMPONENT_NAME,
                ComponentName(this@MainActivity, WalletNotificationListenerService::class.java).flattenToString())
        }
        try {
            startActivity(detailIntent)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            startActivity(Intent(Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_LISTENER_SETTINGS))
        }
    }
}

internal fun JSONObject.toFlutterMap(): Map<String, Any?> {
    val output = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
    keys().forEach { key ->
        output[key] = when (val value = opt(key)) {
            JSONObject.NULL -> null
            is JSONArray -> (0 until value.length()).map { value.opt(it)?.toString() }
            is JSONObject -> value.toFlutterMap()
            else -> value
        }
    }
    return output
}


FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/NotificationActionReceiver.kt SHA256 631771d23c4f9cfc0366e281e514f2db3204e0ea823354cb85607eefd9d45e4e
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent

class NotificationActionReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
        if (intent.action != "py.finanzaslocales.IGNORE_CAPTURED") return
        val key = intent.getStringExtra("notificationKey") ?: return
        val preferences = context.getSharedPreferences(NotificationQueue.preferencesName, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val remaining = NotificationQueue.read(preferences)
            .filterNot { it.optString("notificationKey") == key }
        NotificationQueue.write(preferences, remaining)
        context.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).cancel(key.hashCode())
    }
}


FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/PaymentReminderReceiver.kt SHA256 3adf1f90bce3ab18688e0274183936638a9a375c8e60e0381ca490524bd5f5d2
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

import android.app.AlarmManager
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone
import java.util.UUID

/** No ledger access: this receiver can only display a previously reconciled reminder. */
class PaymentReminderReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
    override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
        try {
            if (intent.action == PaymentReminderAgenda.ALARM) {
                PaymentReminderAgenda.deliver(context, intent.getStringExtra("generation"), intent.getStringExtra("id"))
            } else {
                PaymentReminderAgenda.rebuild(context)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            // Fail closed if encrypted storage/Keystore is unavailable; next foreground reconcile retries.
        }
    }
}

internal object PaymentReminderAgenda {
    const val ALARM = "py.finanzaslocales.PAYMENT_REMINDER"
    const val OPEN = "py.finanzaslocales.OPEN_PAYMENT"
    const val CHANNEL = "payment_reminders_v1"
    private fun preferences(c: Context) = c.getSharedPreferences("payment_reminders", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private fun read(c: Context): JSONObject {
        val value = preferences(c).getString("encrypted", null) ?: return JSONObject().put("generation", "").put("requests", JSONArray()).put("delivered", JSONArray())
        return JSONObject(DeviceCrypto.decrypt(value))
    }
    private fun write(c: Context, state: JSONObject) {
        check(preferences(c).edit().putString("encrypted", DeviceCrypto.encrypt(state.toString())).commit())
    }
    private fun manager(c: Context) = c.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
    private fun channel(c: Context) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) manager(c).createNotificationChannel(NotificationChannel(CHANNEL, "Pagos pendientes", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT))
    }
    fun permission(c: Context): Boolean {
        channel(c)
        return NotificationManagerCompat.from(c).areNotificationsEnabled() &&
            (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 26 || manager(c).getNotificationChannel(CHANNEL).importance != NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_NONE)
    }
    private fun entries(state: JSONObject): List<JSONObject> {
        val array = state.getJSONArray("requests")
        return (0 until array.length()).map { array.getJSONObject(it) }
    }
    private fun delivered(state: JSONObject): Set<String> {
        val array = state.getJSONArray("delivered")
        return (0 until array.length()).map { array.getString(it) }.toSet()
    }
    private fun millis(r: JSONObject): Long {
        val text = r.getString("localDateTime")
        require(text.matches(Regex("[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}\\.[0-9]{3}")))
        val format = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.SSS", Locale.US).apply {
            isLenient = false
            timeZone = TimeZone.getDefault()
        }
        return requireNotNull(format.parse(text)).time
    }
    private fun alarm(c: Context, generation: String, id: String): PendingIntent = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(c, 0,
        Intent(c, PaymentReminderReceiver::class.java).setAction(ALARM)
            .setData(Uri.parse("cabal-reminder://alarm/${Uri.encode(generation)}/${Uri.encode(id)}"))
            .putExtra("generation", generation).putExtra("id", id), PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)

    private fun entry(state: JSONObject, r: JSONObject) = PaymentReminderEntry(
        r.getString("id"),
        listOf("obligationId", "title", "body", "localDateTime", "revision").map { r.optString(it, "") },
        r.optString("generation", state.optString("generation", "")),
        millis(r),
    )

    @Synchronized fun replace(c: Context, requests: List<Map<String, Any?>>): Boolean {
        val unique = mutableSetOf<String>()
        val array = JSONArray()
        requests.forEach { value ->
            val r = JSONObject(value)
            val id = r.getString("id")
            require(id.isNotBlank() && id.length <= 512 && unique.add(id))
            require(r.getString("obligationId").isNotBlank() && r.getString("obligationId").length <= 160)
            require(r.getString("title").length <= 240 && r.getString("body").length <= 500)
            millis(r) // Validate all requests before replacing the valid agenda.
            array.put(r)
        }
        require(requests.size <= 20000)
        val old = read(c)
        val generation = UUID.randomUUID().toString()
        val oldEntries = entries(old).map { entry(old, it) }
        val incoming = (0 until array.length()).map { entry(old, array.getJSONObject(it)) }
        val replacement = replacePaymentReminderEntries(oldEntries, incoming, delivered(old), System.currentTimeMillis(), generation)
        replacement.entries.forEachIndexed { index, value -> array.getJSONObject(index).put("generation", value.generation) }
        val state = JSONObject().put("generation", generation)
            .put("requests", array).put("delivered", JSONArray(replacement.delivered.toList()))
        // Persist before cancellation: queued broadcasts/taps for removed or edited entries fail closed.
        write(c, state)
        replacement.invalidated.forEach { r ->
            c.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).cancel(alarm(c, r.generation, r.id))
            manager(c).cancel("payment:${r.id}", 0)
        }
        // Refresh the bounded future window without discarding a delayed, already due alarm.
        oldEntries.filter { it.at > System.currentTimeMillis() }.forEach { r ->
            c.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).cancel(alarm(c, r.generation, r.id))
        }
        schedule(c, state)
        return permission(c)
    }
    private fun schedule(c: Context, state: JSONObject) {
        val done = delivered(state)
        if (!permission(c)) return
        // Bound outstanding alarms below Android's per-app limit. Each delivery fills the window.
        entries(state).filter { it.getString("id") !in done && millis(it) > System.currentTimeMillis() }
            .sortedBy { millis(it) }.take(400).forEach { r ->
            c.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).setAndAllowWhileIdle(AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, millis(r), alarm(c, entry(state, r).generation, r.getString("id")))
        }
    }
    @Synchronized fun rebuild(c: Context) {
        val state = read(c)
        entries(state).forEach { r -> c.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).cancel(alarm(c, entry(state, r).generation, r.getString("id"))) }
        schedule(c, state)
    }
    @Synchronized fun deliver(c: Context, generation: String?, id: String?) {
        val state = read(c)
        if (id == null || id in delivered(state)) return
        if (entries(state).none { it.getString("id") == id && entry(state, it).generation == generation } || !permission(c)) return
        val done = delivered(state)
        entries(state).filter { it.getString("id") !in done && millis(it) <= System.currentTimeMillis() }.forEach { r ->
            notifyOne(c, state, entry(state, r).generation, r)
        }
        schedule(c, state)
    }
    private fun notifyOne(c: Context, state: JSONObject, generation: String?, r: JSONObject) {
        val id = r.getString("id")
        val contentIntent = Intent(c, MainActivity::class.java).setAction(OPEN)
            .setData(Uri.parse("cabal-reminder://open/${Uri.encode(generation)}/${Uri.encode(id)}"))
            .putExtra("reminderGeneration", generation).putExtra("reminderId", id)
            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP)
        val pending = PendingIntent.getActivity(c, 0, contentIntent, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
        val notification = NotificationCompat.Builder(c, CHANNEL)
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_popup_reminder).setContentTitle(r.getString("title"))
            .setContentText(r.getString("body")).setContentIntent(pending).setAutoCancel(true)
            .setVisibility(NotificationCompat.VISIBILITY_PRIVATE).build()
        // Persist before notify: process death cannot produce a second delivery.
        state.getJSONArray("delivered").put(id)
        write(c, state)
        try { manager(c).notify("payment:$id", 0, notification) } catch (_: SecurityException) { /* revoked concurrently */ }
    }
    @Synchronized fun consume(c: Context, intent: Intent?): String? {
        if (intent?.action != OPEN) return null
        val state = read(c)
        val generation = intent.getStringExtra("reminderGeneration")
        val id = intent.getStringExtra("reminderId")
        intent.removeExtra("reminderGeneration")
        intent.removeExtra("reminderId")
        return entries(state).firstOrNull { it.getString("id") == id && entry(state, it).generation == generation }?.getString("obligationId")
    }
}


FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/PaymentReminderReplacement.kt SHA256 e6bff9c852420ac35bfea16bc880963ab1780dcf4c1fdc6eaad06a68018c8efc
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

/** Identity includes obligation revision so financial edits also invalidate an old notice. */
internal data class PaymentReminderEntry(val id: String, val signature: List<String>, val generation: String, val at: Long)
internal data class PaymentReminderReplacement(val entries: List<PaymentReminderEntry>, val invalidated: List<PaymentReminderEntry>, val delivered: Set<String>)

internal fun replacePaymentReminderEntries(
    old: List<PaymentReminderEntry>,
    incoming: List<PaymentReminderEntry>,
    delivered: Set<String>,
    now: Long,
    generation: String,
): PaymentReminderReplacement {
    val previous = old.associateBy { it.id }
    val current = incoming.associateBy { it.id }
    val unchanged = incoming.filter { previous[it.id]?.signature == it.signature }.map { it.id }.toSet()
    return PaymentReminderReplacement(
        incoming.map { it.copy(generation = if (it.id in unchanged) previous.getValue(it.id).generation else generation) },
        old.filter { current[it.id]?.signature != it.signature },
        delivered.intersect(current.keys) + incoming.filter { it.id !in unchanged && it.at <= now }.map { it.id },
    )
}


FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/ReminderWorker.kt SHA256 5e05810dbe611b4e8bc392038083c6aecf5310bd6763efd6fa08ac8ff3d0044c
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException

/** Serial storage work; the caller supplies the platform-thread result dispatcher. */
internal class ReminderWorker(private val dispatchResult: (() -> Unit) -> Unit) : AutoCloseable {
    private val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor { task ->
        Thread(task, "cabal-payment-reminders")
    }

    fun execute(operation: () -> Any?, reply: (Result<Any?>) -> Unit) {
        try {
            executor.execute {
                val result = runCatching(operation)
                dispatchResult { reply(result) }
            }
        } catch (error: RejectedExecutionException) {
            dispatchResult { reply(Result.failure(error)) }
        }
    }

    // Drain accepted work without blocking the UI during engine cleanup.
    override fun close() { executor.shutdown() }
}


FILE android/app/src/main/kotlin/py/finanzaslocales/finanzas_locales/WalletNotificationListenerService.kt SHA256 d72fd48b81598e587cc6a9ae47bc612aa7a372860190cc6ad9bad877750b61aa
package py.finanzaslocales.finanzas_locales

import android.Manifest
import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.service.notification.NotificationListenerService
import android.service.notification.StatusBarNotification
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

class WalletNotificationListenerService : NotificationListenerService() {
    companion object {
        var connectedInstance: WalletNotificationListenerService? = null
            private set
    }

    override fun onListenerConnected() {
        super.onListenerConnected()
        if (BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED) connectedInstance = this
    }

    override fun onListenerDisconnected() {
        connectedInstance = null
        super.onListenerDisconnected()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        if (connectedInstance === this) connectedInstance = null
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    fun readActiveWalletNotifications(): List<Map<String, Any?>> {
        check(BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED) { "Herramientas de desarrollo desactivadas." }
        return (activeNotifications ?: emptyArray())
            .filter { NotificationQueue.defaultPackages.contains(it.packageName) }
            .sortedByDescending { it.postTime }
            .map { envelope(it, false).toFlutterMap() }
    }

    override fun onNotificationPosted(sbn: StatusBarNotification?) {
        if (sbn == null) return
        val isTest = BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED && sbn.packageName == packageName && sbn.tag == "wallet_developer_test"
        val preferences = getSharedPreferences(NotificationQueue.preferencesName, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        if (!isTest && !preferences.getBoolean(NotificationQueue.enabledKey, false)) return
        val allowed = preferences.getStringSet(NotificationQueue.packagesKey, NotificationQueue.defaultPackages)
            ?: NotificationQueue.defaultPackages
        if (!isTest && (!allowed.contains(sbn.packageName) ||
            (!NotificationQueue.defaultPackages.contains(sbn.packageName) && !BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED))) return

        val queue = NotificationQueue.read(preferences)
        val isUpdate = queue.any { it.optString("notificationKey") == sbn.key }
        val envelope = envelope(sbn, isUpdate)
        queue.add(envelope)
        val cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L
        val retained = queue.filter { it.optLong("postTime", 0L) >= cutoff }.takeLast(100)
        NotificationQueue.write(preferences, retained)
        NotificationQueue.onCaptured?.invoke()
        // Proposals are reviewed inside the app; no system review notification.
    }

    private fun envelope(sbn: StatusBarNotification, isUpdate: Boolean): JSONObject {
        val extras = sbn.notification.extras
        val isTest = BuildConfig.DEVELOPER_TOOLS_ENABLED && sbn.packageName == packageName && sbn.tag == "wallet_developer_test"
        val lines = extras.getCharSequenceArray(Notification.EXTRA_TEXT_LINES)
            ?.map { it.toString() } ?: emptyList()
        return JSONObject().apply {
            put("version", 1)
            put("captureId", java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            put("packageName", if (isTest) "com.google.android.apps.walletnfcrel" else sbn.packageName)
            put("isTest", isTest)
            put("notificationKey", sbn.key)
            put("id", sbn.id)
            put("tag", sbn.tag ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            put("postTime", sbn.postTime)
            put("title", extras.getCharSequence(if (isTest) "testMerchant" else Notification.EXTRA_TITLE)?.toString() ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            put("text", extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TEXT)?.toString() ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            put("bigText", extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_BIG_TEXT)?.toString() ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            put("subText", extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_SUB_TEXT)?.toString() ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            put("summaryText", extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_SUMMARY_TEXT)?.toString() ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            put("textLines", JSONArray(lines))
            put("isUpdate", isUpdate)
        }
    }


}

internal object NotificationQueue {
    var onCaptured: (() -> Unit)? = null
    const val preferencesName = "notification_listener"
    const val enabledKey = "enabled"
    const val diagnosticKey = "diagnostic_mode"
    const val packagesKey = "allowed_packages"
    const val queueKey = "envelope_queue_v1"
    val defaultPackages = setOf("com.google.android.gms", "com.google.android.apps.walletnfcrel")

    fun read(preferences: SharedPreferences): MutableList<JSONObject> {
        val stored = preferences.getString(queueKey, "[]") ?: "[]"
        val json = if (stored.startsWith("v1:")) DeviceCrypto.decrypt(stored) else stored
        val array = JSONArray(json)
        if (!stored.startsWith("v1:")) {
            check(preferences.edit().putString(queueKey, DeviceCrypto.encrypt(json)).commit())
        }
        return (0 until array.length()).mapNotNull { array.optJSONObject(it) }.toMutableList()
    }

    fun write(preferences: SharedPreferences, values: List<JSONObject>) {
        check(preferences.edit().putString(queueKey, DeviceCrypto.encrypt(JSONArray(values).toString())).commit())
    }
}


FILE lib/app/app.dart SHA256 04054695b37885b88a1253505841ee3b4986a5eaf0729f49fb64b7c49e356121
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_localizations/flutter_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../core/security/app_lock_service.dart';
import '../core/notifications/native_notification_bridge.dart';
import '../core/notifications/payment_reminder_coordinator.dart';
import '../core/config/feature_flags.dart';
import '../features/payments/domain/pending_payment_service.dart';
import '../shared/widgets/app_lock_gate.dart';
import 'providers.dart';
import 'router.dart';
import 'theme/app_theme.dart';

final class FinanzasApp extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const FinanzasApp({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<FinanzasApp> createState() => _FinanzasAppState();
}

class _FinanzasAppState extends ConsumerState<FinanzasApp>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  bool _processing = false;
  bool _initialReminderActionRead = false;
  bool _syncingReminders = false;
  bool _reminderRefreshRequested = false;
  bool _refreshRequested = false;
  List<PendingPayment>? _reminderPayments;
  bool _reminderForceRequested = false;
  final _reminderCoordinator = PaymentReminderCoordinator();
  final _messenger = GlobalKey<ScaffoldMessengerState>();
  late final NativeNotificationBridge _captureBridge;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    _captureBridge = ref.read(nativeNotificationBridgeProvider);
    _captureBridge.setCaptureHandler(() async {
      if (mounted &&
          WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {
        await _ingestNativeQueue();
      }
    });
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _ingestNativeQueue());
  }

  Future<void> _reconcileReminders({
    List<PendingPayment>? payments,
    bool force = false,
  }) async {
    if (payments != null) _reminderPayments = payments;
    _reminderForceRequested |= force;
    if (!_initialReminderActionRead || !mounted) return;
    if (_syncingReminders) {
      _reminderRefreshRequested = true;
      return;
    }
    _syncingReminders = true;
    try {
      do {
        _reminderRefreshRequested = false;
        final force = _reminderForceRequested;
        _reminderForceRequested = false;
        final payments =
            (force ? null : _reminderPayments) ??
            await ref.read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider).list();
        if (!mounted) return;
        await _reminderCoordinator.synchronize(
          ref.read(paymentReminderBridgeProvider),
          payments,
          force: force,
        );
      } while (_reminderRefreshRequested && mounted);
    } catch (_) {
      // Storage and platform failures are retried on the next change/resume;
      // permission is requested only from the explicit reminder controls.
    } finally {
      _syncingReminders = false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> _ingestNativeQueue() async {
    if (_processing) {
      _refreshRequested = true;
      return;
    }
    _processing = true;
    try {
      try {
        final paymentId = await ref
            .read(paymentReminderBridgeProvider)
            .consumeOpenAction();
        if (mounted && paymentId != null) {
          final payment = await ref
              .read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider)
              .read(paymentId);
          if (mounted) {
            ref
                .read(routerProvider)
                .go(
                  payment == null
                      ? '/payments'
                      : '/payments/${Uri.encodeComponent(paymentId)}',
                );
          }
        }
      } catch (_) {
        // Platform channel is unavailable in host rendering/tests.
      } finally {
        _initialReminderActionRead = true;
      }
      final action = await ref
          .read(nativeNotificationBridgeProvider)
          .getInitialAction();
      if (action == 'py.finanzaslocales.QUICK_EXPENSE') {
        ref.read(routerProvider).go('/transactions/new?type=expense');
      } else if (action == 'py.finanzaslocales.OPEN_INBOX') {
        ref.read(routerProvider).go('/inbox');
      }
      final settings = ref.read(settingsProvider);
      final wallet = await settings.readBool('notification_listener_enabled');
      final sms =
          developerToolsEnabled &&
          await settings.readBool('sms_notifications_enabled');
      if (!developerToolsEnabled) {
        await settings.write('diagnostic_mode', 'false');
        await settings.write('sms_notifications_enabled', 'false');
      }
      await ref
          .read(nativeNotificationBridgeProvider)
          .configure(
            enabled: wallet || sms,
            diagnosticMode:
                developerToolsEnabled &&
                await settings.readBool('diagnostic_mode'),
            allowedPackages: {
              if (wallet) ...{
                'com.google.android.gms',
                'com.google.android.apps.walletnfcrel',
              },
              if (sms) 'com.google.android.apps.messaging',
            },
          );
      final events = await ref
          .read(nativeNotificationBridgeProvider)
          .queuedEnvelopes();
      for (final event in events) {
        if (event.isTest && !developerToolsEnabled) continue;
        if (event.packageName == 'com.google.android.apps.messaging' && sms) {
          await ref.read(smsNotificationIngestionProvider).ingest(event);
        } else if (wallet || (developerToolsEnabled && event.isTest)) {
          await ref.read(notificationIngestionProvider).process(event);
        }
        await ref.read(nativeNotificationBridgeProvider).acknowledge(event);
      }
      await ref.read(notificationIngestionProvider).purgeExpiredRawEvents();
    } catch (_) {
      // Android access may be unavailable; explicit refresh remains available.
    } finally {
      try {
        final result = await ref
            .read(scheduledTemplateServiceProvider)
            .processDue();
        if (mounted &&
            (result.registered.isNotEmpty || result.pending.isNotEmpty)) {
          _messenger.currentState?.showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text(
                '${result.registered.length} pagos pendientes preparados. ${result.pending.length} por revisar.',
              ),
              action: SnackBarAction(
                label: 'Ver',
                onPressed: () => ref.read(routerProvider).push('/payments'),
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      } catch (_) {
        // Failed storage access leaves the schedule intact for the next opening.
      }
      try {
        await ref.read(budgetServiceProvider).ensureCurrentMonth();
      } catch (_) {
        if (mounted) {
          _messenger.currentState?.showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text(
                'No se pudieron preparar los presupuestos mensuales. Volvé a intentar desde Análisis.',
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      await _reconcileReminders(force: true);
      _processing = false;
      if (_refreshRequested && mounted) {
        _refreshRequested = false;
        await _ingestNativeQueue();
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _captureBridge.setCaptureHandler(null);
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) _ingestNativeQueue();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    ref.listen(pendingPaymentsProvider, (_, next) {
      if (next.hasValue) _reconcileReminders(payments: next.requireValue);
    });
    final router = ref.watch(routerProvider);
    final lock = AppLockService(ref.watch(settingsProvider));
    return MaterialApp.router(
      scaffoldMessengerKey: _messenger,
      title: 'Cabal',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.light(),
      darkTheme: AppTheme.dark(),
      themeMode: ThemeMode.system,
      locale: const Locale('es', 'PY'),
      supportedLocales: const [Locale('es', 'PY'), Locale('es')],
      localizationsDelegates: const [
        GlobalMaterialLocalizations.delegate,
        GlobalWidgetsLocalizations.delegate,
        GlobalCupertinoLocalizations.delegate,
      ],
      routerConfig: router,
      builder: (context, child) =>
          AppLockGate(service: lock, child: child ?? const SizedBox.shrink()),
    );
  }
}

TransactionType _parseType(String? value) {
  if (value == null) return TransactionType.expense;
  return TransactionType.values
          .where((type) => type.name == value)
          .firstOrNull ??
      TransactionType.expense;
}

final class _StartScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _StartScreen();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    return AsyncValueView(
      value: ref.watch(onboardingCompleteProvider),
      data: (complete) {
        WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
          if (context.mounted) {
            context.go(complete ? '/dashboard' : '/onboarding');
          }
        });
        return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
      },
    );
  }
}

final class _AppShell extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AppShell({required this.shell});
  final StatefulNavigationShell shell;
  static const destinations = [
    NavigationDestination(
      icon: PendingCountBadge(
        showCount: false,
        child: Icon(Icons.home_outlined),
      ),
      label: 'Inicio',
    ),
    NavigationDestination(
      icon: Icon(Icons.receipt_long_outlined),
      label: 'Movimientos',
    ),
    NavigationDestination(
      icon: Icon(Icons.account_balance_wallet_outlined),
      label: 'Cuentas',
    ),
    NavigationDestination(
      icon: Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined),
      label: 'Análisis',
    ),
    NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.more_horiz), label: 'Más'),
  ];
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, c) {
      if (c.maxWidth >= 840) {
        return Scaffold(
          body: SafeArea(
            child: Row(
              children: [
                NavigationRail(
                  selectedIndex: shell.currentIndex,
                  onDestinationSelected: (i) => shell.goBranch(i),
                  labelType: NavigationRailLabelType.all,
                  destinations: [
                    for (final d in destinations)
                      NavigationRailDestination(
                        icon: d.icon,
                        label: Text(d.label),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                Expanded(child: shell),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
      return Scaffold(
        body: SafeArea(bottom: false, child: shell),
        bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
          selectedIndex: shell.currentIndex,
          onDestinationSelected: (i) => shell.goBranch(i),
          destinations: destinations,
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}


FILE lib/core/backup/backup_service.dart SHA256 8deff42b0076d3e37aeba4eaeafc6b94bea4ffd933c07d385598ab10cdfbd8bc
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:isolate';

import 'package:sqlite3/sqlite3.dart' as sqlite;

import '../database/encrypted_storage.dart';
import '../security/device_crypto.dart';

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../database/app_database.dart';
import '../errors/app_exception.dart';
import 'backup_codec.dart';
import '../../features/transactions/domain/ledger_models.dart';

final class BackupService {
  BackupService(
    this.database, {
    BackupCodec? codec,
    Uuid? uuid,
    this.directory,
    this.encryptionKey,
  }) : codec = codec ?? BackupCodec(),
       uuid = uuid ?? const Uuid();

  final Directory? directory;
  final Future<String> Function()? encryptionKey;
  final AppDatabase database;
  final BackupCodec codec;
  final Uuid uuid;

  Future<Uint8List> createBytes(String password) async {
    final tempDirectory = directory ?? await getTemporaryDirectory();
    final snapshot = File(
      p.join(tempDirectory.path, 'backup-${uuid.v4()}.sqlite'),
    );
    try {
      final escapedPath = snapshot.path.replaceAll("'", "''");
      await database.customStatement("VACUUM INTO '$escapedPath'");
      final accountCountExpression = database.accounts.id.count();
      final accountCountQuery = database.selectOnly(database.accounts)
        ..addColumns([accountCountExpression]);
      final accounts = await accountCountQuery
          .map((row) => row.read(accountCountExpression) ?? 0)
          .getSingle();
      final transactionCountExpression = database.ledgerTransactions.id.count();
      final transactionCountQuery = database.selectOnly(
        database.ledgerTransactions,
      )..addColumns([transactionCountExpression]);
      final transactions = await transactionCountQuery
          .map((row) => row.read(transactionCountExpression) ?? 0)
          .getSingle();
      final currencies =
          await (database.selectOnly(database.accounts, distinct: true)
                ..addColumns([database.accounts.currencyCode]))
              .map((row) => row.read(database.accounts.currencyCode)!)
              .get();
      if (!isPlaintextDatabase(snapshot)) {
        final key = await (encryptionKey?.call() ?? DeviceCrypto.databaseKey());
        final path = snapshot.path;
        await portableSnapshotInBackground(path, key);
      }
      return await codec.encode(
        databaseBytes: await snapshot.readAsBytes(),
        password: password,
        summary: BackupSummary(
          createdAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
          schemaVersion: database.schemaVersion,
          accountCount: accounts,
          transactionCount: transactions,
          currencyCodes: currencies,
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      if (await snapshot.exists()) await snapshot.delete();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> createWithPicker(String password) async {
    final bytes = await createBytes(password);
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final name =
        'finanzas-${now.year}${now.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}${now.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}.finbackup';
    final path = await FilePicker.saveFile(
      dialogTitle: 'Guardar backup cifrado',
      fileName: name,
      type: FileType.custom,
      allowedExtensions: const ['finbackup'],
      bytes: bytes,
    );
    return path != null;
  }

  Future<(Uint8List, BackupSummary)?> pickForRestore() async {
    final result = await FilePicker.pickFile(
      type: FileType.custom,
      allowedExtensions: const ['finbackup'],
    );
    if (result == null) return null;
    final Uint8List data;
    try {
      data = await result.readAsBytes();
    } catch (_) {
      throw const BackupException('No se pudo leer el archivo elegido.');
    }
    return (data, codec.inspect(data));
  }

  Future<void> restore(
    Uint8List encrypted,
    String password, {
    void Function(String)? onProgress,
  }) async {
    onProgress?.call('Descifrando backup…');
    final decoded = await codec.decode(encrypted, password: password);
    if (decoded.summary.schemaVersion != database.schemaVersion) {
      throw const BackupException(
        'La versión de la base del backup no es compatible.',
      );
    }
    final tempDirectory = directory ?? await getTemporaryDirectory();
    final candidate = File(
      p.join(tempDirectory.path, 'restore-${uuid.v4()}.sqlite'),
    );
    try {
      onProgress?.call('Validando datos…');
      await candidate.writeAsBytes(decoded.databaseBytes, flush: true);
      final expected = <String, List<String>>{};
      for (final table in database.allTables) {
        expected[table.actualTableName] = table.$columns
            .map((column) => column.$name)
            .toList();
      }
      final path = candidate.path;
      final version = database.schemaVersion;
      final rows = await validateSnapshotInBackground(path, version, expected);
      onProgress?.call('Guardando copia de seguridad…');
      final safetyDirectory =
          directory ?? await getApplicationSupportDirectory();
      final safety = File(
        p.join(safetyDirectory.path, 'before-restore-${uuid.v4()}.finbackup'),
      );
      await safety.writeAsBytes(await createBytes(password), flush: true);
      onProgress?.call('Restaurando datos…');
      await database.transaction(() async {
        database.restoreGeneration++;
        await database.customStatement('PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys = ON');
        for (final table in expected.keys) {
          await database.customStatement('DELETE FROM "$table"');
        }
        for (final entry in rows.entries) {
          final columns = expected[entry.key]!;
          final names = columns.map((name) => '"$name"').join(',');
          final placeholders = List.filled(columns.length, '?').join(',');
          for (final row in entry.value) {
            await database.customStatement(
              'INSERT INTO "${entry.key}" ($names) VALUES ($placeholders)',
              columns.map((column) => row[column]).toList(),
            );
          }
        }
        if ((await database.customSelect('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').get())
            .isNotEmpty) {
          throw const BackupException(
            'El backup contiene referencias de datos inválidas.',
          );
        }
      });
      database.notifyUpdates({
        for (final table in database.allTables)
          TableUpdate(table.actualTableName),
      });
      onProgress?.call('Restauración completada');
    } finally {
      if (await candidate.exists()) await candidate.delete();
    }
  }
}

Map<String, List<Map<String, Object?>>> readValidatedSnapshot(
  String path,
  int version,
  Map<String, List<String>> expected,
) {
  final source = sqlite.sqlite3.open(path, mode: sqlite.OpenMode.readOnly);
  try {
    if (source.select('PRAGMA integrity_check').single.values.single != 'ok' ||
        source.select('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').isNotEmpty ||
        source.select('PRAGMA user_version').single.values.single != version) {
      throw const BackupException(
        'La base del backup no pasó la validación de integridad.',
      );
    }
    final actual = source
        .select(
          "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'",
        )
        .map((row) => row['name'] as String)
        .toSet();
    if (actual.length != expected.length ||
        !actual.containsAll(expected.keys)) {
      throw const BackupException(
        'El esquema del backup no corresponde a esta app.',
      );
    }
    final result = <String, List<Map<String, Object?>>>{};
    for (final entry in expected.entries) {
      final columns = source
          .select('PRAGMA table_info("${entry.key}")')
          .map((row) => row['name'] as String)
          .toSet();
      if (columns.length != entry.value.length ||
          !columns.containsAll(entry.value)) {
        throw const BackupException(
          'El esquema del backup contiene columnas incompatibles.',
        );
      }
      result[entry.key] = source
          .select('SELECT * FROM "${entry.key}"')
          .map((row) => Map<String, Object?>.from(row))
          .toList();
    }
    final accounts = {
      for (final row in result['accounts'] ?? <Map<String, Object?>>[])
        row['id'],
    };
    final merchants = {
      for (final row in result['merchants'] ?? <Map<String, Object?>>[])
        row['id'],
    };
    final categories = {
      for (final row in result['categories'] ?? <Map<String, Object?>>[])
        row['id'],
    };
    final transactions = {
      for (final row in result['transactions'] ?? <Map<String, Object?>>[])
        row['id']: row,
    };
    final pending = <String, Map<String, dynamic>>{};
    final linkedTransactions = <String>{};
    for (final row in result['app_settings'] ?? <Map<String, Object?>>[]) {
      final key = row['key'] as String;
      if (!key.startsWith('pending_payment:')) continue;
      if (!key.startsWith('pending_payment:v1:')) {
        throw const FormatException('Unknown payment version');
      }
      final p = Map<String, dynamic>.from(
        jsonDecode(row['value'] as String) as Map,
      );
      if (p['version'] != 1 ||
          key != 'pending_payment:v1:${p['id']}' ||
          !accounts.contains(p['accountId']) ||
          (p['merchantId'] != null && !merchants.contains(p['merchantId'])) ||
          p['amountMinor'] is! int ||
          (p['amountMinor'] as int) <= 0 ||
          p['revision'] is! int ||
          (p['revision'] as int) < 0 ||
          p['cancelled'] is! bool ||
          p['planId'] is! String ||
          p['description'] is! String ||
          p['note'] is! String ||
          !PaymentChannel.values.any(
            (channel) => channel.name == p['paymentChannel'],
          ) ||
          p['installmentNumber'] is! int ||
          p['installmentCount'] is! int ||
          (p['installmentCount'] as int) < 1 ||
          (p['installmentCount'] as int) > 120 ||
          (p['installmentNumber'] as int) < 1 ||
          (p['installmentNumber'] as int) > (p['installmentCount'] as int)) {
        throw const FormatException('Invalid pending payment');
      }
      DateTime.parse(p['dueDate'] as String);
      var total = 0;
      final splitCategories = <String>{};
      for (final part in p['splits'] as List) {
        if (!categories.contains(part['categoryId']) ||
            !splitCategories.add(part['categoryId'] as String) ||
            part['amountMinor'] is! int ||
            (part['amountMinor'] as int) < 0) {
          throw const FormatException('Invalid split');
        }
        total += part['amountMinor'] as int;
      }
      if ((p['splits'] as List).isNotEmpty && total != p['amountMinor']) {
        throw const FormatException('Invalid split total');
      }
      var paid = 0;
      final seen = <String>{};
      for (final payment in p['payments'] as List) {
        final id = payment['transactionId'] as String;
        final tx = transactions[id];
        if (!seen.add(id) ||
            !linkedTransactions.add(id) ||
            tx == null ||
            tx['type'] != 'expense' ||
            !(tx['external_fingerprint'] as String? ?? '').startsWith(
              'pending:${p['id']}:',
            ) ||
            payment['amountMinor'] is! int ||
            (payment['amountMinor'] as int) <= 0 ||
            !accounts.contains(payment['accountId'])) {
          throw const FormatException('Invalid payment reference');
        }
        if (tx['status'] == 'confirmed') paid += tx['amount_minor'] as int;
      }
      if ((p['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0) < 0 ||
          paid + (p['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0) > (p['amountMinor'] as int)) {
        throw const FormatException('Overpaid obligation');
      }
      final reminders = p['reminders'] as Map;
      if (reminders['enabled'] is! bool ||
          reminders['onDueDate'] is! bool ||
          (reminders['hour'] as int) < 0 ||
          (reminders['hour'] as int) > 23 ||
          (reminders['minute'] as int) < 0 ||
          (reminders['minute'] as int) > 59 ||
          (reminders['daysBefore'] as int) < 0 ||
          (reminders['daysBefore'] as int) > 60) {
        throw const FormatException('Invalid reminders');
      }
      pending[p['id'] as String] = p;
    }
    for (final p in pending.values) {
      final carriedMinor = p['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0;
      final classified = (p['splits'] as List).isNotEmpty;
      Map<String, int> transferParts(Object? raw, int amount) {
        final values = <String, int>{};
        for (final part in raw as List? ?? []) {
          final id = part['categoryId'];
          final value = part['amountMinor'];
          if (id is! String ||
              !categories.contains(id) ||
              value is! int ||
              value < 0 ||
              values.containsKey(id)) {
            throw const FormatException('Invalid carry category');
          }
          values[id] = value;
        }
        final sum = values.values.fold<int>(0, (n, v) => n + v);
        if (classified ? sum != amount : values.isNotEmpty) {
          throw const FormatException('Invalid carry category total');
        }
        values.removeWhere((_, value) => value == 0);
        return values;
      }

      final cumulative = transferParts(p['carriedSplits'], carriedMinor);
      var history = p['carries'] as List?;
      // Old v1 records with a concrete target can be normalized without
      // inventing debt; an orphaned carried amount never qualifies.
      if (carriedMinor > 0 && history == null && p['carriedTo'] != null) {
        history = [
          {
            'to': p['carriedTo'],
            'amountMinor': carriedMinor,
            'splits': p['carriedSplits'] ?? [],
          },
        ];
        p['carries'] = history;
      }
      history ??= [];
      if (carriedMinor == 0) {
        if (history.isNotEmpty ||
            p['carriedTo'] != null ||
            cumulative.isNotEmpty) {
          throw const FormatException('Unexpected carry trace');
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (history.isEmpty || p['carriedTo'] != history.last['to']) {
        throw const FormatException('Missing carry history');
      }
      var total = 0;
      final categoriesTransferred = <String, int>{};
      for (final carry in history) {
        final target = pending[carry['to']];
        final amount = carry['amountMinor'];
        if (target == null ||
            target['planId'] != p['planId'] ||
            target['installmentCount'] != p['installmentCount'] ||
            (target['installmentNumber'] as int) <=
                (p['installmentNumber'] as int) ||
            amount is! int ||
            amount <= 0) {
          throw const FormatException('Invalid carry destination');
        }
        total += amount;
        for (final entry in transferParts(carry['splits'], amount).entries) {
          categoriesTransferred[entry.key] =
              (categoriesTransferred[entry.key] ?? 0) + entry.value;
        }
      }
      if (total != carriedMinor ||
          cumulative.length != categoriesTransferred.length ||
          cumulative.entries.any(
            (entry) => categoriesTransferred[entry.key] != entry.value,
          )) {
        throw const FormatException('Invalid carry history total');
      }
      final sourceCategories = {
        for (final part in p['splits'] as List)
          part['categoryId']: part['amountMinor'] as int,
      };
      if (cumulative.entries.any(
        (entry) => (sourceCategories[entry.key] ?? 0) < entry.value,
      )) {
        throw const FormatException('Carry exceeds source category');
      }
    }
    for (final row in result['app_settings'] ?? <Map<String, Object?>>[]) {
      final payment =
          pending[(row['key'] as String).replaceFirst(
            'pending_payment:v1:',
            '',
          )];
      if (payment != null) row['value'] = jsonEncode(payment);
    }
    return result;
  } on BackupException {
    rethrow;
  } catch (_) {
    throw const BackupException('No se pudo validar la base del backup.');
  } finally {
    source.close();
  }
}

Future<void> portableSnapshotInBackground(String path, String key) =>
    Isolate.run(() => makePortableSnapshot(path, key));
Future<Map<String, List<Map<String, Object?>>>> validateSnapshotInBackground(
  String path,
  int version,
  Map<String, List<String>> expected,
) => Isolate.run(() => readValidatedSnapshot(path, version, expected));

Future<void> cleanupBackupWorkingFiles() async {
  final directory = await getTemporaryDirectory();
  final owned = RegExp(
    r'^(backup|restore)-[0-9a-f-]{36}\.sqlite(?:-(?:wal|shm))?$',
  );
  await for (final entry in directory.list(followLinks: false)) {
    if (entry is File && owned.hasMatch(p.basename(entry.path))) {
      await entry.delete();
    }
  }
}

FILE lib/core/notifications/payment_reminder_bridge.dart SHA256 135f2f0abd52ea87c49744fd3e7e5e4bfb24860721cdc698ba37c0097eb8d917
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

final class PaymentReminderRequest {
  const PaymentReminderRequest({
    required this.id,
    required this.obligationId,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    required this.localDateTime,
    this.revision,
  });
  final String id, obligationId, title, body;
  final DateTime localDateTime;
  final int? revision;
  Map<String, Object> toMap() {
    if (id.isEmpty || obligationId.isEmpty || localDateTime.isUtc) {
      throw ArgumentError('El recordatorio requiere identidad y hora local.');
    }
    return {
      'id': id,
      'obligationId': obligationId,
      'title': title,
      'body': body,
      'localDateTime': localDateTime.toIso8601String(),
      'revision': ?revision,
    };
  }
}

final class PaymentReminderBridge {
  const PaymentReminderBridge({
    this.channel = const MethodChannel('finanzas_locales/payment_reminders'),
  });
  final MethodChannel channel;
  Future<bool> permission() async =>
      await channel.invokeMethod<bool>('permission') ?? false;
  Future<bool> requestPermission() async =>
      await channel.invokeMethod<bool>('requestPermission') ?? false;

  /// Replaces the entire agenda, including while permission is denied.
  Future<bool> reconcile(List<PaymentReminderRequest> requests) async =>
      await channel.invokeMethod<bool>('replaceSchedule', {
        'requests': requests.map((r) => r.toMap()).toList(),
      }) ??
      false;
  Future<String?> consumeOpenAction() =>
      channel.invokeMethod<String>('consumeOpenAction');
}


FILE lib/core/notifications/payment_reminder_coordinator.dart SHA256 cd33334a99ec1d041cb070903194a2775bdd637dbacdfc478eaf57b72eb246d4
import 'dart:convert';

import '../../features/payments/domain/pending_payment_service.dart';
import 'payment_reminder_bridge.dart';
import 'payment_reminder_scheduler.dart';

/// The native agenda contains notifications only, never financial mutations.
Future<bool> synchronizePaymentReminders(
  PaymentReminderBridge bridge,
  List<PendingPayment> payments, {
  DateTime? now,
}) async {
  return PaymentReminderCoordinator().synchronize(bridge, payments, now: now);
}

/// Reuses an unchanged foreground agenda; opening/resuming forces a native refresh.
final class PaymentReminderCoordinator {
  String? _lastSignature;
  bool _lastPermitted = false;

  Future<bool> synchronize(
    PaymentReminderBridge bridge,
    List<PendingPayment> payments, {
    DateTime? now,
    bool force = false,
  }) async {
    final inputs = <PaymentReminderInput>[];
    var invalidSettings = false;
    for (final payment in payments) {
      if (payment.cancelled || payment.remainingMinor <= 0) continue;
      try {
        final settings = payment.reminders;
        settings.validate();
        inputs.add(
          PaymentReminderInput(
            id: payment.id,
            revision: payment.revision,
            title: payment.description.isEmpty
                ? 'Pago pendiente'
                : payment.description,
            dueDate: payment.dueDate,
            enabled: settings.enabled,
            daysBefore: settings.daysBefore,
            hour: settings.hour,
            minute: settings.minute,
            onDueDate: settings.onDueDate,
          ),
        );
      } on FormatException {
        // Legacy unsupported settings cannot retain another obligation's stale alarm.
        invalidSettings = true;
      }
    }
    // Native storage needs historical identities to preserve displayed notices;
    // only future requests become alarms, and newly introduced past ones are skipped.
    final requests = const PaymentReminderScheduler().schedule(
      inputs,
      now: now ?? DateTime.now(),
      retainElapsed: true,
    );
    final signature = jsonEncode(requests.map((r) => r.toMap()).toList());
    if (!force && signature == _lastSignature) {
      return _lastPermitted && !invalidSettings;
    }
    final permitted = await bridge.reconcile(requests);
    _lastSignature = signature;
    _lastPermitted = permitted;
    return permitted && !invalidSettings;
  }
}


FILE lib/core/notifications/payment_reminder_scheduler.dart SHA256 17601308a139a55ffa6bf62a19f3304713e3afeb039001266448b0ee9e64b98a
import 'payment_reminder_bridge.dart';

final class PaymentReminderInput {
  const PaymentReminderInput({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.dueDate,
    this.revision,
    this.enabled = true,
    this.onDueDate = true,
    this.daysBefore = 3,
    this.hour = 9,
    this.minute = 0,
  });
  final String id, title;
  final DateTime dueDate;
  final int? revision;
  final bool enabled, onDueDate;
  final int daysBefore, hour, minute;
}

final class PaymentReminderScheduler {
  const PaymentReminderScheduler();
  List<PaymentReminderRequest> schedule(
    Iterable<PaymentReminderInput> inputs, {
    required DateTime now,
    bool retainElapsed = false,
  }) {
    final result = <PaymentReminderRequest>[];
    final seen = <String>{};
    for (final input in inputs) {
      if (input.daysBefore < 0 ||
          input.daysBefore > 60 ||
          input.hour < 0 ||
          input.hour > 23 ||
          input.minute < 0 ||
          input.minute > 59 ||
          input.id.isEmpty ||
          !seen.add(input.id)) {
        throw ArgumentError('Configuración de recordatorio inválida.');
      }
      if (!input.enabled) continue;
      for (final offset in {input.daysBefore, if (input.onDueDate) 0}) {
        final date = DateTime(
          input.dueDate.year,
          input.dueDate.month,
          input.dueDate.day - offset,
          input.hour,
          input.minute,
        );
        if (!retainElapsed && !date.isAfter(now)) continue;
        result.add(
          PaymentReminderRequest(
            id: '${input.id}:${date.toIso8601String()}',
            obligationId: input.id,
            title: input.title,
            body: 'Revisar pago',
            localDateTime: date,
            revision: input.revision,
          ),
        );
      }
    }
    result.sort((a, b) => a.localDateTime.compareTo(b.localDateTime));
    return result;
  }
}


FILE lib/core/settings/settings_repository.dart SHA256 10ac4697cc4cdd0487b730cb787344ddedaf33bacbb42ae183f2a1a6fba45770
import '../database/app_database.dart';

final class SettingsRepository {
  SettingsRepository(this.database);

  final AppDatabase database;

  Future<String?> read(String key) async {
    final row = await (database.select(
      database.appSettings,
    )..where((table) => table.key.equals(key))).getSingleOrNull();
    return row?.value;
  }

  Stream<String?> watch(String key) {
    return (database.select(database.appSettings)
          ..where((table) => table.key.equals(key)))
        .watchSingleOrNull()
        .map((row) => row?.value);
  }

  Future<void> write(String key, String value) =>
      database.transaction(() async {
        if (await read(key) == value) return;
        await database
            .into(database.appSettings)
            .insertOnConflictUpdate(
              AppSettingsCompanion.insert(
                key: key,
                value: value,
                updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
              ),
            );
      });

  Future<bool> readBool(String key, {bool fallback = false}) async {
    final value = await read(key);
    if (value == null) return fallback;
    return value == 'true';
  }
}

  Future<void> _edit(BuildContext context, AccountRecord account) async {
    try {
      final db = ref.read(databaseProvider);
      final aliases = await (db.select(
        db.accountAliases,
      )..where((t) => t.accountId.equals(account.id))).get();
      final card = await (db.select(
        db.creditCardProfiles,
      )..where((t) => t.accountId.equals(account.id))).getSingleOrNull();
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      final draft = await showCreateAccountDialog(
        context,
        initial: CreateAccountDraft(
          name: account.name,
          type: AccountType.values.byName(account.type),
          currencyCode: account.currencyCode,
          initialBalanceMinor: account.initialBalanceMinor,
          aliases: aliases.map((a) => a.alias).toList(),
          includeInAvailableMoney: account.includeInAvailableMoney,
          includeInNetWorth: account.includeInNetWorth,
          creditLimitMinor: card?.creditLimitMinor,
          closingDay: card?.closingDay,
          dueDay: card?.dueDay,
        ),
      );
      if (draft == null || !mounted) return;
      await ref.read(accountsServiceProvider).edit(account.id, draft);
    } catch (error) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _archive(BuildContext context, AccountRecord account) async {
    try {
      await ref
          .read(accountsServiceProvider)
          .setArchived(account.id, !account.isArchived);
    } catch (error) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showCreate(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    final draft = await showCreateAccountDialog(context);
    if (draft == null || !context.mounted) return;
    try {
      await ref.read(accountsServiceProvider).createAccount(draft);
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Cuenta creada.')));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
      }
    }
  }
}
