FILE lib/features/payments/domain/pending_payment_models.dart SHA256 1bf24178ce93681bb694d4215f8baa6d9688a14e0da04704da51d49c40f37728
import '../../transactions/domain/ledger_models.dart';

final class PaymentReminderSettings {
  const PaymentReminderSettings({
    this.enabled = true,
    this.daysBefore = 3,
    this.hour = 9,
    this.minute = 0,
    this.onDueDate = true,
  });
  final bool enabled, onDueDate;
  final int daysBefore, hour, minute;
  void validate() {
    if (daysBefore < 0 ||
        daysBefore > 60 ||
        hour < 0 ||
        hour > 23 ||
        minute < 0 ||
        minute > 59) {
      throw const FormatException('Recordatorio inválido.');
    }
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'enabled': enabled,
    'daysBefore': daysBefore,
    'hour': hour,
    'minute': minute,
    'onDueDate': onDueDate,
  };
  factory PaymentReminderSettings.fromJson(Map j) => PaymentReminderSettings(
    enabled: j['enabled'] as bool,
    daysBefore: j['daysBefore'] as int,
    hour: j['hour'] as int,
    minute: j['minute'] as int,
    onDueDate: j['onDueDate'] as bool,
  );
}

final class PendingPayment {
  PendingPayment(this.data);
  final Map<String, dynamic> data;
  String get id => data['id'] as String;
  String get planId => data['planId'] as String;
  int get installmentNumber => data['installmentNumber'] as int;
  int get installmentCount => data['installmentCount'] as int;
  int get amountMinor => data['amountMinor'] as int;
  DateTime get dueDate => DateTime.parse(data['dueDate'] as String);
  String get accountId => data['accountId'] as String;
  String? get merchantId => data['merchantId'] as String?;
  String get description => data['description'] as String;
  String get note => data['note'] as String;
  String? get sourceReference => data['sourceReference'] as String?;
  PaymentChannel get paymentChannel =>
      PaymentChannel.values.byName(data['paymentChannel'] as String);
  int get revision => data['revision'] as int;
  bool get cancelled => data['cancelled'] as bool;
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get payments => (data['payments'] as List)
      .map((p) => Map<String, dynamic>.from(p as Map))
      .toList();
  List<Map<String, dynamic>>? effectivePayments;
  int? effectivePaidMinor;
  int get paidMinor =>
      effectivePaidMinor ??
      payments.fold(0, (n, p) => n + (p['amountMinor'] as int));
  int get remainingMinor => cancelled
      ? 0
      : amountMinor - paidMinor - (data['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0);
  List<SplitDraft> get splits => (data['splits'] as List)
      .map(
        (s) => SplitDraft(
          categoryId: s['categoryId'] as String,
          amountMinor: s['amountMinor'] as int,
        ),
      )
      .toList();
  Map<String, int> get remainingSplits {
    final result = <String, int>{
      for (final s in splits) s.categoryId: s.amountMinor,
    };
    for (final payment in effectivePayments ?? payments) {
      for (final part in payment['splits'] as List? ?? []) {
        final id = part['categoryId'] as String;
        result[id] = (result[id] ?? 0) - (part['amountMinor'] as int);
      }
    }
    for (final part in data['carriedSplits'] as List? ?? []) {
      final id = part['categoryId'] as String;
      result[id] = (result[id] ?? 0) - (part['amountMinor'] as int);
    }
    result.removeWhere((_, amount) => amount <= 0);
    final total = result.values.fold<int>(0, (sum, amount) => sum + amount);
    if (total != remainingMinor && total > 0) {
      var allocated = 0;
      final keys = result.keys.toList();
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        final amount = i == keys.length - 1
            ? remainingMinor - allocated
            : remainingMinor * result[keys[i]]! ~/ total;
        result[keys[i]] = amount;
        allocated += amount;
      }
      result.removeWhere((_, amount) => amount <= 0);
    }
    return result;
  }

  PaymentReminderSettings get reminders =>
      PaymentReminderSettings.fromJson(data['reminders'] as Map);
}


FILE lib/features/payments/domain/pending_payment_service.dart SHA256 aa20f1c759f85d392c41d711778a5dcecc6abed80fa09c137af1734467df85c7
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../../core/money/money.dart';
import '../../transactions/data/ledger_service.dart';
import '../../transactions/domain/ledger_models.dart';
import '../../transactions/domain/payment_channel_policy.dart';
import 'pending_payment_models.dart';
export 'pending_payment_models.dart';

final class PendingPaymentService {
  PendingPaymentService(this.database);
  final AppDatabase database;
  static const prefix = 'pending_payment:v1:';
  void _checkBook(int? expected) {
    if (expected != null && expected != database.restoreGeneration) {
      throw StateError('Los datos fueron restaurados.');
    }
  }

  Stream<List<PendingPayment>> watch() => database
      .customSelect(
        'SELECT count(*) AS count FROM app_settings',
        readsFrom: {
          database.appSettings,
          database.ledgerTransactions,
          database.transactionSplits,
        },
      )
      .watch()
      .asyncMap((_) => list());
  Future<List<PendingPayment>> _rawList() async =>
      (await (database.select(
            database.appSettings,
          )..where((t) => t.key.like('$prefix%'))).get())
          .map(
            (r) => PendingPayment(
              Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(r.value) as Map),
            ),
          )
          .toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => a.dueDate.compareTo(b.dueDate));
  Future<List<PendingPayment>> list() async {
    final rows = await _rawList();
    for (final p in rows) {
      await _hydrate(p);
    }
    return rows;
  }

  Future<void> _hydrate(PendingPayment p) async {
    final active = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
    for (final payment in p.payments) {
      final tx =
          await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)
                ..where((t) => t.id.equals(payment['transactionId'] as String)))
              .getSingleOrNull();
      if (tx != null && tx.status != 'deleted' && tx.status != 'reversed') {
        final splits = await (database.select(
          database.transactionSplits,
        )..where((s) => s.transactionId.equals(tx.id))).get();
        active.add({
          ...payment,
          'amountMinor': tx.amountMinor,
          'paidAt': tx.occurredAt.toIso8601String(),
          'accountId': tx.fromAccountId,
          'splits': [
            for (final s in splits)
              {'categoryId': s.categoryId, 'amountMinor': s.amountMinor},
          ],
        });
      }
    }
    p.effectivePayments = active;
    p.effectivePaidMinor = active.fold<int>(
      0,
      (n, p) => n + (p['amountMinor'] as int),
    );
  }

  Future<PendingPayment?> read(String id) async {
    final row = await (database.select(
      database.appSettings,
    )..where((t) => t.key.equals('$prefix$id'))).getSingleOrNull();
    if (row == null) return null;
    final p = PendingPayment(
      Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(row.value) as Map),
    );
    await _hydrate(p);
    return p;
  }

  Future<void> _save(PendingPayment p) async {
    await database
        .into(database.appSettings)
        .insertOnConflictUpdate(
          AppSettingsCompanion.insert(
            key: '$prefix${p.id}',
            value: jsonEncode(p.data),
            updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
          ),
        );
  }

  Future<PendingPayment> _checked(String id, int revision) async {
    final p = await read(id);
    if (p == null || p.revision != revision || p.cancelled) {
      throw StateError('El pendiente cambió.');
    }
    return p;
  }

  Future<void> _account(String id, {bool allowCredit = false}) async {
    final a = await (database.select(
      database.accounts,
    )..where((a) => a.id.equals(id))).getSingleOrNull();
    if (a == null ||
        a.isArchived ||
        a.currencyCode != 'PYG' ||
        (!allowCredit && a.type == 'creditCard')) {
      throw StateError('Elegí una cuenta activa sin crédito.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> validateDraft({
    required int amountMinor,
    required String accountId,
    String? merchantId,
    PaymentChannel paymentChannel = PaymentChannel.other,
    List<SplitDraft> splits = const [],
    PaymentReminderSettings reminders = const PaymentReminderSettings(),
    bool allowCredit = false,
  }) async {
    reminders.validate();
    if (amountMinor <= 0) {
      throw const FormatException('Importe o cuotas inválidos.');
    }
    await _account(accountId, allowCredit: allowCredit);
    final account = await (database.select(
      database.accounts,
    )..where((a) => a.id.equals(accountId))).getSingle();
    if (!PaymentChannelPolicy.allowed(
      TransactionType.expense,
      from: AccountType.values.byName(account.type),
    ).contains(PaymentChannelPolicy.canonical(paymentChannel))) {
      throw const FormatException('Canal inválido.');
    }
    if (splits.isNotEmpty &&
        splits.fold<int>(0, (n, s) => n + s.amountMinor) != amountMinor) {
      throw const FormatException('Las categorías deben sumar el importe.');
    }
    for (final s in splits) {
      final c = await (database.select(
        database.categories,
      )..where((c) => c.id.equals(s.categoryId))).getSingleOrNull();
      if (s.amountMinor <= 0 ||
          c == null ||
          c.isArchived ||
          c.kind == 'income') {
        throw const FormatException('Categoría inválida.');
      }
    }
    if (merchantId != null) {
      final m = await (database.select(
        database.merchants,
      )..where((m) => m.id.equals(merchantId))).getSingleOrNull();
      if (m == null || m.isArchived) throw StateError('Comercio inválido.');
    }
  }

  // Largest remainders keep integer row totals exact. When amount is at most
  // the remaining capacities, no category can exceed its available capacity.
  List<int> _allocateAmounts(int amount, List<int> weights) {
    final total = weights.fold<int>(0, (sum, value) => sum + value);
    if (total == 0) return List<int>.filled(weights.length, 0);
    final result = [for (final weight in weights) amount * weight ~/ total];
    var residue = amount - result.fold<int>(0, (sum, value) => sum + value);
    final order = [for (var i = 0; i < weights.length; i++) i]
      ..sort((a, b) {
        final remainder = (amount * weights[b] % total).compareTo(
          amount * weights[a] % total,
        );
        return remainder != 0 ? remainder : a.compareTo(b);
      });
    for (final index in order) {
      if (residue == 0) break;
      if (weights[index] > 0) {
        result[index]++;
        residue--;
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  Future<List<PendingPayment>> createPlan({
    required int amountMinor,
    required int installmentCount,
    required DateTime firstDueDate,
    required String accountId,
    String? merchantId,
    String description = '',
    String note = '',
    PaymentChannel paymentChannel = PaymentChannel.other,
    List<SplitDraft> splits = const [],
    String? sourceReference,
    DateTime? occurredAt,
    PaymentReminderSettings reminders = const PaymentReminderSettings(),
    int? expectedBookGeneration,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    _checkBook(expectedBookGeneration);
    if (installmentCount < 1 ||
        installmentCount > 120 ||
        amountMinor < installmentCount) {
      throw const FormatException('Importe o cuotas inválidos.');
    }
    await validateDraft(
      amountMinor: amountMinor,
      accountId: accountId,
      merchantId: merchantId,
      paymentChannel: paymentChannel,
      splits: splits,
      reminders: reminders,
      allowCredit: sourceReference != null,
    );
    final plan = const Uuid().v4(), result = <PendingPayment>[];
    var allocated = 0;
    final categoryAllocated = List<int>.filled(splits.length, 0);
    for (var i = 0; i < installmentCount; i++) {
      final amount = i == installmentCount - 1
          ? amountMinor - allocated
          : amountMinor ~/ installmentCount;
      allocated += amount;
      final dueMonth = DateTime(firstDueDate.year, firstDueDate.month + i, 1);
      final last = DateTime(dueMonth.year, dueMonth.month + 1, 0).day;
      final parts = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
      final row = _allocateAmounts(amount, [
        for (var j = 0; j < splits.length; j++)
          splits[j].amountMinor - categoryAllocated[j],
      ]);
      for (var j = 0; j < splits.length; j++) {
        categoryAllocated[j] += row[j];
        parts.add({'categoryId': splits[j].categoryId, 'amountMinor': row[j]});
      }
      final p = PendingPayment({
        'version': 1,
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'planId': plan,
        'installmentNumber': i + 1,
        'installmentCount': installmentCount,
        'amountMinor': amount,
        'dueDate': DateTime(
          dueMonth.year,
          dueMonth.month,
          firstDueDate.day > last ? last : firstDueDate.day,
        ).toIso8601String(),
        'accountId': accountId,
        'merchantId': merchantId,
        'description': description,
        'note': note,
        'paymentChannel': paymentChannel.name,
        'splits': parts,
        'revision': 0,
        'cancelled': false,
        'payments': <Map<String, dynamic>>[],
        'reminders': reminders.toJson(),
        'occurredAt': occurredAt?.toIso8601String(),
        'sourceReference': sourceReference,
      });
      await _save(p);
      result.add(p);
    }
    return result;
  });
  Future<String> pay({
    required String id,
    required int expectedRevision,
    required int amountMinor,
    required DateTime paidAt,
    required String accountId,
    bool carryRemainder = false,
    int? expectedBookGeneration,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    _checkBook(expectedBookGeneration);
    final p = await _checked(id, expectedRevision);
    await _account(accountId);
    if (amountMinor <= 0 || amountMinor > p.remainingMinor) {
      throw const FormatException('Importe inválido.');
    }
    PendingPayment? next;
    final remainder = p.remainingMinor - amountMinor;
    if (carryRemainder && remainder > 0) {
      final following =
          (await list())
              .where(
                (q) =>
                    q.planId == p.planId &&
                    q.installmentNumber > p.installmentNumber &&
                    !q.cancelled &&
                    q.remainingMinor > 0,
              )
              .toList()
            ..sort(
              (a, b) => a.installmentNumber.compareTo(b.installmentNumber),
            );
      if (following.isEmpty) throw StateError('No existe otra cuota.');
      next = following.first;
    }
    final residual = p.remainingSplits;
    var allocated = 0;
    final portions = <SplitDraft>[];
    final keys = residual.keys.toList();
    for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      final key = keys[i];
      final part = i == keys.length - 1
          ? amountMinor - allocated
          : amountMinor * residual[key]! ~/ p.remainingMinor;
      allocated += part;
      if (part > 0) {
        portions.add(SplitDraft(categoryId: key, amountMinor: part));
      }
    }
    final payingAccount = await (database.select(
      database.accounts,
    )..where((a) => a.id.equals(accountId))).getSingle();
    final effectiveChannel = payingAccount.type == 'cash'
        ? PaymentChannel.cash
        : (p.paymentChannel == PaymentChannel.cash
              ? PaymentChannel.other
              : p.paymentChannel);
    final tx = await LedgerService(database).create(
      TransactionDraft(
        type: TransactionType.expense,
        amount: Money(amountMinor, currencyCode: 'PYG'),
        occurredAt: paidAt,
        fromAccountId: accountId,
        merchantId: p.merchantId,
        description: p.description,
        note: p.note,
        paymentChannel: effectiveChannel,
        splits: portions,
        externalFingerprint: 'pending:${p.id}:${p.revision}',
        sourceReference: p.sourceReference ?? p.planId,
      ),
    );
    p.data['payments'] = [
      ...p.payments,
      {
        'transactionId': tx,
        'amountMinor': amountMinor,
        'paidAt': paidAt.toIso8601String(),
        'accountId': accountId,
        'splits': [
          for (final s in portions)
            {'categoryId': s.categoryId, 'amountMinor': s.amountMinor},
        ],
      },
    ];
    p.data['accountId'] = accountId;
    p.data['revision'] = p.revision + 1;
    if (next != null) {
      final carried = [
        for (final key in keys)
          {
            'categoryId': key,
            'amountMinor':
                residual[key]! -
                portions
                    .where((s) => s.categoryId == key)
                    .fold<int>(0, (n, s) => n + s.amountMinor),
          },
      ];
      final cumulativeCarry = <String, int>{};
      for (final part in [
        ...(p.data['carriedSplits'] as List? ?? []),
        ...carried,
      ]) {
        final key = part['categoryId'] as String;
        cumulativeCarry[key] =
            (cumulativeCarry[key] ?? 0) + (part['amountMinor'] as int);
      }
      p.data['carriedSplits'] = [
        for (final e in cumulativeCarry.entries)
          {'categoryId': e.key, 'amountMinor': e.value},
      ];
      p.data['carries'] = [
        ...(p.data['carries'] as List? ?? []),
        {'to': next.id, 'amountMinor': remainder, 'splits': carried},
      ];
      p.data['carriedTo'] = next.id;
      final nextSplits = <String, int>{
        for (final s in next.splits) s.categoryId: s.amountMinor,
      };
      for (final part in carried) {
        final key = part['categoryId'] as String;
        nextSplits[key] = (nextSplits[key] ?? 0) + (part['amountMinor'] as int);
      }
      next.data['splits'] = [
        for (final e in nextSplits.entries)
          {'categoryId': e.key, 'amountMinor': e.value},
      ];
      p.data['carriedMinor'] =
          (p.data['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0) + remainder;
      next.data['amountMinor'] = next.amountMinor + remainder;
      next.data['revision'] = next.revision + 1;
      await _save(next);
    }
    await _save(p);
    return tx;
  });
  Future<void> edit({
    required String id,
    required int expectedRevision,
    required int amountMinor,
    required DateTime dueDate,
    String? accountId,
    bool following = false,
    int? expectedBookGeneration,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    _checkBook(expectedBookGeneration);
    final p = await _checked(id, expectedRevision);
    if (accountId != null) await _account(accountId);
    final targets = following
        ? (await list())
              .where(
                (q) =>
                    q.planId == p.planId &&
                    q.installmentNumber >= p.installmentNumber &&
                    !q.cancelled &&
                    q.remainingMinor > 0,
              )
              .toList()
        : [p];
    for (final q in targets) {
      if (q.paidMinor > 0) continue;
      final carriedMinor = q.data['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0;
      if (amountMinor <= 0 || amountMinor < q.paidMinor + carriedMinor) {
        throw const FormatException(
          'El importe debe cubrir lo pagado y trasladado.',
        );
      }
      final carried = <String, int>{};
      for (final part in q.data['carriedSplits'] as List? ?? []) {
        final key = part['categoryId'] as String;
        carried[key] = (carried[key] ?? 0) + (part['amountMinor'] as int);
      }
      final residual = [
        for (final split in q.splits)
          split.amountMinor - (carried[split.categoryId] ?? 0),
      ];
      final weights = residual.any((value) => value > 0)
          ? residual
          : [for (final split in q.splits) split.amountMinor];
      final allocation = _allocateAmounts(amountMinor - carriedMinor, weights);
      final parts = <Map<String, dynamic>>[
        for (var i = 0; i < q.splits.length; i++)
          {
            'categoryId': q.splits[i].categoryId,
            'amountMinor':
                (carried[q.splits[i].categoryId] ?? 0) + allocation[i],
          },
      ];
      q.data['splits'] = parts;
      q.data['amountMinor'] = amountMinor;
      final offset = q.installmentNumber - p.installmentNumber;
      final month = DateTime(dueDate.year, dueDate.month + offset, 1);
      final last = DateTime(month.year, month.month + 1, 0).day;
      q.data['dueDate'] = DateTime(
        month.year,
        month.month,
        dueDate.day > last ? last : dueDate.day,
      ).toIso8601String();
      if (accountId != null) q.data['accountId'] = accountId;
      q.data['revision'] = q.revision + 1;
      await _save(q);
    }
  });
  Future<void> cancel(
    String id, {
    required int expectedRevision,
    int? expectedBookGeneration,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    _checkBook(expectedBookGeneration);
    final p = await _checked(id, expectedRevision);
    p.data['cancelled'] = true;
    p.data['revision'] = p.revision + 1;
    await _save(p);
  });
  Future<void> updateReminders(
    String id, {
    required int expectedRevision,
    required PaymentReminderSettings settings,
    bool following = true,
    int? expectedBookGeneration,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    _checkBook(expectedBookGeneration);
    final p = await _checked(id, expectedRevision);
    settings.validate();
    final targets = following
        ? (await list()).where(
            (q) =>
                q.planId == p.planId &&
                q.installmentNumber >= p.installmentNumber &&
                !q.cancelled &&
                q.remainingMinor > 0,
          )
        : [p];
    for (final q in targets) {
      q.data['reminders'] = settings.toJson();
      q.data['revision'] = q.revision + 1;
      await _save(q);
    }
  });
}


FILE lib/features/payments/presentation/payment_reminder_fields.dart SHA256 e792df8a787ae99a070d5aa76e6c9bdff261c735337c4518a4ecf4b276cf860e
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../domain/pending_payment_models.dart';

class PaymentReminderFields extends StatelessWidget {
  const PaymentReminderFields({
    required this.value,
    required this.onChanged,
    super.key,
  });
  final PaymentReminderSettings value;
  final ValueChanged<PaymentReminderSettings> onChanged;
  void change({bool? enabled, int? days, int? hour, int? minute, bool? due}) =>
      onChanged(
        PaymentReminderSettings(
          enabled: enabled ?? value.enabled,
          daysBefore: days ?? value.daysBefore,
          hour: hour ?? value.hour,
          minute: minute ?? value.minute,
          onDueDate: due ?? value.onDueDate,
        ),
      );
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Column(
    children: [
      SwitchListTile(
        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
        title: const Text('Recordatorios'),
        value: value.enabled,
        onChanged: (v) => change(enabled: v),
      ),
      if (value.enabled) ...[
        DropdownButtonFormField<int>(
          initialValue: value.daysBefore,
          isExpanded: true,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Días antes'),
          items: [
            for (var i = 0; i <= 60; i++)
              DropdownMenuItem(value: i, child: Text('$i')),
          ],
          onChanged: (v) => change(days: v),
        ),
        SwitchListTile(
          contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          title: const Text('El día del vencimiento'),
          value: value.onDueDate,
          onChanged: (v) => change(due: v),
        ),
        ListTile(
          contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          title: const Text('Hora'),
          trailing: Text(
            '${value.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${value.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}',
          ),
          onTap: () async {
            final t = await showTimePicker(
              context: context,
              initialTime: TimeOfDay(hour: value.hour, minute: value.minute),
            );
            if (t != null) change(hour: t.hour, minute: t.minute);
          },
        ),
      ],
    ],
  );
}


FILE lib/features/payments/presentation/payments_screen.dart SHA256 9a9a1823237e3e1abe7f4d6f1a55209342b67771c6dd66c55d3e4dd25a21813a
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../../core/money/money.dart';
import '../../../core/money/pyg_input_formatter.dart';
import '../domain/pending_payment_models.dart';
import 'payment_reminder_fields.dart';

class PaymentsScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const PaymentsScreen({super.key});
  @override
  ConsumerState<PaymentsScreen> createState() => _PaymentsScreenState();
}

class _PaymentsScreenState extends ConsumerState<PaymentsScreen> {
  String _filter = 'pending';
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
    appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Pagos pendientes')),
    body: Column(
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
          child: Wrap(
            spacing: 8,
            children: [
              for (final entry in const {
                'pending': 'Pendientes',
                'paid': 'Con pagos',
                'all': 'Todos',
              }.entries)
                ChoiceChip(
                  label: Text(entry.value),
                  selected: _filter == entry.key,
                  onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _filter = entry.key),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: ref
              .watch(pendingPaymentsProvider)
              .when(
                loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                error: (e, _) => Center(child: Text('$e')),
                data: (rows) {
                  final payments = rows
                      .where(
                        (p) =>
                            _filter == 'all' ||
                            (_filter == 'paid'
                                ? p.paidMinor > 0
                                : !p.cancelled && p.remainingMinor > 0),
                      )
                      .toList();
                  if (payments.isEmpty) {
                    return Center(
                      child: Text(
                        _filter == 'pending'
                            ? 'Sin pagos pendientes'
                            : 'Sin pagos en esta vista',
                      ),
                    );
                  }
                  return ListView.builder(
                    itemCount: payments.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                      final p = payments[i];
                      return ListTile(
                        title: Text(
                          p.description.isEmpty
                              ? 'Pago pendiente'
                              : p.description,
                        ),
                        subtitle: Text(
                          '${MaterialLocalizations.of(context).formatShortDate(p.dueDate)} · ${p.installmentNumber}/${p.installmentCount}\n${p.cancelled
                              ? 'Cancelado'
                              : p.remainingMinor <= 0
                              ? 'Pagado'
                              : Money(p.remainingMinor, currencyCode: 'PYG').format()}',
                        ),
                        onTap: () => context.push('/payments/${p.id}'),
                      );
                    },
                  );
                },
              ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
}

class PaymentDetailScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const PaymentDetailScreen({required this.paymentId, super.key});
  final String paymentId;
  @override
  ConsumerState<PaymentDetailScreen> createState() =>
      _PaymentDetailScreenState();
}

class _PaymentDetailScreenState extends ConsumerState<PaymentDetailScreen> {
  final _amount = TextEditingController();
  late final int _bookGeneration;
  int? _loadedRevision;
  String? _account;
  DateTime _paidAt = DateTime.now();
  bool _carry = false, _busy = false, _userSelectedAccount = false;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _bookGeneration = ref.read(databaseProvider).restoreGeneration;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _amount.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _message(Object error) {
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _run(Future<void> Function() action) async {
    if (_busy) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (e) {
      _message(e);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  String _money(int value) => Money(value, currencyCode: 'PYG').format();
  String _date(DateTime value) =>
      MaterialLocalizations.of(context).formatShortDate(value);
  Future<DateTime?> _pickDate(DateTime value) => showDatePicker(
    context: context,
    initialDate: value,
    firstDate: DateTime(2000),
    lastDate: DateTime(2100),
  );
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
    appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Detalle de pago')),
    body: ref
        .watch(pendingPaymentsProvider)
        .when(
          loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
          error: (e, _) => Center(child: Text('$e')),
          data: (rows) {
            final matches = rows.where((p) => p.id == widget.paymentId);
            if (matches.isEmpty) {
              return const Center(child: Text('Pago no disponible'));
            }
            final p = matches.first;
            if (_loadedRevision != p.revision) {
              _loadedRevision = p.revision;
              _amount.text = p.remainingMinor > 0
                  ? Money(p.remainingMinor).format(withCode: false).trim()
                  : '';
              _account = null;
              _userSelectedAccount = false;
              _carry = false;
            }
            final hasNext = rows.any(
              (q) =>
                  q.planId == p.planId &&
                  q.installmentNumber > p.installmentNumber &&
                  !q.cancelled &&
                  q.remainingMinor > 0,
            );
            final payable = !p.cancelled && p.remainingMinor > 0;
            return ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              children: [
                Text(
                  p.description.isEmpty ? 'Pago pendiente' : p.description,
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
                ),
                ListTile(
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  title: Text(
                    'Cuota ${p.installmentNumber}/${p.installmentCount}',
                  ),
                  subtitle: Text(_date(p.dueDate)),
                ),
                Text('Previsto: ${_money(p.amountMinor)}'),
                Text('Pagado: ${_money(p.paidMinor)}'),
                Text(
                  p.cancelled
                      ? 'Cancelado'
                      : 'Pendiente: ${_money(p.remainingMinor)}',
                ),
                if (p.data['carriedTo'] != null)
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () =>
                        context.push('/payments/${p.data['carriedTo']}'),
                    child: const Text('Ver cuota con resto trasladado'),
                  ),
                for (final payment in p.effectivePayments ?? p.payments)
                  ListTile(
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    title: Text(_money(payment['amountMinor'] as int)),
                    subtitle: Text(
                      _date(DateTime.parse(payment['paidAt'] as String)),
                    ),
                    onTap: () => context.push(
                      '/transactions/new?edit=${payment['transactionId']}',
                    ),
                  ),
                if (payable) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextFormField(
                    key: const ValueKey('paid-amount'),
                    controller: _amount,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                    inputFormatters: [PygInputFormatter()],
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'Importe pagado',
                    ),
                  ),
                  ListTile(
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    title: const Text('Fecha de pago'),
                    subtitle: Text(_date(_paidAt)),
                    onTap: _busy
                        ? null
                        : () async {
                            final date = await _pickDate(_paidAt);
                            if (date != null && mounted) {
                              setState(() => _paidAt = date);
                            }
                          },
                  ),
                  ref
                      .watch(accountsProvider)
                      .when(
                        loading: () => const LinearProgressIndicator(),
                        error: (e, _) => Text('$e'),
                        data: (all) {
                          final accounts = all
                              .where(
                                (a) =>
                                    !a.isArchived &&
                                    a.type != 'creditCard' &&
                                    a.currencyCode == 'PYG',
                              )
                              .toList();
                          String? suggested = p.accountId;
                          DateTime? latestPaidAt;
                          for (final installment in rows.where(
                            (q) => q.planId == p.planId,
                          )) {
                            for (final payment
                                in installment.effectivePayments ??
                                    installment.payments) {
                              final date = DateTime.parse(
                                payment['paidAt'] as String,
                              );
                              if (latestPaidAt == null ||
                                  !date.isBefore(latestPaidAt)) {
                                latestPaidAt = date;
                                suggested = payment['accountId'] as String?;
                              }
                            }
                          }
                          final candidate = _userSelectedAccount
                              ? _account
                              : suggested;
                          final selected =
                              accounts.any((a) => a.id == candidate)
                              ? candidate
                              : !_userSelectedAccount &&
                                    accounts.any((a) => a.id == p.accountId)
                              ? p.accountId
                              : null;
                          _account = selected;
                          return DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                            key: ValueKey('account-$selected'),
                            initialValue: selected,
                            isExpanded: true,
                            decoration: const InputDecoration(
                              labelText: 'Cuenta',
                            ),
                            items: [
                              for (final a in accounts)
                                DropdownMenuItem(
                                  value: a.id,
                                  child: Text(a.name),
                                ),
                            ],
                            onChanged: _busy
                                ? null
                                : (v) => setState(() {
                                    _account = v;
                                    _userSelectedAccount = true;
                                  }),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                  if (hasNext)
                    CheckboxListTile(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      title: const Text('Trasladar resto a la siguiente cuota'),
                      value: _carry,
                      onChanged: _busy
                          ? null
                          : (v) => setState(() => _carry = v ?? false),
                    ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  FilledButton(
                    onPressed: _busy
                        ? null
                        : () => _run(() async {
                            if (_account == null) {
                              throw StateError('Elegí una cuenta.');
                            }
                            await ref
                                .read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider)
                                .pay(
                                  id: p.id,
                                  expectedRevision: p.revision,
                                  amountMinor: Money.parse(_amount.text)
                                      .minorUnits,
                                  paidAt: _paidAt,
                                  accountId: _account!,
                                  carryRemainder: hasNext && _carry,
                                  expectedBookGeneration: _bookGeneration,
                                );
                          }),
                    child: const Text('Confirmar pago'),
                  ),
                  if (p.paidMinor == 0)
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _edit(p),
                      child: const Text('Editar pendiente'),
                    ),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _reminders(p),
                    child: const Text('Recordatorios'),
                  ),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _cancel(p),
                    child: const Text('Cancelar pendiente'),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
  );
  Future<void> _cancel(PendingPayment p) async {
    final accepted = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Cancelar pendiente'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('Volver'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('Confirmar cancelación'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (accepted == true && mounted) {
      await _run(
        () => ref
            .read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider)
            .cancel(
              p.id,
              expectedRevision: p.revision,
              expectedBookGeneration: _bookGeneration,
            ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _edit(PendingPayment p) async {
    final amount = TextEditingController(
      text: Money(p.amountMinor).format(withCode: false).trim(),
    );
    var due = p.dueDate, following = false;
    final accepted = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, update) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Editar pendiente'),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                TextField(
                  controller: amount,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                  inputFormatters: [PygInputFormatter()],
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Importe previsto',
                  ),
                ),
                ListTile(
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  title: const Text('Vencimiento'),
                  subtitle: Text(_date(due)),
                  onTap: () async {
                    final date = await _pickDate(due);
                    if (date != null && context.mounted) {
                      update(() => due = date);
                    }
                  },
                ),
                if (p.installmentNumber < p.installmentCount)
                  CheckboxListTile(
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    title: const Text('Esta cuota y las siguientes'),
                    value: following,
                    onChanged: (v) => update(() => following = v ?? false),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
              child: const Text('Cancelar'),
            ),
            FilledButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
              child: const Text('Guardar'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (accepted == true && mounted) {
      await _run(
        () => ref
            .read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider)
            .edit(
              id: p.id,
              expectedRevision: p.revision,
              amountMinor: Money.parse(amount.text).minorUnits,
              dueDate: due,
              following: following,
              expectedBookGeneration: _bookGeneration,
            ),
      );
    }
    // The dialog route may still be animating when its future completes.
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => amount.dispose());
  }

  Future<void> _reminders(PendingPayment p) async {
    var settings = p.reminders, following = true;
    final accepted = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, update) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Recordatorios'),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                PaymentReminderFields(
                  value: settings,
                  onChanged: (v) => update(() => settings = v),
                ),
                if (p.installmentNumber < p.installmentCount)
                  CheckboxListTile(
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    title: const Text('Esta cuota y las siguientes'),
                    value: following,
                    onChanged: (v) => update(() => following = v ?? false),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
              child: const Text('Cancelar'),
            ),
            FilledButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
              child: const Text('Guardar'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (accepted == true && mounted) {
      await _run(() async {
        await ref
            .read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider)
            .updateReminders(
              p.id,
              expectedRevision: p.revision,
              settings: settings,
              following: following,
              expectedBookGeneration: _bookGeneration,
            );
        if (settings.enabled) {
          try {
            if (!await ref
                .read(paymentReminderBridgeProvider)
                .requestPermission()) {
              _message('Guardado. Recordatorios sin permiso.');
            }
          } catch (_) {
            _message('Guardado. Recordatorios no disponibles.');
          }
        }
      });
    }
  }
}


FILE lib/features/reports/data/report_service.dart SHA256 8fbf8dda082deedea6c58ebf8fc4e2d92fdbffbfc2a28902c376a8a0e15d3cd0
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import '../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../payments/domain/pending_payment_service.dart';

typedef ReportRequest = ({
  DateTime start,
  DateTime end,
  String? category,
  bool monthlyInstallments,
});
typedef AnalysisRequest = ({ReportRequest period, String? account});

class ReportTotals {
  const ReportTotals({
    required this.income,
    required this.expenses,
    required this.refunds,
    this.pendingExpenses = 0,
  });
  final int income, expenses, refunds, pendingExpenses;
  int get netExpenses => expenses - refunds;
  int get balance => income - netExpenses;
}

class ReportGroup {
  const ReportGroup({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.totals,
    required this.expenseCount,
  });
  final String? id;
  final String name;
  final ReportTotals totals;
  final int expenseCount;
}

class ReportMonth {
  const ReportMonth(this.month, this.totals);
  final DateTime month;
  final ReportTotals totals;
}

class ReportAnalysis {
  const ReportAnalysis({
    required this.totals,
    required this.previous,
    required this.previousStart,
    required this.previousEnd,
    required this.months,
    required this.categories,
    required this.merchants,
    required this.accounts,
    required this.expenseCount,
    required this.unclassifiedAmount,
    required this.unclassifiedCount,
  });
  final ReportTotals totals, previous;
  final DateTime previousStart, previousEnd;
  final List<ReportMonth> months;
  final List<ReportGroup> categories, merchants, accounts;
  final int expenseCount, unclassifiedAmount, unclassifiedCount;
  int get pendingMinor => totals.pendingExpenses;
  int get difference => totals.netExpenses - previous.netExpenses;
  double? get changePercent =>
      previous.netExpenses > 0 ? difference * 100 / previous.netExpenses : null;
}

class ReportService {
  ReportService(this.database);
  final AppDatabase database;
  Stream<ReportTotals> watch(ReportRequest request) =>
      watchAnalysis((period: request, account: null)).map((a) => a.totals);

  Stream<ReportAnalysis> watchAnalysis(AnalysisRequest request) {
    final period = request.period;
    final calendarMonth =
        period.start.day == 1 &&
        period.end == DateTime(period.start.year, period.start.month + 1);
    // Calendar months compare to the previous calendar month; custom ranges to
    // the preceding range with the same number of calendar days, including DST.
    final days = DateTime.utc(period.end.year, period.end.month, period.end.day)
        .difference(
          DateTime.utc(period.start.year, period.start.month, period.start.day),
        )
        .inDays;
    final previousStart = calendarMonth
        ? DateTime(period.start.year, period.start.month - 1)
        : DateTime(
            period.start.year,
            period.start.month,
            period.start.day - days,
          );
    final trendStart = calendarMonth
        ? DateTime(period.start.year, period.start.month - 5)
        : DateTime(period.start.year, period.start.month);
    final queryStart = previousStart.isBefore(trendStart)
        ? previousStart
        : trendStart;
    return database
        .customSelect(
          '''
      SELECT t.id, t.type, t.amount_minor, t.occurred_at, t.merchant_id,
        m.name AS merchant_name,
        CASE WHEN t.type = 'expense' THEN t.from_account_id ELSE t.to_account_id END AS account_id,
        a.name AS account_name, ip.id AS plan_id,
        ie.id AS entry_id, ie.period_start, ie.amount_minor AS quota, ie.installment_number,
        s.id AS split_id, s.category_id, s.amount_minor AS split_amount, c.name AS category_name
      FROM transactions t
      LEFT JOIN merchants m ON m.id = t.merchant_id
      LEFT JOIN accounts a ON a.id = CASE WHEN t.type = 'expense' THEN t.from_account_id ELSE t.to_account_id END
      LEFT JOIN installment_plans ip ON ip.transaction_id = t.id
      LEFT JOIN installment_entries ie ON ie.plan_id = ip.id
      LEFT JOIN transaction_splits s ON s.transaction_id = t.id
      LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id = s.category_id
      WHERE t.status = 'confirmed' AND t.currency_code = 'PYG'
        AND t.type IN ('income', 'expense', 'refund')
        AND (? IS NULL OR a.id = ?)
        AND ((t.occurred_at >= ? AND t.occurred_at < ?) OR EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM installment_entries eligible WHERE eligible.plan_id = ip.id
          AND eligible.period_start >= ? AND eligible.period_start < ?
        ))
      ORDER BY t.id, ie.installment_number, s.id
    ''',
          variables: [
            Variable<String>(request.account),
            Variable<String>(request.account),
            Variable.withDateTime(queryStart.toUtc()),
            Variable.withDateTime(period.end.toUtc()),
            Variable.withDateTime(
              DateTime.utc(
                queryStart.year,
                queryStart.month,
                queryStart.day,
                12,
              ),
            ),
            Variable.withDateTime(
              DateTime.utc(
                period.end.year,
                period.end.month,
                period.end.day,
                12,
              ),
            ),
          ],
          readsFrom: {
            database.ledgerTransactions,
            database.transactionSplits,
            database.installmentPlans,
            database.installmentEntries,
            database.categories,
            database.merchants,
            database.accounts,
            database.appSettings,
          },
        )
        .watch()
        .asyncMap((rows) async {
          final transactions = <String, _Transaction>{};
          for (final row in rows) {
            final t = transactions.putIfAbsent(
              row.read<String>('id'),
              () => _Transaction(row),
            );
            final split = row.readNullable<String>('split_id');
            if (split != null) {
              t.splits[split] = (
                id: row.readNullable<String>('category_id'),
                name:
                    row.readNullable<String>('category_name') ??
                    'Sin categoría',
                amount: row.read<int>('split_amount'),
              );
            }
            final entry = row.readNullable<String>('entry_id');
            if (entry != null) {
              final raw = row.read<DateTime>('period_start').toUtc();
              t.entries[entry] = (
                date: DateTime(raw.year, raw.month, raw.day),
                amount: row.read<int>('quota'),
                number: row.read<int>('installment_number'),
              );
            }
          }
          final current = _Totals(), previous = _Totals();
          final categories = <String?, _Group>{},
              merchants = <String?, _Group>{},
              accounts = <String?, _Group>{};
          final months = <DateTime, _Totals>{};
          for (
            var month = trendStart;
            month.isBefore(period.end);
            month = DateTime(month.year, month.month + 1)
          ) {
            months[month] = _Totals();
          }
          final expenseIds = <String>{}, unclassifiedIds = <String>{};
          var unclassified = 0;
          for (final transaction in transactions.values) {
            for (final impact in transaction.impacts(
              period.monthlyInstallments,
            )) {
              final parts = period.category == null
                  ? impact.parts
                  : impact.parts.where((p) => p.id == period.category).toList();
              final amount = parts.fold<int>(0, (sum, p) => sum + p.amount);
              if (amount == 0) continue;
              if (!impact.date.isBefore(
                    calendarMonth ? trendStart : period.start,
                  ) &&
                  impact.date.isBefore(period.end)) {
                months[DateTime(impact.date.year, impact.date.month)]?.add(
                  transaction.type,
                  amount,
                );
              }
              if (!impact.date.isBefore(previousStart) &&
                  impact.date.isBefore(period.start)) {
                previous.add(transaction.type, amount);
              }
              if (impact.date.isBefore(period.start) ||
                  !impact.date.isBefore(period.end)) {
                continue;
              }
              current.add(transaction.type, amount);
              accounts
                  .putIfAbsent(
                    transaction.accountId,
                    () =>
                        _Group(transaction.accountId, transaction.accountName),
                  )
                  .add(transaction, amount);
              if (transaction.type == 'income') continue;
              merchants
                  .putIfAbsent(
                    transaction.merchantId,
                    () => _Group(
                      transaction.merchantId,
                      transaction.merchantName,
                    ),
                  )
                  .add(transaction, amount);
              for (final part in parts) {
                if (part.amount == 0) continue;
                categories
                    .putIfAbsent(part.id, () => _Group(part.id, part.name))
                    .add(transaction, part.amount);
                if (part.id == null && transaction.type == 'expense') {
                  unclassified += part.amount;
                  unclassifiedIds.add(transaction.id);
                }
              }
              if (transaction.type == 'expense') expenseIds.add(transaction.id);
            }
          }
          List<ReportGroup> groups(Map<String?, _Group> values) =>
              values.values.map((g) => g.value).toList()..sort((a, b) {
                final byAmount = b.totals.expenses.compareTo(a.totals.expenses);
                return byAmount != 0 ? byAmount : a.name.compareTo(b.name);
              });
          var pending = 0;
          for (final p in await PendingPaymentService(database).list()) {
            if (p.cancelled ||
                p.remainingMinor <= 0 ||
                p.dueDate.isBefore(period.start) ||
                !p.dueDate.isBefore(period.end) ||
                (request.account != null && p.accountId != request.account)) {
              continue;
            }
            if (period.category == null) {
              pending += p.remainingMinor;
            } else {
              pending += p.remainingSplits[period.category] ?? 0;
            }
          }
          return ReportAnalysis(
            totals: ReportTotals(
              income: current.value.income,
              expenses: current.value.expenses,
              refunds: current.value.refunds,
              pendingExpenses: pending,
            ),
            previous: previous.value,
            previousStart: previousStart,
            previousEnd: period.start,
            months: months.entries
                .map((e) => ReportMonth(e.key, e.value.value))
                .toList(),
            categories: groups(categories),
            merchants: groups(merchants),
            accounts: groups(accounts),
            expenseCount: expenseIds.length,
            unclassifiedAmount: unclassified,
            unclassifiedCount: unclassifiedIds.length,
          );
        });
  }
}

typedef _Part = ({String? id, String name, int amount});
typedef _Entry = ({DateTime date, int amount, int number});

class _Impact {
  const _Impact(this.date, this.parts);
  final DateTime date;
  final List<_Part> parts;
}

class _Transaction {
  _Transaction(QueryRow row)
    : id = row.read<String>('id'),
      type = row.read<String>('type'),
      amount = row.read<int>('amount_minor'),
      date = row.read<DateTime>('occurred_at').toLocal(),
      merchantId = row.readNullable<String>('merchant_id'),
      merchantName =
          row.readNullable<String>('merchant_name') ?? 'Sin comercio',
      accountId = row.readNullable<String>('account_id'),
      accountName = row.readNullable<String>('account_name') ?? 'Sin cuenta',
      hasPlan = row.readNullable<String>('plan_id') != null;
  final String id, type, merchantName, accountName;
  final String? merchantId, accountId;
  final int amount;
  final DateTime date;
  final bool hasPlan;
  final splits = <String, _Part>{};
  final entries = <String, _Entry>{};
  Iterable<_Impact> impacts(bool quotas) sync* {
    final weights = splits.values.toList();
    if (weights.isEmpty) {
      weights.add((id: null, name: 'Sin categoría', amount: amount));
    }
    if (!quotas || type != 'expense' || !hasPlan) {
      yield _Impact(date, weights);
      return;
    }
    final schedule = entries.values.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => a.number.compareTo(b.number));
    final remaining = [for (final part in weights) part.amount];
    // Allocate each quota from remaining category weights. Integer rounding is
    // deterministic and the entire plan sums exactly to each original split.
    for (final entry in schedule) {
      final totalWeight = remaining.fold<int>(0, (sum, v) => sum + v);
      if (totalWeight <= 0) continue;
      var cumulative = 0, assigned = 0;
      final parts = <_Part>[];
      for (var i = 0; i < weights.length; i++) {
        cumulative += remaining[i];
        final total = entry.amount * cumulative ~/ totalWeight;
        final portion = total - assigned;
        assigned = total;
        remaining[i] -= portion;
        parts.add((id: weights[i].id, name: weights[i].name, amount: portion));
      }
      yield _Impact(entry.date, parts);
    }
  }
}

class _Totals {
  int income = 0, expenses = 0, refunds = 0;
  void add(String type, int amount) {
    switch (type) {
      case 'income':
        income += amount;
      case 'expense':
        expenses += amount;
      case 'refund':
        refunds += amount;
    }
  }

  ReportTotals get value =>
      ReportTotals(income: income, expenses: expenses, refunds: refunds);
}

class _Group {
  _Group(this.id, this.name);
  final String? id;
  final String name;
  final totals = _Totals();
  final ids = <String>{};
  void add(_Transaction t, int amount) {
    totals.add(t.type, amount);
    if (t.type == 'expense') ids.add(t.id);
  }

  ReportGroup get value => ReportGroup(
    id: id,
    name: name,
    totals: totals.value,
    expenseCount: ids.length,
  );
}


FILE lib/features/reports/presentation/analysis_screen.dart SHA256 c1e56c038b704b7c19d3f0a7c8c31d8262bba811d38b38ad91051a8c0b4ab996
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../budgets/presentation/budgets_screen.dart';
import 'reports_screen.dart';

class AnalysisScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AnalysisScreen({this.reports = false, super.key});
  final bool reports;
  @override
  State<AnalysisScreen> createState() => _AnalysisScreenState();
}

class _AnalysisScreenState extends State<AnalysisScreen> {
  late bool _reports = widget.reports;
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant AnalysisScreen oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.reports != widget.reports) {
      _reports = widget.reports;
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, constraints) {
      final labelStyle = Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge!;
      final textScaler = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context);
      final labelWidth = ((constraints.maxWidth - 32) / 2 - 32).clamp(
        1.0,
        double.infinity,
      );
      var segmentHeight = 48.0;
      for (final label in ['Presupuestos', 'Reportes']) {
        final painter = TextPainter(
          text: TextSpan(text: label, style: labelStyle),
          textDirection: Directionality.of(context),
          textScaler: textScaler,
        )..layout(maxWidth: labelWidth);
        if (painter.height + 24 > segmentHeight) {
          segmentHeight = painter.height + 24;
        }
        painter.dispose();
      }
      final tabs = PreferredSize(
        preferredSize: Size.fromHeight(segmentHeight + 12),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 12),
          child: SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: SegmentedButton<bool>(
              style: SegmentedButton.styleFrom(
                textStyle: labelStyle,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 16,
                  vertical: 12,
                ),
                fixedSize: Size.fromHeight(segmentHeight),
                tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
                visualDensity: VisualDensity.standard,
              ),
              segments: const [
                ButtonSegment(value: false, label: Text('Presupuestos')),
                ButtonSegment(value: true, label: Text('Reportes')),
              ],
              selected: {_reports},
              onSelectionChanged: (v) => setState(() => _reports = v.first),
              showSelectedIcon: false,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
      return IndexedStack(
        index: _reports ? 1 : 0,
        children: [
          BudgetsScreen(navigation: tabs),
          ReportsScreen(navigation: tabs),
        ],
      );
    },
  );
}

                        : '${loc.formatShortDate(_start)} – ${loc.formatShortDate(_end.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)))}',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                ),

              IconButton(
                tooltip: 'Mes siguiente',
                onPressed: () => _month(1),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),

                'Evolución',
                'Categorías',
                'Comercios',
                'Cuentas',
              ].indexed)

              4 => _ranking(
                a.accounts,

  List<Widget> _summary(ReportAnalysis a) => [
    Table(
      columnWidths: const {0: FlexColumnWidth(), 1: FlexColumnWidth(2)},
      defaultVerticalAlignment: TableCellVerticalAlignment.middle,
      children: [

              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        _summaryRow('Ingresos', a.totals.income, a.previous.income),
        _summaryRow('Gastos', a.totals.netExpenses, a.previous.netExpenses),

                'Neto',

              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20),
              child: Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                child: Semantics(
                  label:
                      'Ingresos menos gastos; no representa saldo de cuentas',
                  child: MoneyText(
                    a.totals.balance,

          ],
        ),
      ],

        child: Text('${a.unclassifiedCount} compras sin categoría'),
      ),
  ];
  TableRow _summaryRow(String label, int amount, int previous) {
    final delta = amount - previous;
    final ratio = previous == 0 ? null : delta.abs() * 100 / previous.abs();
    final change = delta == 0
        ? '— 0%'
        : previous == 0
        ? 'Nuevo'
        : '${delta > 0 ? '↑' : '↓'} ${ratio! < 1 ? '<1' : ratio.round()}%';
    return TableRow(
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
          child: Text(label),
        ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
          child: Wrap(
            alignment: WrapAlignment.end,
            crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
            spacing: 12,
            children: [
              MoneyText(amount),
              Semantics(
                label: delta == 0
                    ? 'Sin cambios respecto al período anterior'
                    : '${delta > 0 ? 'Subió' : 'Bajó'} ${Money(delta.abs()).format()} respecto al período anterior',
                child: Text(
                  change,
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelMedium,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Future<void> _filters() async {
    String? account = _account, category = _category;

        categories = ref.read(allCategoriesProvider).value ?? [];
    final accepted = await showDialog<bool>(

                ],
                onChanged: (v) => account = v,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                initialValue: category,
                isExpanded: true,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Categoría'),
                items: [
                  const DropdownMenuItem(
                    value: null,
                    child: Text('Todas las categorías'),
                  ),
                  for (final c in categories)
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: c.id, child: Text(c.name)),
                ],
                onChanged: (v) => category = v,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialog, false),
            child: const Text('Cancelar'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialog, true),
            child: const Text('Aplicar'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (accepted == true && mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _account = account;
        _category = category;
      });
    }
  }

  List<Widget> _evolution(ReportAnalysis a) => [
    _heading('Evolución mensual'),
    const SizedBox(height: 16),
    _TrendChart(months: a.months),
    const SizedBox(height: 16),
    for (final m in a.months)
      Card(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                MaterialLocalizations.of(context).formatMonthYear(m.month),
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              _amountRow('Gastos netos', m.totals.netExpenses),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              _amountRow('Ingresos', m.totals.income),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
  ];
  List<Widget> _ranking(
    List<ReportGroup> rows,
    int total,
    String title,
    String kind,
  ) => [
    _heading(title),
    if (rows.isEmpty)
      const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Text(
          'No hay datos para este reporte.',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
        ),
      ),
    for (final row in rows)
      Card(
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          onTap: () => _details(kind, row.id),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        row.name,
                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 20),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 12,
                  runSpacing: 4,
                  children: [
                    MoneyText(row.totals.expenses),
                    if (total > 0)
                      Text(
                        '${(row.totals.expenses * 100 / total).toStringAsFixed(1)} % del gasto',
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                LinearProgressIndicator(
                  value: total <= 0
                      ? 0
                      : (row.totals.expenses / total).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                  minHeight: 6,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text('${row.expenseCount} compras'),
                if (row.totals.refunds > 0) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  _amountRow('Devoluciones', row.totals.refunds),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  _amountRow('Gastos netos', row.totals.netExpenses),
                ],
                if (kind == 'account' && row.totals.income > 0) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  _amountRow('Ingresos', row.totals.income),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
  ];
  Widget _heading(String text) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
    child: Text(text, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),
  );
  Widget _amountRow(String label, int value) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, constraints) {
      final amount = Money(value).format();
      final style = Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium;
      final painter = TextPainter(
        text: TextSpan(text: amount, style: style),
        textDirection: Directionality.of(context),
        textScaler: MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context),
      )..layout();
      final amountWidth = painter.width;
      painter.dispose();
      if (constraints.maxWidth - amountWidth < 112) {
        return Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(label),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
              child: Text(amount, textAlign: TextAlign.right),
            ),
          ],
        );
      }
      return Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Expanded(child: Text(label)),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Text(amount, textAlign: TextAlign.right),
        ],
      );
    },
  );
  void _details(String kind, String? id) {
    final parameters = <String, String>{
      'report': 'true',
      'start': _start.toIso8601String(),
      'end': _end.toIso8601String(),
      if (_quotas) 'quotaPeriod': 'true',
      'category': ?_category,
      'account': ?_account,
    };
    if (id != null) parameters[kind] = id;
    if (kind == 'merchant' || kind == 'category') {
      parameters['type'] = 'purchases';
    }
    if (kind == 'merchant') {
      if (id == null) parameters['withoutMerchant'] = 'true';
    }
    if (kind == 'unclassified') parameters['type'] = 'expense';
    if (kind == 'unclassified' || kind == 'category' && id == null) {
      parameters.remove('category');
      parameters['unclassified'] = 'true';
    }
    context.push(
      Uri(path: '/transactions', queryParameters: parameters).toString(),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _period() async {
    final range = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2000),
      lastDate: DateTime(2100),
      initialDateRange: DateTimeRange(
        start: _start,
        end: _end.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
      ),
    );
    if (range != null && mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _start = range.start;
        _end = DateTime(range.end.year, range.end.month, range.end.day + 1);
      });
    }
  }
}

class _TrendChart extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TrendChart({required this.months});
  final List<ReportMonth> months;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (months.isEmpty) return const SizedBox();
    final values = [
      0,
      for (final month in months) ...[
        month.totals.income,
        month.totals.netExpenses,
      ],
    ];
    final low = values.reduce((a, b) => a < b ? a : b).toDouble(),
        high = values.reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b).toDouble();
    final padding = (high - low) == 0 ? 1.0 : (high - low) * .15;
    final colors = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Card(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Wrap(
              spacing: 20,
              children: [
                _legend('Gastos netos', colors.error),
                _legend('Ingresos', colors.primary),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              height: 210,
              child: LineChart(
                LineChartData(
                  minX: 0,
                  maxX: months.length == 1 ? 1 : (months.length - 1).toDouble(),
                  minY: low - padding,
                  maxY: high + padding,
                  borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
                  gridData: const FlGridData(
                    show: true,
                    drawVerticalLine: false,
                  ),
                  titlesData: FlTitlesData(
                    topTitles: const AxisTitles(
                      sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                    ),
                    rightTitles: const AxisTitles(
                      sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                    ),
                    leftTitles: const AxisTitles(
                      sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false),
                    ),
                    bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                      sideTitles: SideTitles(
                        showTitles: true,
                        reservedSize: 32,
                        interval: 1,
                        getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
                          final index = value.round();
                          if (value != index.toDouble() ||
                              index < 0 ||
                              index >= months.length ||
                              months.length > 6 &&
                                  index != 0 &&
                                  index != months.length - 1 &&
                                  index % ((months.length / 5).ceil()) != 0) {
                            return const SizedBox();
                          }
                          final date = months[index].month;
                          return Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                            child: Text(
                              '${date.month}/${date.year % 100}',
                              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelSmall,
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  lineTouchData: LineTouchData(
                    touchTooltipData: LineTouchTooltipData(
                      getTooltipItems: (spots) => spots
                          .map(
                            (spot) => LineTooltipItem(
                              '${spot.barIndex == 0 ? 'Gastos' : 'Ingresos'}\n${Money(spot.y.round()).format()}',
                              TextStyle(
                                color: spot.bar.color,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              ),
                            ),
                          )
                          .toList(),
                    ),
                  ),
                  lineBarsData: [
                    for (var series = 0; series < 2; series++)
                      LineChartBarData(
                        color: series == 0 ? colors.error : colors.primary,
                        barWidth: 3,
                        isCurved: false,
                        spots: [
                          for (var i = 0; i < months.length; i++)
                            FlSpot(
                              i.toDouble(),
                              (series == 0
                                      ? months[i].totals.netExpenses
                                      : months[i].totals.income)
                                  .toDouble(),
                            ),
                        ],
                        dotData: FlDotData(show: months.length <= 12),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _legend(String label, Color color) => Row(
    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
    children: [
      Container(
        width: 10,
        height: 10,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
      ),
      const SizedBox(width: 6),
      Text(label),
    ],
  );
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../app/providers.dart';

import '../../../core/security/app_lock_service.dart';

final class SettingsScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const SettingsScreen({this.backups = false, super.key});
  final bool backups;

  @override
  ConsumerState<SettingsScreen> createState() => _SettingsScreenState();
}

class _SettingsScreenState extends ConsumerState<SettingsScreen> {
  bool? _lock;
  var _busy = false;
  String? _progress;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    final settings = ref.read(settingsProvider);
    final lock = await settings.readBool('app_lock_enabled');
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _lock = lock;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: widget.backups
          ? AppBar(title: const Text('Backup cifrado'))
          : null,
      body: ListView(

          if (_busy) ...[
            const LinearProgressIndicator(),
            if (_progress != null)
