                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                child: Text(_progress!),
              ),
          ],
          if (!widget.backups) ...[
            const _SectionTitle('Organización'),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.event_outlined),
              title: const Text('Pagos pendientes'),
              onTap: () => context.push('/payments'),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.bookmark_outline),
              title: const Text('Plantillas'),
              onTap: () => context.push('/templates'),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.event_repeat),
              title: const Text('Movimientos programados'),
              onTap: () => context.push('/templates?scheduled=true'),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.contactless),
              title: const Text('Google Wallet'),
              onTap: () => context.push('/wallet-settings'),
            ),
            const _SectionTitle('Tus datos'),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.store_outlined),
              title: const Text('Comercios, categorías y asociaciones'),
              onTap: () => context.push('/catalog'),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.shield_outlined),
              title: const Text('Backup cifrado'),
              onTap: () => context.push('/backups'),
            ),
          ],
          if (widget.backups) ...[
            const _SectionTitle('Backup cifrado'),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.backup_outlined),
              title: const Text('Crear backup'),
              onTap: _busy ? null : _createBackup,
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.restore),
              title: const Text('Restaurar backup'),
              onTap: _busy ? null : _restoreBackup,
            ),
          ],
          if (!widget.backups) ...[
            const Divider(),
            const _SectionTitle('Seguridad'),
            SwitchListTile(
              secondary: const Icon(Icons.fingerprint),
              value: _lock ?? false,
              onChanged: _lock == null || _busy ? null : _setLock,
              title: const Text('Bloqueo del dispositivo'),
            ),
            const Divider(),
            if (developerToolsEnabled) ...[
              const _SectionTitle('Desarrollo'),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.developer_mode),
                title: const Text('Herramientas de desarrollo'),
                onTap: () => context.push('/developer-tools'),
              ),
              const Divider(),
            ],
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.info_outline),
              title: const Text('Información de la aplicación'),
              onTap: () => showLicensePage(
                context: context,
                applicationName: 'Cabal',
                applicationVersion: '0.1.0',
              ),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _setLock(bool value) async {
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final service = AppLockService(ref.read(settingsProvider));
      final changed = await service.setEnabled(value);
      if (changed && mounted) setState(() => _lock = value);
    } catch (error) {
      _message('$error');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _busy = false;
          _progress = null;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _createBackup() async {
    final password = await _passwordDialog('Contraseña para el backup');
    if (password == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final saved = await ref
          .read(backupServiceProvider)
          .createWithPicker(password);
      if (saved) _message('Backup cifrado guardado.');
    } catch (error) {
      _message('$error');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _busy = false;
          _progress = null;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _restoreBackup() async {
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final selected = await ref.read(backupServiceProvider).pickForRestore();
      if (selected == null || !mounted) return;
      final (bytes, summary) = selected;
      final accepted = await _confirmRestore(summary);
      if (!accepted || !mounted) return;
      final password = await _passwordDialog('Contraseña del backup');
      if (password == null) return;
      await ref
          .read(backupServiceProvider)
          .restore(
            bytes,
            password,
            onProgress: (value) {
              if (mounted) setState(() => _progress = value);
            },
          );
      if (!mounted) return;
      ref.invalidate(settingsProvider);
      _message('Backup restaurado.');
      context.go('/start');
    } catch (error) {
      _message('$error');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _busy = false;
          _progress = null;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  Future<String?> _passwordDialog(String title) async {
    final controller = TextEditingController();
    return showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(title),
        content: TextField(
          controller: controller,
          obscureText: true,
          autofocus: true,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Contraseña',
            helperText:
                'Mínimo 8 caracteres. No puede recuperarse si la olvidás.',
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext),
            child: const Text('Cancelar'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, controller.text),
            child: const Text('Continuar'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<bool> _confirmRestore(BackupSummary summary) async {
    return await showDialog<bool>(
          context: context,
          builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
            title: const Text('Revisar backup'),
            content: Text(
              'Creado: ${summary.createdAt.toLocal()}\n'
              'Esquema: ${summary.schemaVersion}\n'
              'Cuentas: ${summary.accountCount}\n'
              'Movimientos: ${summary.transactionCount}\n\n'
              'La base actual se guardará antes de ser reemplazada.',
            ),
            actions: [
              TextButton(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, false),
                child: const Text('Cancelar'),
              ),
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, true),
                child: const Text('Restaurar'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ) ??
        false;
  }

  void _message(String value) {
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(value)));
    }
  }
}

final class _SectionTitle extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SectionTitle(this.label);

  final String label;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 20, 16, 8),
    child: Text(
      label,
      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall
          ?.copyWith(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary),
    ),
  );
}


FILE lib/features/templates/manual_template_service.dart SHA256 57019494fc0bbfb1ef138099e8d3e3eef60cd55579e3e05989f0fd2e8c196c6b
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../payments/domain/pending_payment_service.dart';
import '../transactions/domain/ledger_models.dart';

final class ManualTemplate {
  const ManualTemplate(this.id, this.name, this.fields);
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final Map<String, dynamic> fields;
}

final class ManualTemplateService {
  ManualTemplateService(this.database);
  final AppDatabase database;
  static const prefix = 'manual_template:';
  ManualTemplate _decode(SettingRecord row) {
    final json = jsonDecode(row.value) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    return ManualTemplate(
      row.key.substring(prefix.length),
      json['name'] as String,
      Map<String, dynamic>.from(json['fields'] as Map),
    );
  }

  Stream<List<ManualTemplate>> watch() =>
      (database.select(
        database.appSettings,
      )..where((t) => t.key.like('$prefix%'))).watch().map(
        (rows) => rows.map(_decode).toList()
          ..sort(
            (a, b) => a.name.toLowerCase().compareTo(b.name.toLowerCase()),
          ),
      );
  Future<ManualTemplate?> read(String id) async {
    final row = await (database.select(
      database.appSettings,
    )..where((t) => t.key.equals('$prefix$id'))).getSingleOrNull();
    return row == null ? null : _decode(row);
  }

  Future<List<ManualTemplate>> list() async => (await (database.select(
    database.appSettings,
  )..where((t) => t.key.like('$prefix%'))).get()).map(_decode).toList();

  Future<String> save(
    String name,
    Map<String, dynamic> fields, {
    String? id,
    bool reconcilePreparedSchedule = true,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    if (name.trim().isEmpty) {
      throw const FormatException('Ingresá un nombre de plantilla.');
    }
    final schedule = fields['schedule'] as Map?;
    if (schedule?['enabled'] == true && schedule?['pendingReason'] == null) {
      final rawDate = schedule?['nextDate'];
      final parsed = rawDate is String ? DateTime.tryParse(rawDate) : null;
      if (parsed == null ||
          rawDate !=
              '${parsed.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-${parsed.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${parsed.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}') {
        throw const FormatException('Elegí una fecha válida.');
      }
      if (fields['type'] != 'expense' ||
          fields['installments'] == true ||
          fields['amountMinor'] is! int ||
          (fields['amountMinor'] as int) <= 0 ||
          fields['from'] == null ||
          !['monthly', 'yearly'].contains(schedule?['frequency']) ||
          schedule?['nextDate'] is! String) {
        throw const FormatException(
          'El registro automático requiere un gasto fijo, cuenta, frecuencia y fecha, sin plan de cuotas.',
        );
      }
    }
    final templateId = id ?? const Uuid().v4();
    final previous = id == null ? null : await read(id);
    if (previous != null && reconcilePreparedSchedule) {
      final oldSchedule = previous.fields['schedule'] as Map? ?? const {};
      final newSchedule = fields['schedule'] as Map? ?? const {};
      final calendarChanged = [
        'nextDate',
        'frequency',
        'anchorDay',
        'anchorMonth',
      ].any((key) => oldSchedule[key] != newSchedule[key]);
      if (calendarChanged) {
        final now = DateTime.now();
        final today = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
        for (final p in await PendingPaymentService(database).list()) {
          if (!(p.sourceReference ?? '').startsWith('scheduled:$templateId:') ||
              p.cancelled ||
              p.payments.isNotEmpty ||
              p.dueDate.isBefore(today)) {
            continue;
          }
          p.data.addAll({
            'cancelled': true,
            'supersededBySchedule': true,
            'revision': p.revision + 1,
          });
          await database
              .into(database.appSettings)
              .insertOnConflictUpdate(
                AppSettingsCompanion.insert(
                  key: '${PendingPaymentService.prefix}${p.id}',
                  value: jsonEncode(p.data),
                  updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
                ),
              );
        }
      }
    }
    final oldEconomic = Map<String, dynamic>.from(previous?.fields ?? {})
      ..remove('schedule');
    final newEconomic = Map<String, dynamic>.from(fields)..remove('schedule');
    if (previous != null &&
        (previous.name != name.trim() ||
            jsonEncode(oldEconomic) != jsonEncode(newEconomic))) {
      final service = PendingPaymentService(database);
      final today = DateTime.now();
      final day = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
      final future = (await service.list())
          .where(
            (p) =>
                (p.sourceReference ?? '').startsWith(
                  'scheduled:$templateId:',
                ) &&
                !p.cancelled &&
                p.paidMinor == 0 &&
                !p.dueDate.isBefore(day),
          )
          .toList();
      if (future.isNotEmpty) {
        final splits = fields['splitEnabled'] == true
            ? [
                for (final part in fields['splits'] as List? ?? [])
                  SplitDraft(
                    categoryId: part['category'] as String,
                    amountMinor: part['amountMinor'] as int,
                  ),
              ]
            : fields['category'] != null
            ? [
                SplitDraft(
                  categoryId: fields['category'] as String,
                  amountMinor: fields['amountMinor'] as int,
                ),
              ]
            : <SplitDraft>[];
        final reminders = fields['reminders'] is Map
            ? PaymentReminderSettings.fromJson(fields['reminders'] as Map)
            : const PaymentReminderSettings();
        final channel = PaymentChannel.values.byName(
          fields['channel'] as String? ?? 'other',
        );
        if (fields['amountMinor'] is int &&
            (fields['amountMinor'] as int) > 0) {
          await service.validateDraft(
            amountMinor: fields['amountMinor'] as int,
            accountId: fields['from'] as String,
            merchantId: fields['merchantId'] as String?,
            paymentChannel: channel,
            splits: splits,
            reminders: reminders,
            allowCredit: true,
          );
          for (final p in future) {
            p.data.addAll({
              'description': name.trim(),
              'note': fields['note'] as String? ?? '',
              'revision': p.revision + 1,
              'amountMinor': fields['amountMinor'],
              'accountId': fields['from'],
              'merchantId': fields['merchantId'],
              'paymentChannel': channel.name,
              'reminders': reminders.toJson(),
              'splits': [
                for (final split in splits)
                  {
                    'categoryId': split.categoryId,
                    'amountMinor': split.amountMinor,
                  },
              ],
            });
            await database
                .into(database.appSettings)
                .insertOnConflictUpdate(
                  AppSettingsCompanion.insert(
                    key: '${PendingPaymentService.prefix}${p.id}',
                    value: jsonEncode(p.data),
                    updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
                  ),
                );
          }
        }
      }
    }
    await database
        .into(database.appSettings)
        .insertOnConflictUpdate(
          AppSettingsCompanion.insert(
            key: '$prefix$templateId',
            value: jsonEncode({'name': name.trim(), 'fields': fields}),
            updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
          ),
        );
    return templateId;
  });

  Future<void> delete(String id) async {
    await (database.delete(
      database.appSettings,
    )..where((t) => t.key.equals('$prefix$id'))).go();
  }
}


FILE lib/features/templates/scheduled_template_service.dart SHA256 88e110af5d2128ef5e62a04fca5710b8387e82ed93fc07e8be4a6b95b68c0ad0
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import '../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../core/text/normalization.dart';
import '../merchants/merchant_service.dart';
import '../transactions/domain/ledger_models.dart';
import 'manual_template_service.dart';
import '../payments/domain/pending_payment_service.dart';

String scheduledDate(DateTime value) =>
    '${value.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-${value.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${value.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
DateTime parseScheduledDate(String value) {
  final date = DateTime.parse(value);
  if (scheduledDate(date) != value) {
    throw const FormatException('Fecha de vencimiento inválida.');
  }
  return DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day);
}

DateTime nextScheduledDate(DateTime due, Map<String, dynamic> schedule) {
  final yearly = schedule['frequency'] == 'yearly';
  final year = due.year + (yearly ? 1 : (due.month == 12 ? 1 : 0));
  final month = yearly
      ? (schedule['anchorMonth'] as int? ?? due.month)
      : due.month % 12 + 1;
  final last = DateTime(year, month + 1, 0).day;
  final day = schedule['anchorDay'] as int? ?? due.day;
  return DateTime(year, month, day > last ? last : day);
}

final class ScheduledProcessResult {
  final List<String> registered = [];
  final List<String> pending = [];
}

final class ScheduledTemplateService {
  ScheduledTemplateService(this.database);
  final AppDatabase database;
  ManualTemplateService get templates => ManualTemplateService(database);

  Future<void> pause(String id) => database.transaction(() async {
    final template = await templates.read(id);
    if (template == null) return;
    final fields = Map<String, dynamic>.from(template.fields);
    final schedule = Map<String, dynamic>.from(
      fields['schedule'] as Map? ?? {},
    );
    schedule['enabled'] = false;
    schedule['paused'] = true;
    fields['schedule'] = schedule;
    await templates.save(
      template.name,
      fields,
      id: id,
      reconcilePreparedSchedule: false,
    );
  });

  Future<void> skipPending(String id) => database.transaction(() async {
    final template = await templates.read(id);
    if (template == null) return;
    final fields = Map<String, dynamic>.from(template.fields);
    final schedule = Map<String, dynamic>.from(
      fields['schedule'] as Map? ?? {},
    );
    if (schedule['pendingReason'] == null) return;
    final due = parseScheduledDate(schedule['nextDate'] as String);
    schedule['nextDate'] = scheduledDate(nextScheduledDate(due, schedule));
    schedule.remove('pendingReason');
    fields['schedule'] = schedule;
    await templates.save(
      template.name,
      fields,
      id: id,
      reconcilePreparedSchedule: false,
    );
  });

  Future<ScheduledProcessResult> processDue({DateTime? now}) async {
    final today = parseScheduledDate(scheduledDate(now ?? DateTime.now()));
    final result = ScheduledProcessResult();
    for (final initial in await templates.list()) {
      for (var count = 0; count < 120; count++) {
        final outcome = await database.transaction(() async {
          final template = await templates.read(initial.id);
          if (template == null) return 'stop';
          final fields = Map<String, dynamic>.from(template.fields);
          final schedule = Map<String, dynamic>.from(
            fields['schedule'] as Map? ?? {},
          );
          if (schedule['enabled'] != true ||
              schedule['pendingReason'] != null) {
            return 'stop';
          }
          try {
            final due = parseScheduledDate(schedule['nextDate'] as String);
            if (due.isAfter(DateTime(today.year, today.month + 2, 0))) {
              return 'stop';
            }
            if (!['monthly', 'yearly'].contains(schedule['frequency'])) {
              throw StateError('Revisá la frecuencia.');
            }
            final fingerprint =
                'scheduled:${template.id}:${scheduledDate(due)}';
            final previous = await (database.select(
              database.ledgerTransactions,
            )..where((t) => t.externalFingerprint.equals(fingerprint))).get();
            if (previous.isEmpty) {
              if (fields['type'] != 'expense' ||
                  fields['installments'] == true) {
                throw StateError(
                  'Revisá el tipo de gasto y el plan de cuotas.',
                );
              }
              final amount = fields['amountMinor'] as int?;
              if (amount == null || amount <= 0) {
                throw StateError('Hace falta un importe fijo.');
              }
              final accountId = fields['from'] as String?;
              final account = accountId == null
                  ? null
                  : await (database.select(
                      database.accounts,
                    )..where((t) => t.id.equals(accountId))).getSingleOrNull();
              if (account == null || account.isArchived) {
                throw StateError('Elegí una cuenta activa.');
              }
              final occurredAt = DateTime(
                due.year,
                due.month,
                due.day,
                12,
              ).toUtc();
              if (account.currencyCode != 'PYG') {
                throw StateError('La programación admite PYG por ahora.');
              }
              String? merchantId = fields['merchantId'] as String?;
              if (merchantId != null) {
                final merchant = await (database.select(
                  database.merchants,
                )..where((t) => t.id.equals(merchantId!))).getSingleOrNull();
                if (merchant == null || merchant.isArchived) {
                  throw StateError('Revisá el comercio.');
                }
              } else if ((fields['merchant'] as String? ?? '')
                  .trim()
                  .isNotEmpty) {
                final matches = await MerchantService(database)
                    .matches(fields['merchant'] as String);
                if (matches.length > 1) {
                  throw StateError('El comercio es ambiguo.');
                }
                // Creating a new merchant happens only inside the successful ledger transaction.
                merchantId = matches.isEmpty ? null : matches.single.id;
              }
              final notifications =
                  await (database.select(database.paymentCandidates)..where(
                        (t) =>
                            t.status.equals('pending') &
                            t.amountMinor.equals(amount) &
                            t.occurredAt.isBetweenValues(
                              occurredAt.subtract(const Duration(days: 3)),
                              occurredAt.add(const Duration(days: 3)),
                            ),
                      ))
                      .get();
              for (final notification in notifications) {
                final notificationAccount =
                    notification.suggestedAccountId ??
                    notification.suggestedFromAccountId;
                final merchants = await MerchantService(database)
                    .matches(notification.rawMerchant ?? '');
                if (notificationAccount == accountId &&
                    ((merchantId != null &&
                            merchants.any((m) => m.id == merchantId)) ||
                        (normalizeForMatching(
                              fields['merchant'] as String? ?? '',
                            ).isNotEmpty &&
                            normalizeForMatching(
                                  notification.rawMerchant ?? '',
                                ) ==
                                normalizeForMatching(
                                  fields['merchant'] as String? ?? '',
                                )))) {
                  throw StateError(
                    'Hay una notificación pendiente de esta compra.',
                  );
                }
              }
              if (merchantId != null) {
                final duplicates =
                    await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
                          (t) =>
                              t.fromAccountId.equals(accountId!) &
                              t.merchantId.equals(merchantId!) &
                              t.amountMinor.equals(amount) &
                              t.status.equals('deleted').not() &
                              t.occurredAt.isBetweenValues(
                                occurredAt.subtract(const Duration(days: 3)),
                                occurredAt.add(const Duration(days: 3)),
                              ),
                        ))
                        .get();
                if (duplicates.isNotEmpty) {
                  throw StateError(
                    'Posible duplicado: revisá el movimiento existente.',
                  );
                }
              }
              final splits = <SplitDraft>[];
              if (fields['splitEnabled'] == true) {
                for (final split in fields['splits'] as List? ?? []) {
                  splits.add(
                    SplitDraft(
                      categoryId: split['category'] as String,
                      amountMinor: split['amountMinor'] as int,
                    ),
                  );
                }
              } else if (fields['category'] != null) {
                splits.add(
                  SplitDraft(
                    categoryId: fields['category'] as String,
                    amountMinor: amount,
                  ),
                );
              }
              for (final split in splits) {
                final category =
                    await (database.select(database.categories)
                          ..where((t) => t.id.equals(split.categoryId)))
                        .getSingleOrNull();
                if (category == null ||
                    category.isArchived ||
                    category.kind == 'income') {
                  throw StateError('Revisá la categoría.');
                }
              }
              await database.transaction(() async {
                merchantId ??=
                    (fields['merchant'] as String? ?? '').trim().isEmpty
                    ? null
                    : await MerchantService(database)
                          .findOrCreate(fields['merchant'] as String);
                final existing = (await PendingPaymentService(database).list())
                    .where(
                      (p) =>
                          p.sourceReference == fingerprint &&
                          p.data['supersededBySchedule'] != true,
                    );
                if (existing.isEmpty) {
                  result.registered.add(template.name);
                  await PendingPaymentService(database).createPlan(
                    amountMinor: amount,
                    installmentCount: 1,
                    firstDueDate: due,
                    accountId: account.id,
                    merchantId: merchantId,
                    description: template.name,
                    note: fields['note'] as String? ?? '',
                    paymentChannel: PaymentChannel.values.byName(
                      fields['channel'] as String? ?? 'other',
                    ),
                    splits: splits,
                    reminders: fields['reminders'] is Map
                        ? PaymentReminderSettings.fromJson(
                            fields['reminders'] as Map,
                          )
                        : const PaymentReminderSettings(),
                    sourceReference: fingerprint,
                  );
                }
              });
            }
            schedule['nextDate'] = scheduledDate(
              nextScheduledDate(due, schedule),
            );
            fields['schedule'] = schedule;
            await templates.save(
              template.name,
              fields,
              id: template.id,
              reconcilePreparedSchedule: false,
            );
            return 'next';
          } catch (error) {
            schedule['pendingReason'] = error is StateError
                ? error.message
                : error is FormatException
                ? error.message
                : '$error';
            fields['schedule'] = schedule;
            await templates.save(
              template.name,
              fields,
              id: template.id,
              reconcilePreparedSchedule: false,
            );
            result.pending.add(template.name);
            return 'stop';
          }
        });
        if (outcome == 'stop') break;
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
}


FILE lib/features/templates/templates_screen.dart SHA256 e6655a607fedb4303001310dad8873632e93411fca33f8911aadf1652969d932
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../shared/widgets/async_value_view.dart';
import 'manual_template_service.dart';
import 'template_summary.dart';

final class TemplatesScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const TemplatesScreen({this.scheduledOnly = false, super.key});
  final bool scheduledOnly;
  @override
  ConsumerState<TemplatesScreen> createState() => _TemplatesScreenState();
}

class _TemplatesScreenState extends ConsumerState<TemplatesScreen> {
  late bool scheduledOnly = widget.scheduledOnly;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
    appBar: AppBar(
      title: const Text('Plantillas'),
      bottom: PreferredSize(
        preferredSize: const Size.fromHeight(64),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
          child: SegmentedButton<bool>(
            segments: const [
              ButtonSegment(value: false, label: Text('Todas')),
              ButtonSegment(value: true, label: Text('Programadas')),
            ],
            selected: {scheduledOnly},
            onSelectionChanged: (v) => setState(() => scheduledOnly = v.first),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
    body: AsyncValueView(
      value: ref.watch(manualTemplatesProvider),
      data: (allTemplates) {
        final templates = scheduledOnly
            ? allTemplates.where((template) {
                final schedule = template.fields['schedule'] as Map?;
                return schedule?['enabled'] == true ||
                    schedule?['paused'] == true ||
                    schedule?['pendingReason'] != null;
              }).toList()
            : allTemplates;
        return templates.isEmpty
            ? Center(
                child: Text(
                  scheduledOnly
                      ? 'Sin plantillas programadas'
                      : 'Sin plantillas',
                ),
              )
            : ListView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                children: [
                  for (final template in templates)
                    Card(
                      child: ListTile(
                        title: Text(template.name),
                        subtitle: Text(
                          templateScheduleLabel(context, template),
                        ),
                        onTap: () => context.push(
                          Uri(
                            path: '/transactions/new',
                            queryParameters: {'savedTemplate': template.id},
                          ).toString(),
                        ),
                        trailing: PopupMenuButton<String>(
                          tooltip: 'Opciones de plantilla',
                          itemBuilder: (context) => [
                            const PopupMenuItem(
                              value: 'edit',
                              child: Text('Editar'),
                            ),
                            if ((template.fields['schedule']
                                    as Map?)?['enabled'] ==
                                true)
                              const PopupMenuItem(
                                value: 'pause',
                                child: Text('Pausar programación'),
                              ),
                            if ((template.fields['schedule']
                                    as Map?)?['pendingReason'] !=
                                null)
                              const PopupMenuItem(
                                value: 'skip',
                                child: Text('Omitir este vencimiento'),
                              ),
                            const PopupMenuItem(
                              value: 'delete',
                              child: Text('Eliminar'),
                            ),
                          ],
                          onSelected: (action) async {
                            if (action == 'edit') {
                              context.push(
                                Uri(
                                  path: '/transactions/new',
                                  queryParameters: {
                                    'savedTemplate': template.id,
                                    'editTemplate': 'true',
                                  },
                                ).toString(),
                              );
                            } else if (action == 'delete') {
                              await _deleteTemplate(context, ref, template);
                            } else {
                              await _scheduleAction(
                                context,
                                ref,
                                template.id,
                                skip: action == 'skip',
                              );
                            }
                          },
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              );
      },
    ),
    floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
      icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
      label: const Text('Crear plantilla'),
      onPressed: () => context.push('/transactions/new?editTemplate=true'),
    ),
  );
  Future<void> _deleteTemplate(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    ManualTemplate template,
  ) async {
    final service = ref.read(manualTemplateServiceProvider);
    try {
      await service.delete(template.id);
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: const Text('Plantilla eliminada.'),
            action: SnackBarAction(
              label: 'Deshacer',
              onPressed: () async {
                try {
                  await service.save(
                    template.name,
                    template.fields,
                    id: template.id,
                  );
                } catch (error) {
                  if (context.mounted) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
                        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
                  }
                }
              },
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _scheduleAction(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    String id, {
    required bool skip,
  }) async {
    try {
      final service = ref.read(scheduledTemplateServiceProvider);
      if (skip) {
        await service.skipPending(id);
      } else {
        await service.pause(id);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
      }
    }
  }
}


FILE lib/features/transactions/data/ledger_service.dart SHA256 891b7b0c2ffcaaa96a429f364478a0247ac60044f01cd79fbb35a721bdccc489
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../../core/errors/app_exception.dart';
import '../../../core/money/money.dart';
import '../domain/ledger_models.dart';
import '../domain/posting_calculator.dart';
import '../domain/payment_channel_policy.dart';
import '../../installments/domain/installment_service.dart';

final class LedgerService {
  LedgerService(this.database, {Uuid? uuid, PostingCalculator? calculator})
    : uuid = uuid ?? const Uuid(),
      calculator = calculator ?? const PostingCalculator();

  final AppDatabase database;
  final Uuid uuid;
  final PostingCalculator calculator;

  Future<String> create(TransactionDraft draft) =>
      database.transaction(() async {
        await _validateRefund(draft);
        final from = await _loadAccount(draft.fromAccountId);
        final to = await _loadAccount(draft.toAccountId);
        PaymentChannelPolicy.validate(draft, from: from, to: to);
        final postings = calculator.calculate(draft, from: from, to: to);
        final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
        final transactionId = uuid.v4();

        await database.transaction(() async {
          await database
              .into(database.ledgerTransactions)
              .insert(
                LedgerTransactionsCompanion.insert(
                  id: transactionId,
                  type: draft.type.name,
                  status: draft.status.name,
                  source: draft.source.name,
                  occurredAt: draft.occurredAt.toUtc(),
                  occurredTimeZone: Value(draft.timeZone),
                  createdAt: now,
                  updatedAt: now,
                  currencyCode: draft.amount.currencyCode,
                  amountMinor: draft.amount.minorUnits,
                  fromAccountId: Value(draft.fromAccountId),
                  toAccountId: Value(draft.toAccountId),
                  merchantId: Value(draft.merchantId),
                  description: Value(draft.description.trim()),
                  note: Value(draft.note.trim()),
                  paymentChannel: Value(draft.paymentChannel.name),
                  externalFingerprint: Value(draft.externalFingerprint),
                  sourceReference: Value(draft.sourceReference),
                  importBatchId: Value(draft.importBatchId),
                  parentTransactionId: Value(draft.parentTransactionId),
                ),
              );

          for (final posting in postings) {
            await database
                .into(database.accountPostings)
                .insert(
                  AccountPostingsCompanion.insert(
                    id: uuid.v4(),
                    transactionId: transactionId,
                    accountId: posting.accountId,
                    deltaMinor: posting.deltaMinor,
                    currencyCode: draft.amount.currencyCode,
                    occurredAt: draft.occurredAt.toUtc(),
                    createdAt: now,
                  ),
                );
          }

          for (final split in draft.splits) {
            await database
                .into(database.transactionSplits)
                .insert(
                  TransactionSplitsCompanion.insert(
                    id: uuid.v4(),
                    transactionId: transactionId,
                    categoryId: split.categoryId,
                    amountMinor: split.amountMinor,
                    note: Value(split.note),
                  ),
                );
          }

          await database
              .into(database.auditLogs)
              .insert(
                AuditLogsCompanion.insert(
                  id: uuid.v4(),
                  entityType: 'transaction',
                  entityId: transactionId,
                  action: 'created',
                  changesJson: Value(jsonEncode({'source': draft.source.name})),
                  createdAt: now,
                ),
              );
        });
        return transactionId;
      });

  Future<int> balance(String accountId) async {
    final account = await (database.select(
      database.accounts,
    )..where((t) => t.id.equals(accountId))).getSingle();
    final postings = await (database.select(
      database.accountPostings,
    )..where((t) => t.accountId.equals(accountId))).get();
    return account.initialBalanceMinor +
        postings.fold<int>(0, (sum, p) => sum + p.deltaMinor);
  }

  Future<String> reconcile({
    required String accountId,
    required int targetMinor,
    required int expectedBalance,
    required String note,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    final account = await _loadAccount(accountId);
    final current = await balance(accountId);
    if (current != expectedBalance) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'El saldo cambió mientras conciliabas. Volvé a intentarlo.',
      );
    }
    final delta = targetMinor - current;
    if (delta == 0) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'El saldo ya coincide; no hace falta un ajuste.',
      );
    }
    final increaseByInflow = account!.isLiability ? delta < 0 : delta > 0;
    return create(
      TransactionDraft(
        type: TransactionType.adjustment,
        source: TransactionSource.adjustment,
        amount: Money(delta.abs(), currencyCode: account.currencyCode),
        occurredAt: DateTime.now(),
        fromAccountId: increaseByInflow ? null : accountId,
        toAccountId: increaseByInflow ? accountId : null,
        description: 'Conciliación de saldo',
        note: note,
      ),
    );
  });

  Future<String> refund({
    required String purchaseId,
    required int amountMinor,
    required DateTime occurredAt,
    String note = '',
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    final purchase =
        await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
              (t) =>
                  t.id.equals(purchaseId) &
                  t.status.equals('confirmed') &
                  t.type.equals('expense'),
            ))
            .getSingleOrNull();
    if (purchase == null) {
      throw const ValidationException('Elegí una compra confirmada.');
    }
    final originals =
        await (database.select(database.transactionSplits)
              ..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(purchaseId))
              ..orderBy([(t) => OrderingTerm.asc(t.id)]))
            .get();
    final previous =
        await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
              (t) =>
                  t.parentTransactionId.equals(purchaseId) &
                  t.type.equals('refund') &
                  t.status.equals('confirmed'),
            ))
            .get();
    final priorSplits = await (database.select(
      database.transactionSplits,
    )..where((t) => t.transactionId.isIn(previous.map((r) => r.id)))).get();
    final weights = <String, int>{};
    for (final split in originals) {
      weights.update(
        split.categoryId,
        (v) => v + split.amountMinor,
        ifAbsent: () => split.amountMinor,
      );
    }
    for (final split in priorSplits) {
      if (weights.containsKey(split.categoryId)) {
        weights[split.categoryId] =
            (weights[split.categoryId]! - split.amountMinor).clamp(0, 1 << 62);
      }
    }
    final remainingWeight = weights.values.fold<int>(0, (sum, v) => sum + v);
    int cumulative = 0, assigned = 0;
    final splits = <SplitDraft>[];
    for (final entry in weights.entries) {
      if (remainingWeight == 0) break;
      cumulative += entry.value;
      final total = amountMinor * cumulative ~/ remainingWeight;
      final portion = total - assigned;
      assigned = total;
      if (portion > 0) {
        splits.add(SplitDraft(categoryId: entry.key, amountMinor: portion));
      }
    }
    return create(
      TransactionDraft(
        type: TransactionType.refund,
        amount: Money(amountMinor, currencyCode: purchase.currencyCode),
        occurredAt: occurredAt,
        toAccountId: purchase.fromAccountId,
        merchantId: purchase.merchantId,
        description: 'Devolución · ${purchase.description}',
        note: note,
        paymentChannel: PaymentChannel.other,
        parentTransactionId: purchaseId,
        splits: splits,
      ),
    );
  });

  Future<void> _validateRefund(
    TransactionDraft draft, {
    String? excludingId,
  }) async {
    if (draft.type != TransactionType.refund ||
        draft.parentTransactionId == null) {
      return;
    }
    final parent = await (database.select(
      database.ledgerTransactions,
    )..where((t) => t.id.equals(draft.parentTransactionId!))).getSingleOrNull();
    if (parent == null ||
        parent.type != 'expense' ||
        parent.status != 'confirmed' ||
        parent.currencyCode != draft.amount.currencyCode ||
        parent.fromAccountId != draft.toAccountId) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'La devolución debe corresponder a una compra activa y su cuenta de origen.',
      );
    }
    if (DateTime(
      draft.occurredAt.toLocal().year,
      draft.occurredAt.toLocal().month,
      draft.occurredAt.toLocal().day,
    ).isBefore(
      DateTime(
        parent.occurredAt.toLocal().year,
        parent.occurredAt.toLocal().month,
        parent.occurredAt.toLocal().day,
      ),
    )) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'La devolución no puede ser anterior a la compra.',
      );
    }
    final children =
        await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
              (t) =>
                  t.parentTransactionId.equals(parent.id) &
                  t.type.equals('refund') &
                  t.status.equals('confirmed'),
            ))
            .get();
    final already = children
        .where((r) => r.id != excludingId)
        .fold<int>(0, (sum, r) => sum + r.amountMinor);
    if (already + draft.amount.minorUnits > parent.amountMinor) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'Las devoluciones no pueden superar el importe de la compra.',
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> update(
    String id,
    TransactionDraft draft, {
    DateTime? expectedUpdatedAt,
    int? installmentCount,
    DateTime? firstInstallmentDate,
    bool preserveInstallments = true,
  }) => database.transaction(() async {
    final old = await (database.select(
      database.ledgerTransactions,
    )..where((t) => t.id.equals(id))).getSingleOrNull();
    if (old == null || old.status != 'confirmed') {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'El movimiento ya no está disponible para editar.',
      );
    }
    if (expectedUpdatedAt != null && old.updatedAt != expectedUpdatedAt) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'El movimiento cambió. Volvé a abrirlo antes de guardar.',
      );
    }
    if (!['expense', 'income', 'transfer'].contains(old.type)) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'Para corregir una devolución o ajuste, eliminá el registro y crealo de nuevo.',
      );
    }
    if (old.type != draft.type.name ||
        old.currencyCode != draft.amount.currencyCode) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'No se puede cambiar la naturaleza ni la moneda de un movimiento existente.',
      );
    }
    final children =
        await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
              (t) =>
                  t.parentTransactionId.equals(id) &
                  t.status.equals('confirmed'),
            ))
            .get();
    final refunded = children
        .where((r) => r.type == 'refund')
        .fold<int>(0, (sum, r) => sum + r.amountMinor);
    if (refunded > draft.amount.minorUnits ||
        (refunded > 0 && old.fromAccountId != draft.fromAccountId)) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'La compra tiene devoluciones; conservá su cuenta y un importe que las cubra.',
      );
    }
    if (refunded > 0) {
      final refundSplits =
          await (database.select(database.transactionSplits)..where(
                (t) => t.transactionId.isIn(
                  children.where((r) => r.type == 'refund').map((r) => r.id),
                ),
              ))
              .get();
      final credited = <String, int>{};
      for (final s in refundSplits) {
        credited.update(
          s.categoryId,
          (v) => v + s.amountMinor,
          ifAbsent: () => s.amountMinor,
        );
      }
      final proposed = <String, int>{};
      for (final s in draft.splits) {
        proposed.update(
          s.categoryId,
          (v) => v + s.amountMinor,
          ifAbsent: () => s.amountMinor,
        );
      }
      if (credited.entries.any((e) => (proposed[e.key] ?? 0) < e.value)) {
        throw const ValidationException(
          'Las categorías deben cubrir las devoluciones ya registradas.',
        );
      }
    }
    final from = await _loadAccount(
      draft.fromAccountId,
      allowArchived: old.fromAccountId == draft.fromAccountId,
    );
    final to = await _loadAccount(
      draft.toAccountId,
      allowArchived: old.toAccountId == draft.toAccountId,
    );
    PaymentChannelPolicy.validate(draft, from: from, to: to);
    final postings = calculator.calculate(draft, from: from, to: to);
    final plan = await (database.select(
      database.installmentPlans,
    )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).getSingleOrNull();
    final oldDay = old.occurredAt.toLocal(),
        newDay = draft.occurredAt.toLocal();
    final samePurchaseDay =
        oldDay.year == newDay.year &&
        oldDay.month == newDay.month &&
        oldDay.day == newDay.day;
    final count =
        installmentCount ??
        (preserveInstallments ? plan?.installmentCount : null);
    if (count != null &&
        (draft.type != TransactionType.expense ||
            from?.type != AccountType.creditCard)) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'Las cuotas requieren una compra con tarjeta de crédito.',
      );
    }
    await _refreshPendingLink(id, newAmount: draft.amount.minorUnits);
    final clockNow = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    final now = clockNow.difference(old.updatedAt).inSeconds < 1
        ? old.updatedAt.add(const Duration(seconds: 1))
        : clockNow;
    await (database.update(
      database.ledgerTransactions,
    )..where((t) => t.id.equals(id))).write(
      LedgerTransactionsCompanion(
        amountMinor: Value(draft.amount.minorUnits),
        occurredAt: Value(draft.occurredAt.toUtc()),
        fromAccountId: Value(draft.fromAccountId),
        toAccountId: Value(draft.toAccountId),
        merchantId: Value(draft.merchantId),
        description: Value(draft.description.trim()),
        note: Value(draft.note.trim()),
        paymentChannel: Value(draft.paymentChannel.name),
        updatedAt: Value(now),
      ),
    );
    await (database.delete(
      database.accountPostings,
    )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).go();
    // Preserve split identity/order: reports allocate quota residues by split ID.
    // Editing a note must not move one guaraní between historical months.
    final previousSplits = await (database.select(
      database.transactionSplits,
    )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).get();
    await (database.delete(
      database.transactionSplits,
    )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).go();
    for (final posting in postings) {
      await database
          .into(database.accountPostings)
          .insert(
            AccountPostingsCompanion.insert(
              id: uuid.v4(),
              transactionId: id,
              accountId: posting.accountId,
              deltaMinor: posting.deltaMinor,
              currencyCode: draft.amount.currencyCode,
              occurredAt: draft.occurredAt.toUtc(),
              createdAt: now,
            ),
          );
    }
    for (final split in draft.splits) {
      final previousIndex = previousSplits.indexWhere(
        (part) => part.categoryId == split.categoryId,
      );
      final splitId = previousIndex < 0
          ? uuid.v4()
          : previousSplits.removeAt(previousIndex).id;
      await database
          .into(database.transactionSplits)
          .insert(
            TransactionSplitsCompanion.insert(
              id: splitId,
              transactionId: id,
              categoryId: split.categoryId,
              amountMinor: split.amountMinor,
              note: Value(split.note),
            ),
          );
    }
    await (database.delete(
      database.installmentPlans,
    )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).go();
    if (count != null) {
      await InstallmentService(database).attachPlan(
        transactionId: id,
        count: count,
        firstPeriod:
            firstInstallmentDate ??
            (plan != null && samePurchaseDay
                ? plan.firstPeriod.toUtc()
                : draft.occurredAt.toLocal()),
        preserveHistoricalDate:
            plan != null &&
            samePurchaseDay &&
            (firstInstallmentDate == null ||
                firstInstallmentDate == plan.firstPeriod.toUtc()),
      );
    }
    await database
        .into(database.auditLogs)
        .insert(
          AuditLogsCompanion.insert(
            id: uuid.v4(),
            entityType: 'transaction',
            entityId: id,
            action: 'edited',
            changesJson: Value(
              jsonEncode({
                'before': {
                  'amountMinor': old.amountMinor,
                  'from': old.fromAccountId,
                  'to': old.toAccountId,
                },
                'after': {
                  'amountMinor': draft.amount.minorUnits,
                  'from': draft.fromAccountId,
                  'to': draft.toAccountId,
                },
              }),
            ),
            createdAt: now,
          ),
        );
  });

  Future<void> softDelete(String transactionId) async {
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    await database.transaction(() async {
      final children =
          await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
                (t) =>
                    t.parentTransactionId.equals(transactionId) &
                    t.status.equals('confirmed'),
              ))
              .get();
      if (children.isNotEmpty) {
        throw const ValidationException(
          'Eliminá primero las devoluciones vinculadas a esta compra.',
        );
      }
      final changed =
          await (database.update(
            database.ledgerTransactions,
          )..where((table) => table.id.equals(transactionId))).write(
            LedgerTransactionsCompanion(
              status: const Value('deleted'),
              deletedAt: Value(now),
              updatedAt: Value(now),
            ),
          );
      await _refreshPendingLink(transactionId);
      if (changed != 1) {
        throw const ValidationException('El movimiento ya no existe.');
      }
      await (database.delete(
        database.accountPostings,
      )..where((table) => table.transactionId.equals(transactionId))).go();
      await database
          .into(database.auditLogs)
          .insert(
            AuditLogsCompanion.insert(
              id: uuid.v4(),
              entityType: 'transaction',
              entityId: transactionId,
              action: 'soft_deleted',
              createdAt: now,
            ),
          );
    });
  }

  Future<void> restore(String transactionId) => database.transaction(() async {
    final row = await (database.select(
      database.ledgerTransactions,
    )..where((table) => table.id.equals(transactionId))).getSingleOrNull();
    if (row == null || row.status != TransactionStatus.deleted.name) {
      throw const ValidationException('No se puede restaurar este movimiento.');
    }
    final draft = TransactionDraft(
      type: TransactionType.values.byName(row.type),
      amount: Money(row.amountMinor, currencyCode: row.currencyCode),
      occurredAt: row.occurredAt,
      status: TransactionStatus.confirmed,
      source: TransactionSource.values.byName(row.source),
      fromAccountId: row.fromAccountId,
      toAccountId: row.toAccountId,
      merchantId: row.merchantId,
      description: row.description,
      note: row.note,
      paymentChannel: PaymentChannel.values.byName(row.paymentChannel),
      externalFingerprint: row.externalFingerprint,
      sourceReference: row.sourceReference,
      importBatchId: row.importBatchId,
      parentTransactionId: row.parentTransactionId,
      timeZone: row.occurredTimeZone,
    );
    await _validateRefund(draft, excludingId: row.id);
    await _refreshPendingLink(transactionId, newAmount: row.amountMinor);
    final from = await _loadAccount(row.fromAccountId);
    final to = await _loadAccount(row.toAccountId);
    final postings = calculator.calculate(draft, from: from, to: to);
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    if (row.externalFingerprint != null) {
      final active =
          await (database.select(database.ledgerTransactions)..where(
                (table) =>
                    table.externalFingerprint.equals(row.externalFingerprint!) &
                    table.status.equals('deleted').not(),
              ))
              .get();
      if (active.isNotEmpty) {
        throw const ValidationException(
          'Esta compra ya tiene otro movimiento activo.',
        );
      }
    }
    await (database.update(
      database.ledgerTransactions,
    )..where((table) => table.id.equals(transactionId))).write(
      LedgerTransactionsCompanion(
        status: const Value('confirmed'),
        deletedAt: const Value(null),
        updatedAt: Value(now),
      ),
    );
    for (final posting in postings) {
      await database
          .into(database.accountPostings)
          .insert(
            AccountPostingsCompanion.insert(
              id: uuid.v4(),
              transactionId: transactionId,
              accountId: posting.accountId,
              deltaMinor: posting.deltaMinor,
              currencyCode: row.currencyCode,
              occurredAt: row.occurredAt,
              createdAt: now,
            ),
          );
    }
  });

  // Pending obligations retain their historical links when a payment changes.
  Future<void> _refreshPendingLink(
    String transactionId, {
    int? newAmount,
  }) async {
    final rows = await (database.select(
      database.appSettings,
    )..where((s) => s.key.like('pending_payment:v1:%'))).get();
    for (final row in rows) {
      final pending = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(row.value) as Map);
      final links = pending['payments'] as List;
      if (!links.any((p) => p['transactionId'] == transactionId)) continue;
      if (newAmount != null) {
        var otherPaid = 0;
        for (final link in links) {
          if (link['transactionId'] == transactionId) continue;
          final tx =
              await (database.select(
                    database.ledgerTransactions,
                  )..where((t) => t.id.equals(link['transactionId'] as String)))
                  .getSingleOrNull();
          if (tx?.status == 'confirmed') otherPaid += tx!.amountMinor;
        }
        if (newAmount + otherPaid + (pending['carriedMinor'] as int? ?? 0) >
            (pending['amountMinor'] as int)) {
          throw const ValidationException(
            'El pago supera el importe pendiente.',
          );
        }
      }
      pending['revision'] = (pending['revision'] as int) + 1;
      await database
          .into(database.appSettings)
          .insertOnConflictUpdate(
            AppSettingsCompanion.insert(
              key: row.key,
              value: jsonEncode(pending),
              updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
            ),
          );
    }
  }

  Future<AccountSnapshot?> _loadAccount(
    String? accountId, {
    bool allowArchived = false,
  }) async {
    if (accountId == null) return null;
    final row = await (database.select(
      database.accounts,
    )..where((table) => table.id.equals(accountId))).getSingleOrNull();
    if (row == null || (row.isArchived && !allowArchived)) {
      throw const ValidationException(
        'La cuenta seleccionada no está disponible.',
      );
    }
    return AccountSnapshot(
      id: row.id,
      type: AccountType.values.byName(row.type),
      currencyCode: row.currencyCode,
    );
  }
}

                  CheckboxListTile(

                            : _save(accounts),
