                  label: Text(
                    _saving
                        ? 'Guardando…'
                        : widget.editTemplate
                        ? 'Guardar plantilla'
                        : widget.editId != null
                        ? 'Guardar cambios'
                        : 'Guardar movimiento',
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _loadSavedTemplate(String id) async {
    _loadingTemplate = true;
    _templateError = null;
    try {
      final template = await ref.read(manualTemplateServiceProvider).read(id);
      if (template == null) throw StateError('La plantilla ya no existe.');
      if (!mounted) return;
      final f = template.fields;
      final merchantId = f['merchantId'] as String?;
      final linkedMerchant = merchantId == null
          ? null
          : await (ref
                    .read(databaseProvider)
                    .select(ref.read(databaseProvider).merchants)
                  ..where((t) => t.id.equals(merchantId)))
                .getSingleOrNull();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _templateName = template.name;
        if (widget.editTemplate && f['schedule'] is Map) {
          _templateSchedule = Map<String, dynamic>.from(f['schedule'] as Map);
        }
        _type = TransactionType.values.byName(f['type'] as String);
        _amount.text = f['amountMinor'] == null
            ? ''
            : Money(f['amountMinor'] as int).format(withCode: false).trim();
        _from = f['from'] as String?;
        _to = f['to'] as String?;
        _category = f['category'] as String?;
        _selectedMerchant = linkedMerchant?.isArchived == false
            ? linkedMerchant
            : null;
        _merchant.text =
            _selectedMerchant?.name ?? f['merchant'] as String? ?? '';
        _note.text = f['note'] as String? ?? '';
        _channel = PaymentChannel.values.byName(
          f['channel'] as String? ?? 'other',
        );
        _installments = f['installments'] as bool? ?? false;
        _installmentCount = f['installmentCount'] as int? ?? 2;
        _reminders = f['reminders'] is Map
            ? PaymentReminderSettings.fromJson(f['reminders'] as Map)
            : const PaymentReminderSettings();
        _splitEnabled = f['splitEnabled'] as bool? ?? false;
        for (final item in f['splits'] as List? ?? []) {
          _splits.add(
            _SplitInput(categoryId: item['category'] as String?)
              ..amount.text = item['amountMinor'] == null
                  ? ''
                  : Money(item['amountMinor'] as int)
                        .format(withCode: false)
                        .trim(),
          );
        }
        _advanced = _installments || _note.text.isNotEmpty;
        if (widget.savedTemplateId != null) _baseline = _draftSnapshot;
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mounted) _templateError = '$error';
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loadingTemplate = false);
    }
  }

  Future<({String name, Map<String, dynamic> fields})?> _collectTemplate({
    bool afterRegistration = false,
  }) async {
    String name = _templateName;
    bool includeAmount = _amount.text.trim().isNotEmpty;
    bool automatic = _templateSchedule?['enabled'] == true;
    var reminderSettings = _reminders;
    String frequency = _templateSchedule?['frequency'] as String? ?? 'monthly';
    DateTime nextDate = _templateSchedule?['nextDate'] is String
        ? parseScheduledDate(_templateSchedule!['nextDate'] as String)
        : afterRegistration
        ? nextScheduledDate(_occurredAt, {
            'frequency': 'monthly',
            'anchorDay': _occurredAt.day,
          })
        : DateTime.now();
    bool choseDate = false;
    final originalDate = scheduledDate(nextDate);
    final originalFrequency = frequency;
    final accepted = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, update) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Guardar plantilla'),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                TextFormField(
                  initialValue: name,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Nombre de la plantilla',
                  ),
                  onChanged: (value) => update(() => name = value),
                ),
                CheckboxListTile(
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  title: const Text('Recordar importe'),
                  value: includeAmount,
                  onChanged: automatic
                      ? null
                      : (value) => update(() => includeAmount = value ?? false),
                ),
                CheckboxListTile(
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  title: const Text('Preparar pagos pendientes'),
                  value: automatic,
                  onChanged: (value) => update(() {
                    automatic = value ?? false;
                    if (automatic) includeAmount = true;
                  }),
                ),
                if (automatic) ...[
                  DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                    initialValue: frequency,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Frecuencia'),
                    items: const [
                      DropdownMenuItem(
                        value: 'monthly',
                        child: Text('Mensual'),
                      ),
                      DropdownMenuItem(value: 'yearly', child: Text('Anual')),
                    ],
                    onChanged: (value) => update(() {
                      frequency = value!;
                      if (afterRegistration &&
                          _templateSchedule == null &&
                          !choseDate) {
                        nextDate = nextScheduledDate(_occurredAt, {
                          'frequency': frequency,
                          'anchorDay': _occurredAt.day,
                          'anchorMonth': _occurredAt.month,
                        });
                      }
                    }),
                  ),
                  ListTile(
                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    title: const Text('Próxima fecha'),
                    subtitle: Text(scheduledDate(nextDate)),
                    trailing: const Icon(Icons.calendar_today),
                    onTap: () async {
                      final chosen = await showDatePicker(
                        context: context,
                        initialDate: nextDate,
                        firstDate: DateTime(2000),
                        lastDate: DateTime(2100),
                      );
                      if (chosen != null && context.mounted) {
                        update(() {
                          nextDate = chosen;
                          choseDate = true;
                        });
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  PaymentReminderFields(
                    value: reminderSettings,
                    onChanged: (v) => update(() => reminderSettings = v),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
              child: const Text('Cancelar'),
            ),
            FilledButton(
              onPressed: name.trim().isEmpty
                  ? null
                  : () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
              child: const Text('Guardar'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (accepted != true || !mounted) return null;
    try {
      final amount = includeAmount
          ? Money.parse(_amount.text).minorUnits
          : null;
      if (amount != null && amount <= 0) {
        throw const FormatException('El importe debe ser mayor que cero.');
      }
      return (
        name: name,
        fields: <String, dynamic>{
          'schedule': {
            'enabled': automatic,
            'paused':
                !automatic &&
                (_templateSchedule?['enabled'] == true ||
                    _templateSchedule?['paused'] == true),
            'frequency': frequency,
            'nextDate': scheduledDate(nextDate),
            'anchorDay':
                afterRegistration && _templateSchedule == null && !choseDate
                ? _occurredAt.day
                : originalDate == scheduledDate(nextDate) &&
                      originalFrequency == frequency
                ? (_templateSchedule?['anchorDay'] ?? nextDate.day)
                : nextDate.day,
            'anchorMonth':
                afterRegistration && _templateSchedule == null && !choseDate
                ? _occurredAt.month
                : originalDate == scheduledDate(nextDate) &&
                      originalFrequency == frequency
                ? (_templateSchedule?['anchorMonth'] ?? nextDate.month)
                : nextDate.month,
          },
          'type': _type.name,
          'amountMinor': amount,
          'from': _needsFrom ? _from : null,
          'to': _needsTo ? _to : null,
          'category': _category,
          'merchant': _merchant.text.trim(),
          'merchantId': _selectedMerchant?.name == _merchant.text
              ? _selectedMerchant?.id
              : null,
          'note': _note.text.trim(),
          'channel': _channel.name,
          'installments': _installments,
          'installmentCount': _installmentCount,
          'reminders': reminderSettings.toJson(),
          'splitEnabled': includeAmount && _splitEnabled,
          'splits': [
            if (includeAmount && _splitEnabled)
              for (final split in _splits)
                {
                  'category': split.categoryId,
                  'amountMinor': Money.parse(split.amount.text).minorUnits,
                },
          ],
        },
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (mounted) _message('$error');
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveTemplate() async {
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    try {
      final template = await _collectTemplate();
      if (template == null || !mounted) return;
      await ref.read(databaseProvider).transaction(() async {
        if (_bookGeneration != ref.read(databaseProvider).restoreGeneration) {
          throw StateError(
            'La base cambió. Cerrá el formulario y volvé a abrirlo.',
          );
        }
        await ref
            .read(manualTemplateServiceProvider)
            .save(
              template.name,
              template.fields,
              id: widget.editTemplate ? widget.savedTemplateId : null,
            );
      });
      if ((template.fields['schedule'] as Map?)?['enabled'] == true &&
          (template.fields['reminders'] as Map?)?['enabled'] == true) {
        await _requestReminderPermission();
      }
      if (mounted) {
        _message('Plantilla guardada.');
        setState(() {
          _baseline = _draftSnapshot;
          _saving = false;
        });
        await WidgetsBinding.instance.endOfFrame;
        if (mounted && widget.editTemplate) context.pop();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (mounted) _message('$error');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _saving = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _useTemplate() async {
    final templates = await ref.read(manualTemplateServiceProvider).list();
    if (!mounted) return;
    templates.sort((a, b) {
      final aMatches = a.fields['type'] == _type.name;
      final bMatches = b.fields['type'] == _type.name;
      if (aMatches != bMatches) return aMatches ? -1 : 1;
      return a.name.toLowerCase().compareTo(b.name.toLowerCase());
    });
    final selected = await showModalBottomSheet<String>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (context) => SafeArea(
        child: SizedBox(
          height: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * .6,
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                child: Text(
                  'Usar plantilla',
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: templates.isEmpty
                    ? const Center(
                        child: Text('Todavía no guardaste plantillas.'),
                      )
                    : ListView(
                        children: [
                          for (final template in templates)
                            ListTile(
                              title: Text(template.name),
                              subtitle: Text(
                                '${templateMovementLabel(template)} · ${templateScheduleLabel(context, template)}',
                              ),
                              trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context, template.id),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
              ),
              TextButton.icon(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, '__manage__'),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.tune),
                label: const Text(
                  'Administrar plantillas y registros programados',
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (selected == null || !mounted) return;
    if (selected == '__manage__') {
      context.push('/templates');
      return;
    }
    for (final split in _splits) {
      split.dispose();
    }
    setState(() {
      _splits.clear();
      _selectedMerchant = null;
      _templateSchedule = null;
      _alsoSaveTemplate = false;
      _occurredAt = DateTime.now();
      _firstInstallmentDate = null;
    });
    await _loadSavedTemplate(selected);
  }

  bool get _needsFrom =>
      _type == TransactionType.expense || _type == TransactionType.transfer;
  bool get _needsTo =>
      _type == TransactionType.income || _type == TransactionType.transfer;

  bool _categoryMatchesType(CategoryRecord category) =>
      category.kind == 'both' ||
      (_type == TransactionType.income
          ? category.kind == 'income'
          : category.kind == 'expense');

  bool _isCreditExpense(List<AccountRecord> accounts) =>
      _type == TransactionType.expense &&
      accounts.where((account) => account.id == _from).firstOrNull?.type ==
          AccountType.creditCard.name;

  Future<void> _save(List<AccountRecord> accounts) async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    if (_needsFrom && _from == null || _needsTo && _to == null) {
      _message('Seleccioná las cuentas necesarias.');
      return;
    }
    try {
      setState(() => _saving = true);
      final pendingPlan =
          _installments &&
          _type == TransactionType.expense &&
          !_isCreditExpense(accounts) &&
          widget.editId == null;
      final template = _alsoSaveTemplate && !pendingPlan
          ? await _collectTemplate(afterRegistration: true)
          : null;
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (_alsoSaveTemplate && !pendingPlan && template == null) {
        setState(() => _saving = false);
        return;
      }
      final money = Money.parse(_amount.text);
      if (_installments &&
          _type == TransactionType.expense &&
          (money.minorUnits < _installmentCount ||
              _installmentCount < 2 ||
              _installmentCount > 120)) {
        throw const FormatException('Importe o cuotas inválidos.');
      }
      if (pendingPlan && _merchant.text.trim().isEmpty) {
        throw const FormatException('Ingresá una descripción.');
      }
      await ref.read(databaseProvider).transaction(() async {
        if (_bookGeneration != ref.read(databaseProvider).restoreGeneration) {
          throw StateError(
            'Los datos fueron restaurados. Cancelá y volvé a abrir el formulario.',
          );
        }
        String? merchantId;
        if (_merchant.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
          if (_type == TransactionType.expense &&
              _selectedMerchant?.name == _merchant.text) {
            final selected =
                await (ref
                        .read(databaseProvider)
                        .select(ref.read(databaseProvider).merchants)
                      ..where((t) => t.id.equals(_selectedMerchant!.id)))
                    .getSingle();
            if (selected.isArchived && selected.id != _originalMerchantId) {
              throw StateError('El comercio está archivado.');
            }
            merchantId = selected.id;
          } else {
            merchantId = await ref
                .read(merchantServiceProvider)
                .findOrCreate(_merchant.text);
          }
        }
        final splits = <SplitDraft>[];
        if (_type != TransactionType.transfer) {
          if (_splitEnabled) {
            for (final input in _splits) {
              if (input.categoryId == null) {
                throw const FormatException('Elegí todas las categorías.');
              }
              splits.add(
                SplitDraft(
                  categoryId: input.categoryId!,
                  amountMinor: Money.parse(input.amount.text).minorUnits,
                ),
              );
            }
          } else if (_category != null) {
            splits.add(
              SplitDraft(categoryId: _category!, amountMinor: money.minorUnits),
            );
          }
        }
        if (_type == TransactionType.expense &&
            merchantId != null &&
            !_splitEnabled &&
            (_category == null ||
                ref
                        .read(categoriesProvider)
                        .value
                        ?.any((c) => c.id == _category) ==
                    true)) {
          await ref
              .read(merchantServiceProvider)
              .rememberCategory(merchantId, _category);
        }
        if (pendingPlan) {
          await ref
              .read(pendingPaymentServiceProvider)
              .createPlan(
                amountMinor: money.minorUnits,
                installmentCount: _installmentCount,
                firstDueDate: _firstInstallmentDate ?? _occurredAt,
                accountId: _from!,
                merchantId: merchantId,
                description: _merchant.text.trim(),
                note: _note.text,
                paymentChannel: _channel,
                splits: splits,
                reminders: _reminders,
                occurredAt: _occurredAt,
                expectedBookGeneration: _bookGeneration,
              );
          return;
        }
        String? templateId;
        Map<String, dynamic>? schedule;
        if (template != null) {
          final fields = {...template.fields, 'merchantId': merchantId};
          templateId = await ref
              .read(manualTemplateServiceProvider)
              .save(template.name, fields);
          schedule = Map<String, dynamic>.from(fields['schedule'] as Map);
        }
        final consumesDue =
            schedule?['enabled'] == true &&
            schedule?['nextDate'] == scheduledDate(_occurredAt);
        final draft = TransactionDraft(
          type: _type,
          amount: money,
          occurredAt: _occurredAt,
          fromAccountId: _needsFrom ? _from : null,
          toAccountId: _needsTo ? _to : null,
          merchantId: merchantId,
          description: _merchant.text.trim(),
          note: _note.text,
          paymentChannel: _channel,
          splits: splits,
          externalFingerprint: consumesDue
              ? 'scheduled:$templateId:${scheduledDate(_occurredAt)}'
              : null,
          sourceReference: templateId,
        );
        final ledger = ref.read(ledgerServiceProvider);
        final String id;
        if (widget.editId != null) {
          id = widget.editId!;
          await ledger.update(
            id,
            draft,
            expectedUpdatedAt: _editingUpdatedAt,
            installmentCount: _installments ? _installmentCount : null,
            preserveInstallments: false,
            firstInstallmentDate: _firstInstallmentDate ?? _occurredAt,
          );
        } else {
          id = await ledger.create(draft);
        }
        if (_installments &&
            _type == TransactionType.expense &&
            widget.editId == null) {
          await ref
              .read(installmentServiceProvider)
              .attachPlan(
                transactionId: id,
                count: _installmentCount,
                firstPeriod: _firstInstallmentDate ?? _occurredAt,
              );
        }
        if (consumesDue &&
            template != null &&
            templateId != null &&
            schedule != null) {
          schedule['nextDate'] = scheduledDate(
            nextScheduledDate(
              parseScheduledDate(schedule['nextDate'] as String),
              schedule,
            ),
          );
          await ref.read(manualTemplateServiceProvider).save(template.name, {
            ...template.fields,
            'merchantId': merchantId,
            'schedule': schedule,
          }, id: templateId);
        }
      });
      if ((pendingPlan && _reminders.enabled) ||
          ((template?.fields['schedule'] as Map?)?['enabled'] == true &&
              (template?.fields['reminders'] as Map?)?['enabled'] == true)) {
        await _requestReminderPermission();
      }
      if (!mounted) return;
      final settings = ref.read(settingsProvider);
      if (_from != null) {
        await settings.write('last_from_${_type.name}', _from!);
      }
      if (_to != null) await settings.write('last_to_${_type.name}', _to!);
      HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (_saveAnother) {
        _amount.clear();
        _merchant.clear();
        _selectedMerchant = null;
        _alsoSaveTemplate = false;
        _templateName = '';
        _note.clear();
        setState(() {
          _saving = false;
          _baseline = _draftSnapshot;
        });
        _message('Movimiento guardado.');
      } else {
        setState(() {
          _saving = false;
          _baseline = _draftSnapshot;
        });
        await WidgetsBinding.instance.endOfFrame;
        if (mounted) context.pop();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      _message('$error');
      if (mounted) setState(() => _saving = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _requestReminderPermission() async {
    try {
      final allowed = await ref
          .read(paymentReminderBridgeProvider)
          .requestPermission();
      if (!allowed && mounted) _message('Guardado. Recordatorios sin permiso.');
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) _message('Guardado. Recordatorios no disponibles.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate() async {
    final value = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: _occurredAt,
      firstDate: DateTime(2000),
      lastDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 365)),
    );
    if (value != null && mounted) {
      setState(() {
        final old = _occurredAt;
        if (_firstInstallmentDate != null &&
            _firstInstallmentDate!.year == old.year &&
            _firstInstallmentDate!.month == old.month &&
            _firstInstallmentDate!.day == old.day) {
          _firstInstallmentDate = DateTime.utc(
            value.year,
            value.month,
            value.day,
            12,
          );
        }
        _occurredAt = DateTime(
          value.year,
          value.month,
          value.day,
          _occurredAt.hour,
          _occurredAt.minute,
        );
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _createCategory(List<CategoryRecord> categories) async {
    final controller = TextEditingController();
    final value = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Nueva categoría'),
        content: TextField(
          controller: controller,
          autofocus: true,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Nombre'),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Cancelar'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, controller.text),
            child: const Text('Crear'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (value == null || !mounted) return;
    try {
      final id = await ref
          .read(categoryServiceProvider)
          .create(
            value,
            kind: _type == TransactionType.income ? 'income' : 'expense',
          );
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _awaitingCategory = id;
          _category = id;
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      _message('$error');
    }
  }

  void _toggleSplits(List<CategoryRecord> categories) {
    setState(() {
      _splitEnabled = !_splitEnabled;
      if (_splitEnabled && _splits.isEmpty) {
        _splits.addAll([
          _SplitInput(
            categoryId:
                _category ??
                categories.where(_categoryMatchesType).firstOrNull?.id,
          ),
          _SplitInput(),
        ]);
      }
    });
  }

  void _applyTemplate(String template, List<AccountRecord> accounts) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      if (template == 'cardPayment') {
        _type = TransactionType.transfer;
        _from = accounts
            .where((account) => account.type == AccountType.bank.name)
            .firstOrNull
            ?.id;
        _to ??= accounts
            .where((account) => account.type == AccountType.creditCard.name)
            .firstOrNull
            ?.id;
      }
      _baseline = _draftSnapshot;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _loadClone(String id) async {
    final database = ref.read(databaseProvider);
    setState(() => _loadingTemplate = true);
    try {
      final row = await (database.select(
        database.ledgerTransactions,
      )..where((t) => t.id.equals(id))).getSingleOrNull();
      if (row == null || row.status != 'confirmed') {
        throw StateError('El movimiento ya no está disponible.');
      }
      final splits = await (database.select(
        database.transactionSplits,
      )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).get();
      final plan = await (database.select(
        database.installmentPlans,
      )..where((t) => t.transactionId.equals(id))).getSingleOrNull();
      final merchant = row.merchantId == null
          ? null
          : await (database.select(
              database.merchants,
            )..where((t) => t.id.equals(row.merchantId!))).getSingleOrNull();
      final oldAccounts =
          await (database.select(database.accounts)..where(
                (t) => t.id.isIn(
                  [row.fromAccountId, row.toAccountId].whereType<String>(),
                ),
              ))
              .get();
      final oldCategories = await (database.select(
        database.categories,
      )..where((t) => t.id.isIn(splits.map((s) => s.categoryId)))).get();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _editingAccounts = oldAccounts;
        _editingCategories = oldCategories;
        _type = TransactionType.values.byName(row.type);
        _amount.text = Money(row.amountMinor).format(withCode: false).trim();
        _originalMerchantId = widget.editId != null ? row.merchantId : null;
        _selectedMerchant =
            widget.editId != null || merchant?.isArchived == false
            ? merchant
            : null;
        _merchant.text = _selectedMerchant?.name ?? row.description;
        _note.text = row.note;
        _from = row.fromAccountId;
        _to = row.toAccountId;
        _channel = PaymentChannel.values.byName(row.paymentChannel);
        _occurredAt = widget.editId != null
            ? row.occurredAt.toLocal()
            : DateTime.now();
        _editingUpdatedAt = row.updatedAt;
        _installments = plan != null;
        _installmentCount = plan?.installmentCount ?? 2;
        _firstInstallmentDate = widget.editId != null
            ? plan?.firstPeriod.toUtc()
            : null;
        _splitEnabled = splits.length > 1;
        _category = splits.length == 1 ? splits.single.categoryId : null;
        for (final split in splits.where((_) => _splitEnabled)) {
          _splits.add(
            _SplitInput(categoryId: split.categoryId)
              ..amount.text = Money(split.amountMinor)
                  .format(withCode: false)
                  .trim(),
          );
        }
        _advanced = true;
        _baseline = _draftSnapshot;
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mounted) _templateError = '$error';
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loadingTemplate = false);
    }
  }

  String _channelLabel(PaymentChannel channel) => switch (channel) {
    PaymentChannel.cash => 'Efectivo',
    PaymentChannel.physicalCard => 'Tarjeta física',
    PaymentChannel.debitCard => 'Tarjeta de débito',
    PaymentChannel.physicalCreditCard => 'Tarjeta de crédito física',
    PaymentChannel.googleWalletContactless => 'Google Wallet',
    PaymentChannel.onlineCard => 'Pago online',
    PaymentChannel.bankTransfer => 'Transferencia',
    PaymentChannel.other => 'Otro',
  };

  void _message(String text) {
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(text)));
    }
  }
}

final class _AccountField extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AccountField({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.accounts,
    required this.onChanged,
    this.excludedId,
  });

  final String label;
  final String? value;
  final List<AccountRecord> accounts;
  final ValueChanged<String?> onChanged;
  final String? excludedId;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
    key: ValueKey((
      label,
      value,
      excludedId,
      accounts.map((row) => row.id).join(','),
    )),
    initialValue: accounts.any((row) => row.id == value && row.id != excludedId)
        ? value
        : null,
    decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label),
    items: [
      for (final account in accounts.where(
        (account) => account.id != excludedId,
      ))
        DropdownMenuItem(value: account.id, child: Text(account.name)),
    ],
    onChanged: onChanged,
  );
}

final class _SplitInput {
  _SplitInput({this.categoryId}) : amount = TextEditingController();

  String? categoryId;
  final TextEditingController amount;

  void dispose() => amount.dispose();
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:drift/drift.dart' show OrderingTerm;

final class TransactionsScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const TransactionsScreen({
    this.account,
    this.merchant,
    this.category,
    this.start,
    this.end,
    this.quotaPeriod = false,
    this.unclassified = false,
    this.reportOnly = false,
    this.withoutMerchant = false,
    this.type,
    super.key,
  });
  final String? account;
  final String? merchant;
  final String? category;
  final DateTime? start, end;
  final bool quotaPeriod, unclassified, reportOnly, withoutMerchant;
  final String? type;

  @override
  ConsumerState<TransactionsScreen> createState() => _TransactionsScreenState();
}

class _TransactionsScreenState extends ConsumerState<TransactionsScreen> {
  var _limit = 50;
  HistoryFilter _filter = emptyHistoryFilter;
  final _search = TextEditingController();
  bool _quotaPeriod = false,
      _unclassified = false,
      _reportOnly = false,
      _withoutMerchant = false;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _quotaPeriod = widget.quotaPeriod;
    _unclassified = widget.unclassified;
    _reportOnly = widget.reportOnly;
    _withoutMerchant = widget.withoutMerchant;
    _filter = (
      search: '',
      account: widget.account,
      merchant: widget.merchant,
      category: widget.category,
      type: widget.type,
      start: widget.start,
      end: widget.end,
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _search.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _setFilter(HistoryFilter filter) {
    setState(() {
      _filter = filter;
      _limit = 50;
      _previousRows = [];
    });
  }

  List<MovementRow> _previousRows = [];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final transactions = ref.watch(
      movementHistoryProvider((
        filter: _filter,
        limit: _limit + 1,
        unclassified: _unclassified,
        reportOnly: _reportOnly,
        withoutMerchant: _withoutMerchant,
      )),
    );
    if (transactions.hasValue) _previousRows = transactions.value!;
    final display = transactions.isLoading && _previousRows.isNotEmpty
        ? AsyncData(_previousRows)
        : transactions;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'Filtrar movimientos',
            onPressed: _filters,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.filter_list),
          ),
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'Plantillas',
            onPressed: () => context.push('/templates'),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.bookmark_outline),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: TextField(
              controller: _search,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Buscar comercio o nota',
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                suffixIcon:
                    _filter != emptyHistoryFilter ||
                        _quotaPeriod ||
                        _unclassified ||
                        _reportOnly ||
                        _withoutMerchant
                    ? IconButton(
                        tooltip: 'Limpiar filtros',
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
                        onPressed: () {
                          _search.clear();
                          _quotaPeriod = false;
                          _unclassified = false;
                          _reportOnly = false;
                          _withoutMerchant = false;
                          _setFilter(emptyHistoryFilter);
                        },
                      )
                    : null,
              ),
              onChanged: (value) => _setFilter((
                search: value,
                account: _filter.account,
                merchant: _filter.merchant,
                category: _filter.category,
                type: _filter.type,
                start: _filter.start,
                end: _filter.end,
              )),
            ),
          ),
          if (_unclassified || _withoutMerchant)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 12),
              child: Text(
                [
                  if (_unclassified) 'Sin categoría.',
                  if (_withoutMerchant) 'Sin comercio asociado.',
                ].join(' '),
              ),
            ),
          Expanded(
            child: AsyncValueView(
              value: display,
              data: (rows) {
                if (rows.isEmpty) {
                  return const Center(
                    child: Text('No hay movimientos para mostrar.'),
                  );
                }
                return ListView.separated(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 96),
                  itemCount: rows.length > _limit ? _limit + 1 : rows.length,
                  separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const Divider(height: 1),
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    if (index == _limit) {
                      return Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                        child: transactions.isLoading
                            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                            : OutlinedButton(
                                onPressed: () => setState(() => _limit += 50),
                                child: const Text('Cargar 50 movimientos más'),
                              ),
                      );
                    }
                    return _MovementTile(
                      row: rows[index].transaction,
                      movement: rows[index],
                      onDetail: () =>
                          showMovementDetail(context, ref, rows[index]),
                      onRefund: () => showRefundDialog(
                        context,
                        ref,
                        rows[index].transaction,
                      ),
                      onEdit: () => context.push(
                        '/transactions/new?edit=${rows[index].transaction.id}',
                      ),
                      onDelete: () =>
                          _delete(context, ref, rows[index].transaction),
                      onClone: () => context.push(
                        '/transactions/new?clone=${rows[index].transaction.id}',
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: const MovementFab(),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _filters() async {
    final db = ref.read(databaseProvider);
    final accounts = await db.select(db.accounts).get();
    final merchants = await db.select(db.merchants).get();
    final categories = await db.select(db.categories).get();
    if (!mounted) return;
    String? account = _filter.account,
        merchant = _filter.merchant,
        category = _filter.category,
        type = _filter.type;
    DateTime? start = _filter.start, end = _filter.end;
    final accepted = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, update) => AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Filtrar movimientos'),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                _filterField('Cuenta', account, {
                  for (final a in accounts) a.id: a.name,
                }, (v) => account = v),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _filterField('Comercio', merchant, {
                  for (final m in merchants) m.id: m.name,
                }, (v) => merchant = v),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _filterField('Categoría', category, {
                  for (final c in categories) c.id: c.name,
                }, (v) => category = v),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _filterField('Tipo', type, {
                  'purchases': 'Compras y devoluciones',
                  'expense': 'Gasto',
                  'income': 'Ingreso',
                  'transfer': 'Transferencia',
                  'refund': 'Devolución',
                  'adjustment': 'Ajuste',
                }, (v) => type = v),
                ListTile(
                  title: const Text('Período'),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    start == null
                        ? 'Todo el historial'
                        : '${MaterialLocalizations.of(context).formatShortDate(start!)} – ${MaterialLocalizations.of(context).formatShortDate(end!.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)))}',
                  ),
                  trailing: const Icon(Icons.calendar_month),
                  onTap: () async {
                    final range = await showDateRangePicker(
                      context: context,
                      firstDate: DateTime(2000),
                      lastDate: DateTime(2100),
                      initialDateRange: start == null
                          ? null
                          : DateTimeRange(
                              start: start!,
                              end: end!.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
                            ),
                    );
                    if (range != null && context.mounted) {
                      update(() {
                        start = range.start;
                        end = DateTime(
                          range.end.year,
                          range.end.month,
                          range.end.day + 1,
                        );
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
              child: const Text('Cancelar'),
            ),
            FilledButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
              child: const Text('Aplicar'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (accepted == true && mounted) {
      _setFilter((
        search: _search.text,
        account: account,
        merchant: merchant,
        category: category,
        type: type,
        start: start,
        end: end,
      ));
    }
  }

  Widget _filterField(
    String label,
    String? value,
    Map<String, String> choices,
    ValueChanged<String?> onChanged,
  ) => DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
    initialValue: value,
    isExpanded: true,
    decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label),
    items: [
      const DropdownMenuItem<String>(value: null, child: Text('Todos')),
      for (final e in choices.entries)
        DropdownMenuItem(value: e.key, child: Text(e.value)),
    ],
    onChanged: onChanged,
  );

  Future<void> _delete(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
    TransactionRecord row,
  ) async {
    final ledger = ref.read(ledgerServiceProvider);
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    try {
      await ledger.softDelete(row.id);
    } catch (error) {
      if (messenger.mounted) {
        messenger.showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: const Text('Movimiento eliminado.'),
        duration: const Duration(seconds: 8),
        persist: false,
        action: SnackBarAction(
          label: 'Deshacer',
          onPressed: () async {
            try {
              await ledger.restore(row.id);
            } catch (error) {
              if (messenger.mounted) {
                messenger.showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$error')));
              }
            }
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

final class _MovementTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MovementTile({
    required this.row,
    required this.onDelete,
    required this.onClone,
    required this.onEdit,
    required this.onRefund,
    required this.movement,
    required this.onDetail,
  });

  final TransactionRecord row;
  final MovementRow movement;
  final VoidCallback onDetail;
  final VoidCallback onDelete;
  final VoidCallback onClone;
  final VoidCallback onEdit;
  final VoidCallback onRefund;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final signed = row.type == 'expense'
        ? -movement.amountMinor
        : row.type == 'income' || row.type == 'refund'
        ? movement.amountMinor
        : 0;
    return ListTile(
      onTap: onDetail,
      leading: CircleAvatar(child: Icon(_icon(row.type))),
      title: Text(row.description.isEmpty ? _label(row.type) : row.description),
      subtitle: Text(
        '${row.note == 'Registrado automáticamente' ? 'Automático · ' : ''}${movement.isQuota ? 'Cuota ${movement.number}/${movement.count}' : _label(row.type)} · ${movement.occurredAt.day}/${movement.occurredAt.month}/${movement.occurredAt.year}${movement.planned ? ' · Prevista' : ''}',
      ),
      trailing: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          MoneyText(
            signed == 0 ? movement.amountMinor : signed,
            currencyCode: row.currencyCode,
            signed: signed != 0,
          ),
          PopupMenuButton<String>(
            onSelected: (value) {
              if (value == 'refund') {
                onRefund();
              } else if (value == 'edit') {
                onEdit();
              } else if (value == 'clone') {
                onClone();
              } else {
                onDelete();
              }
            },
            itemBuilder: (context) => [
              if (['income', 'expense', 'transfer'].contains(row.type))
                PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'edit',
                  child: Text(movement.isQuota ? 'Editar compra' : 'Editar'),
                ),
              if (row.type == 'expense' && !movement.isQuota)
                const PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'refund',
                  child: Text('Registrar devolución'),
                ),
              if (['income', 'expense', 'transfer'].contains(row.type))
                const PopupMenuItem(value: 'clone', child: Text('Clonar')),
              if (!movement.isQuota)
                const PopupMenuItem(value: 'delete', child: Text('Eliminar')),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  IconData _icon(String type) => switch (type) {
    'expense' => Icons.south_west,
    'income' => Icons.north_east,
    'transfer' => Icons.swap_horiz,
    'refund' => Icons.replay,
    _ => Icons.tune,
  };

  String _label(String type) => switch (type) {
    'expense' => 'Gasto',
    'income' => 'Ingreso',
    'transfer' => 'Transferencia',
    'refund' => 'Reembolso',
    _ => 'Ajuste',
  };
}

Future<void> showMovementDetail(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref,
  MovementRow movement,
) async {
  final row = movement.transaction, db = ref.read(databaseProvider);
  final plans = await (db.select(
    db.installmentPlans,
  )..where((p) => p.transactionId.equals(row.id))).get();
  final entries = plans.isEmpty
      ? <InstallmentEntryRecord>[]
      : await (db.select(db.installmentEntries)
              ..where((e) => e.planId.equals(plans.single.id))
              ..orderBy([(e) => OrderingTerm.asc(e.installmentNumber)]))
            .get();
  final accountRowsFuture = db.select(db.accounts).get();
  final categoriesFuture = db.select(db.categories).get();
  final splitsFuture = (db.select(
    db.transactionSplits,
  )..where((split) => split.transactionId.equals(row.id))).get();
  final accountRows = await accountRowsFuture;
  final categories = await categoriesFuture;
  final splits = await splitsFuture;
  String accountName(String? id) =>
      accountRows.where((a) => a.id == id).firstOrNull?.name ?? '—';
  final categoryNames = splits
      .map(
        (split) =>
            categories
                .where((category) => category.id == split.categoryId)
                .firstOrNull
                ?.name ??
            'Categoría archivada',
      )
      .join(' / ');
  final channel = switch (row.paymentChannel) {
    'cash' => 'Efectivo',
    'physicalCard' => 'Tarjeta física',
    'debitCard' => 'Tarjeta de débito',
    'physicalCreditCard' => 'Tarjeta de crédito',
    'googleWalletContactless' => 'Google Wallet',
    'onlineCard' => 'Tarjeta online',
    'bankTransfer' => 'Transferencia bancaria',
    _ => 'Otro',
  };
  if (!context.mounted) return;
  await showModalBottomSheet<void>(
    context: context,
    showDragHandle: true,
    isScrollControlled: true,
    builder: (sheet) => SafeArea(
      child: FractionallySizedBox(
        heightFactor: .8,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          children: [
            Text(
              movement.isQuota
                  ? 'Cuota ${movement.number}/${movement.count}'
                  : (entries.isEmpty ? 'Movimiento' : 'Compra original'),
              style: Theme.of(sheet).textTheme.titleLarge,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(row.description),
            MoneyText(
              movement.amountMinor,
              style: Theme.of(sheet).textTheme.headlineMedium,
            ),
            Text(
              '${MaterialLocalizations.of(sheet).formatShortDate(movement.occurredAt)}${movement.planned ? ' · Prevista' : ''}',
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            _MovementFact(
              'Cuenta',
              accountName(row.fromAccountId ?? row.toAccountId),
            ),
            if (row.fromAccountId != null && row.toAccountId != null)
              _MovementFact('Destino', accountName(row.toAccountId)),
            if (splits.isNotEmpty) _MovementFact('Categoría', categoryNames),
            _MovementFact('Canal', channel),
            if (row.note.isNotEmpty) _MovementFact('Nota', row.note),
            if (entries.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const Divider(height: 32),
              ListTile(
                title: const Text('Compra original'),
                subtitle: Text(
                  MaterialLocalizations.of(sheet)
                      .formatShortDate(row.occurredAt.toLocal()),
                ),
                trailing: MoneyText(row.amountMinor),
              ),
              for (final entry in entries)
                ListTile(
                  title: Text(
                    'Cuota ${entry.installmentNumber}/${plans.single.installmentCount}',
                  ),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    MaterialLocalizations.of(sheet)
                        .formatShortDate(entry.periodStart.toUtc()),
                  ),
                  trailing: MoneyText(entry.amountMinor),
                ),
            ],
            if (['expense', 'income', 'transfer'].contains(row.type))
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: () {
                  Navigator.pop(sheet);
                  context.push('/transactions/new?edit=${row.id}');
                },
                child: Text(entries.isEmpty ? 'Editar' : 'Editar compra'),
              ),
            if (row.type == 'expense')
              OutlinedButton(
                onPressed: () {
                  Navigator.pop(sheet);
                  showRefundDialog(context, ref, row);
                },
                child: const Text('Registrar devolución'),
              ),
            if (['expense', 'income', 'transfer'].contains(row.type))
              TextButton(
                onPressed: () {
                  Navigator.pop(sheet);
                  context.push('/transactions/new?clone=${row.id}');
                },
                child: const Text('Clonar'),
              ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () async {
                final yes = await showDialog<bool>(
                  context: sheet,
                  builder: (dialog) => AlertDialog(
                    title: Text(
                      entries.isEmpty
                          ? 'Eliminar movimiento'
                          : 'Eliminar compra y sus cuotas',
                    ),
                    actions: [
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialog, false),
                        child: const Text('Cancelar'),
                      ),
                      FilledButton(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialog, true),
                        child: const Text('Eliminar'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
                if (yes != true || !context.mounted) return;
                try {
                  final ledger = ref.read(ledgerServiceProvider);
                  await ledger.softDelete(row.id);
                  if (!sheet.mounted || !context.mounted) return;
                  Navigator.pop(sheet);
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: const Text('Movimiento eliminado.'),
                      action: SnackBarAction(
                        label: 'Deshacer',
                        onPressed: () async {
                          try {
                            await ledger.restore(row.id);
                          } catch (e) {
                            if (context.mounted) {
                              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
                                  .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$e')));
                            }
                          }
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (e) {
                  if (sheet.mounted) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(sheet)
                        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$e')));
                  }
                }
              },
              child: const Text('Eliminar'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

class _MovementFact extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MovementFact(this.label, this.value);
  final String label, value;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
    child: Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Expanded(
          flex: 2,
          child: Text(label, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 16),
        Expanded(
          flex: 3,
          child: Text(
            value,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium
                ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
}


FILE lib/shared/widgets/money_text.dart SHA256 e28fb4f628023ccd5fc1747770e60dab759b35f6fbbacc3ca254963bfd3b546c
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/money/money.dart';

final class MoneyText extends StatelessWidget {
  const MoneyText(
    this.minorUnits, {
    this.currencyCode = 'PYG',
    this.style,
    this.signed = false,
    this.currencySymbol,
    this.maxLines = 1,
    super.key,
  });

  final int minorUnits;
  final String currencyCode;
  final TextStyle? style;
  final bool signed;
  final String? currencySymbol;
  final int? maxLines;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final money = Money(minorUnits, currencyCode: currencyCode);
    final formatted = currencySymbol == null
        ? money.format()
        : '${money.format(withCode: false).trim()} $currencySymbol';
    return Text(
      signed && minorUnits > 0 ? '+$formatted' : formatted,
      style: style,
      maxLines: maxLines,
      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
    );
  }
}


FILE lib/shared/widgets/movement_fab.dart SHA256 973434474214eaa0d96232f26835f3d84459eb4f6538f517b87814cc3c8001b1
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// One entry point, shared by home and history. The menu never writes data.
class MovementFab extends StatefulWidget {
  const MovementFab({super.key});
  @override
  State<MovementFab> createState() => _MovementFabState();
}

class _MovementFabState extends State<MovementFab> {
  bool _open = false;
  final _focus = FocusNode();
  final _triggerFocus = FocusNode();
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _focus.dispose();
    _triggerFocus.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _close({bool returnFocus = true}) {
    if (!_open || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _open = false);
    if (returnFocus) _triggerFocus.requestFocus();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => PopScope(
    canPop: !_open,
    onPopInvokedWithResult: (popped, _) {
      if (!popped) _close();
    },
    child: CallbackShortcuts(
      bindings: {const SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape): _close},
      child: Focus(
        focusNode: _focus,
        onFocusChange: (hasFocus) {
          if (!hasFocus) _close(returnFocus: false);
        },
        child: TapRegion(
          onTapOutside: (_) => _close(),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              if (_open)
                ConstrainedBox(
                  constraints: BoxConstraints(
                    maxHeight:
                        (MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height -
                                MediaQuery.paddingOf(context).vertical -
                                220)
                            .clamp(80, 480),
                    maxWidth: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width - 32,
                  ),
                  child: SingleChildScrollView(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                      children: [
                        for (final action in [
                          ('expense', 'Gasto', Icons.remove),
                          ('income', 'Ingreso', Icons.add),
                          ('transfer', 'Transferir', Icons.swap_horiz),
                        ])
                          Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                            child: FloatingActionButton.extended(
                              heroTag: null,
                              onPressed: () {
                                _close(returnFocus: false);
                                context.push(
                                  '/transactions/new?type=${action.$1}',
                                );
                              },
                              icon: Icon(action.$3),
                              label: Text(action.$2),
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              FloatingActionButton(
                focusNode: _triggerFocus,
                heroTag: null,
                tooltip: _open ? 'Cerrar acciones' : 'Nuevo movimiento',
                onPressed: () {
                  setState(() => _open = !_open);
                  _triggerFocus.requestFocus();
                },
                child: AnimatedRotation(
                  turns: _open ? .125 : 0,
                  duration: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
                      ? Duration.zero
                      : const Duration(milliseconds: 160),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.add),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}


FILE test/features/dashboard/budget_ring_test.dart SHA256 0c69931d1af191fddce9cd9aa914e284c85491ff079bb0383d6692a9b0f5d276
import 'package:finanzas_locales/features/budgets/presentation/budgets_screen.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('250% pendiente normaliza verde 40 rojo 60 con opacidad suave', () {
    final ring = BudgetRingPainter(
      2.5,
      const ColorScheme.light(),
      paidRatio: 0,
    );
    expect(ring.segments.map((s) => s.fraction), [0.4, 0.6]);
    expect(ring.segments.every((s) => s.pending), isTrue);
    expect(ring.segments.first.excess, isFalse);
    expect(ring.segments.last.excess, isTrue);
  });
  test('300% pagado mantiene un único anillo proporcional', () {
    final ring = BudgetRingPainter(3, const ColorScheme.dark(), paidRatio: 3);
    expect(ring.segments.map((s) => s.fraction), [1 / 3, 2 / 3]);
    expect(ring.segments.every((s) => !s.pending), isTrue);
  });
  test('340% mixto y cero conservan escala real sin negativos', () {
    final ring = BudgetRingPainter(
      3.4,
      const ColorScheme.light(),
      paidRatio: .5,
    );
    expect(
      ring.segments.fold<double>(0, (sum, s) => sum + s.fraction),
      closeTo(1, 1e-9),
    );
    expect(
      ring.segments
          .where((s) => s.pending)
          .fold<double>(0, (sum, s) => sum + s.fraction),
      closeTo(2.9 / 3.4, 1e-9),
    );
    expect(
      const BudgetRingPainter(0, ColorScheme.light(), paidRatio: 0).segments,
      isEmpty,
    );
    expect(
      const BudgetRingPainter(-1, ColorScheme.light(), paidRatio: -1).segments,
      isEmpty,
    );
  });
}


FILE test/features/dashboard/movement_fab_focus_test.dart SHA256 7abb164a1a8e1e2f8224d35ff3d2cdfbfda3c507e211c1cceda0aeca7dd04037
import 'package:finanzas_locales/shared/widgets/movement_fab.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  testWidgets(
    'Escape devuelve foco al más y salir del grupo cierra las acciones',
    (tester) async {
      final outside = FocusNode();
      addTearDown(outside.dispose);
      await tester.pumpWidget(
        MaterialApp(
          home: Scaffold(
            body: TextField(focusNode: outside),
            floatingActionButton: const MovementFab(),
          ),
        ),
      );
      await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Nuevo movimiento'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      final fab = tester.widget<FloatingActionButton>(
        find.byType(FloatingActionButton),
      );
      expect(fab.focusNode?.hasFocus, isTrue);
      await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Nuevo movimiento'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      outside.requestFocus();
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Gasto'), findsNothing);
      expect(outside.hasFocus, isTrue);
      await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
    },
  );
}

                    includeInAvailableMoney: false,
                    includeInNetWorth: true,

          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
        });

            .widget<MoneyText>(
              find.descendant(of: netTile, matching: find.byType(MoneyText)),
            )
            .minorUnits;

        expect(find.text('Ver resumen'), findsNothing);
        expect(find.text('Incluir ahorros'), findsNothing);
        expect(find.text('Deuda de tarjetas'), findsNothing);
        await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
        await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          container.dispose();
          await db.close();
        });
      },
    );

        matching: find.byType(MoneyText),
      );

void main() {

    tester,
  ) async {
    final db = AppDatabase.forTesting(NativeDatabase.memory());
    addTearDown(db.close);
    final id = await tester.runAsync(
      () => ManualTemplateService(db).save('Servicio', {
        'type': 'expense',
        'amountMinor': 10000,
        'channel': 'other',
        'schedule': {
          'enabled': false,
          'frequency': 'monthly',

          categoriesProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),

          home: TransactionFormScreen(savedTemplateId: id!, editTemplate: true),

    await tester.tap(find.text('Guardar plantilla'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Preparar pagos pendientes'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.ensureVisible(find.text('Recordatorios'));
    await tester.tap(find.text('Recordatorios'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Días antes'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.textContaining('Registra al abrir'), findsNothing);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Cancelar'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

      (await tester.runAsync(() => ManualTemplateService(db).read(id)))!
          .fields['reminders'],
      isNull,
    );
    await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox.shrink());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  });
  for (final automatic in [false, true]) {
    testWidgets(
      'usar y guardar plantilla con movimiento, automática=$automatic',
      (tester) async {
        final db = AppDatabase.forTesting(NativeDatabase.memory());
        const reminderChannel = MethodChannel(
          'finanzas_locales/payment_reminders',
        );
        tester.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
          reminderChannel,
          (_) async => false,
        );
        addTearDown(
          () => tester.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
            reminderChannel,
            null,
          ),
        );
        late String merchantId;
        late List<AccountRecord> accounts;
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          final account = await AccountsService(db).createAccount(
            const CreateAccountDraft(name: 'Banco', type: AccountType.bank),
          );
          accounts = await db.select(db.accounts).get();
          merchantId = await MerchantService(db).findOrCreate('Alquiler');
          await ManualTemplateService(db).save('A ingreso', {
            'type': 'income',
            'amountMinor': 100,
            'to': account,
          });
          await ManualTemplateService(db).save('Alquiler', {
            'type': 'expense',
            'amountMinor': 120000,
            'from': account,
            'merchant': 'Nombre anterior',
            'merchantId': merchantId,
            'channel': 'bankTransfer',
            if (automatic)
              'schedule': {
                'enabled': true,
                'frequency': 'monthly',
                'nextDate': '2030-11-04',
              },
          });
        });
        await tester.pumpWidget(
          ProviderScope(
            overrides: [
              databaseProvider.overrideWithValue(db),
              accountsProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value(accounts)),
              categoriesProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
              merchantsProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
              merchantAliasesProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
            ],
            child: const MaterialApp(home: TransactionFormScreen()),
          ),
        );
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
        });
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Usar plantilla'));
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
        });
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(
          find.text('Administrar plantillas y registros programados'),
          findsOneWidget,
        );
        expect(
          tester.getTopLeft(find.text('Alquiler')).dy,
          lessThan(tester.getTopLeft(find.text('A ingreso')).dy),
        );
        expect(find.text('Ingreso · Abrir y revisar'), findsOneWidget);
        if (automatic) {
          expect(
            find.textContaining('Mensual · Próximo registro:'),
            findsOneWidget,
          );
        }
        await tester.tap(find.text('Alquiler'));
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
        });
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(
          tester
              .widget<TextFormField>(find.byType(TextFormField).first)
              .controller!
              .text,
          '120.000',
        );
        expect(find.text('Banco'), findsOneWidget);
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          expect(await db.select(db.ledgerTransactions).get(), isEmpty);
        });
        await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
          find.text('Guardar también como plantilla'),
          300,
          scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
        );
        await tester.tap(find.text('Guardar también como plantilla'));
        await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
          find.text('Guardar y crear otro'),
          150,
          scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
        );
        await tester.tap(find.text('Guardar y crear otro'));
        await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
          find.text('Guardar movimiento'),
          200,
          scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
        );
        await tester.tap(find.text('Guardar movimiento'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        await tester.enterText(
          find.widgetWithText(TextFormField, 'Nombre de la plantilla'),
          automatic ? 'Netflix automático' : 'Alquiler variable',
        );
        if (automatic) {
          await tester.tap(find.text('Preparar pagos pendientes'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          expect(find.text('Mensual'), findsOneWidget);
          expect(find.text('Próxima fecha'), findsOneWidget);
        } else {
          await tester.tap(find.text('Recordar importe'));
        }
        await tester.pump();
        await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Guardar'));
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
        });
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        await tester.runAsync(() async {
          final templates = await ManualTemplateService(db).list();
          expect(templates, hasLength(3));
          final saved = templates.firstWhere(
            (t) =>
                t.name ==
                (automatic ? 'Netflix automático' : 'Alquiler variable'),
          );
          expect(saved.fields['merchantId'], merchantId);
          expect(saved.fields['amountMinor'], automatic ? 120000 : null);
          expect((saved.fields['schedule'] as Map)['enabled'], automatic);
          expect(await db.select(db.ledgerTransactions).get(), hasLength(1));
          if (automatic) {
            final result = await ScheduledTemplateService(db).processDue();
            expect(result.registered, ['Netflix automático']);
            final future = await PendingPaymentService(db).list();
            expect(future, hasLength(1));
            expect(future.single.amountMinor, 120000);
            expect(future.single.paidMinor, 0);
            expect(
              future.single.sourceReference,
              startsWith('scheduled:${saved.id}:'),
            );
            expect(
              (await ScheduledTemplateService(db).processDue()).registered,
              isEmpty,
            );
            expect(result.pending, isEmpty);
            expect(await db.select(db.ledgerTransactions).get(), hasLength(1));
          }
        });
        if (automatic) {
          expect(
            find.text('Guardado. Recordatorios sin permiso.'),
            findsOneWidget,
          );
        }
        await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
        await tester.runAsync(db.close);
      },
    );
  }
}

          container: container,

      await tester.pumpAndSettle();

      expect(find.text('Canal'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Nota'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Referencia 42'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
      await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    },
  );
}

            await tester.tap(find.text('Banco').last);
            await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          }
          await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
            find.text('Guardar y crear otro'),
            250,
            scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
          );
          await tester.tap(find.text('Guardar y crear otro'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
            find.text('Guardar movimiento'),
            250,
            scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
          );
          await tester.tap(find.text('Guardar movimiento'));
          await tester.runAsync(() async {
            await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 70));
          });
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          await tester.runAsync(() async {
            final ledger = await db.select(db.ledgerTransactions).get();
            final pending = await PendingPaymentService(db).list();
            if (income) {
              expect(ledger.single.type, 'income');
              expect(pending, isEmpty);
              expect(await db.select(db.installmentPlans).get(), isEmpty);
            } else {
              expect(ledger, isEmpty);
              expect(pending, hasLength(2));
              expect(pending.fold<int>(0, (n, p) => n + p.amountMinor), 100000);
            }
          });
          if (!income) {
            expect(
              find.text('Guardado. Recordatorios sin permiso.'),
              findsOneWidget,
            );
          }
          await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox.shrink());
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        },
      );
    }
  }
  testWidgets('Formulario en cuotas admite 320px y texto 2x', (tester) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(320, 1800);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
    addTearDown(tester.view.resetPhysicalSize);
    addTearDown(tester.view.resetDevicePixelRatio);
    FlutterErrorDetails? layoutError;
    final originalErrorHandler = FlutterError.onError;
    FlutterError.onError = (details) {
      layoutError = details;
      originalErrorHandler?.call(details);
    };
    addTearDown(() => FlutterError.onError = originalErrorHandler);
    final db = AppDatabase.forTesting(NativeDatabase.memory());
    addTearDown(db.close);
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      ProviderScope(
        overrides: [
          databaseProvider.overrideWithValue(db),
          accountsProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
          categoriesProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
          merchantsProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
          merchantAliasesProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value([])),
        ],
        child: MaterialApp(
          builder: (context, child) => MediaQuery(
            data: MediaQuery.of(context)
                .copyWith(textScaler: const TextScaler.linear(2)),
            child: child!,
          ),
          home: const TransactionFormScreen(),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull, reason: layoutError?.toString());
    await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
      find.text('En cuotas'),
      250,
      scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('En cuotas'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull, reason: layoutError?.toString());
    await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
      find.text('Guardar movimiento'),
      250,
      scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull, reason: layoutError?.toString());
    await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox.shrink());
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  });
}

      (tester) async {

        addTearDown(db.close);
        final service = PendingPaymentService(db);

          );

          );

            amountMinor: 100000,
            installmentCount: 2,
            firstDueDate: DateTime(2026, 10, 8),

          );

            expectedRevision: 0,
            amountMinor: 50000,
            paidAt: DateTime(2026, 10, 8),
            accountId: cash,
          );

            await db.customStatement(
              'UPDATE accounts SET is_archived = 1 WHERE id = ?',
              [cash],
            );
          }
        });
        await tester.pumpWidget(
          ProviderScope(
            overrides: [databaseProvider.overrideWithValue(db)],
            child: MaterialApp(home: PaymentDetailScreen(paymentId: nextId)),

        );

            find.widgetWithText(DropdownButtonFormField<String>, 'Cuenta'),
          );

                    'Cuenta',
                  ),

          bank,
        );
        await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox.shrink());
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      },
    );
  }
